PROGRAM create_nine_block_blade_topology

    USE cgns
    IMPLICIT NONE

    TYPE :: block_type
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x(:,:), y(:,:)
    END TYPE block_type

    TYPE :: corner_patch_type
        INTEGER :: Nb = 0
        INTEGER :: block(6) = 0
        INTEGER :: i(6) = 0
        INTEGER :: j(6) = 0
        REAL(8) :: yoff(6) = 0.0D0
    END TYPE corner_patch_type

    REAL(8) :: pitch
    REAL(8) :: Lo
    REAL(8) :: curvature_strength
    REAL(8) :: ywall
    REAL(8) :: extrusion_span
    REAL(8) :: small_2d_cell_edge_length
    INTEGER :: extrusion_nplanes_actual

    ! every Poisson smoothing iteration.
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: max_point_movement_fraction = 0.08D0

    ! Corner forcing strength used by corner_shrink. Larger values push the
    ! multi-block junctions more strongly toward evenly split angles.
REAL(8), PARAMETER :: corner_equal_angle_force = 1000.0D0
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: fixed_initial_blade_wall_enabled = .TRUE.
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: farfield_vertical_line_refinement = 2
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: blade_tangential_smooth_nlines = 8
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: blade_tangential_smooth_relax = 0.40D0
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: wall_first_offline_normalise_enabled = .TRUE.
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: wall_first_offline_normalise_relax = 0.22D0
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: wall_first_offline_normalise_passes = 1

    ! Correct Block 6 radialisation:
    ! keep ALL Block 6 edges fixed, including the Block 6--9 top edge.
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: block6_fixed_edge_radialise_enabled = .TRUE.

    ! Higher power delays tangential recovery until farther from the blade,
    ! keeping near-wall lines more radial while still reaching the fixed top edge.
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block6_fixed_edge_tangent_recovery_power = 3.5D0
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: wall_first_offline_te_skip_fraction = 0.16D0
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: block9_tfi_reset_enabled = .TRUE.
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: block9_smooth_passes = 120
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block9_smooth_relax = 0.18D0
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block9_skewness_limit = 0.40D0
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: block9_force_smooth_passes = 250
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block9_force_smooth_relax = 0.45D0
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block9_boundary_line_bias = 0.65D0

    ! Safer interface policy:
    ! keep Blocks 6 and 8 completely fixed and smooth only Block 9 interior.
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: block9_only_safe_interface_smoothing_enabled = .TRUE.
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: block9_only_interface_smooth_passes = 400
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block9_only_interface_smooth_relax = 0.35D0

    ! Block 9 smoothing.
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block9_smooth_relax_near_block6_factor = 0.0D0

    ! Number of Block 9 interior layers from the detected Block 6-facing edge
    ! over which the weaker/frozen smoothing is applied.
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: block9_smooth_near_block6_layers = 10

    ! During every smoothing iteration, straighten the middle Block 6 radial
    ! lines.  The leftmost/rightmost i-lines are excluded so the Block 5/6 and
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: block6_middle_radial_straighten_each_iter_enabled = .TRUE.

    ! Number of i-lines to protect at each side of Block 6.
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: block6_middle_radial_protect_i_lines = 6

    ! 1.0D0 = fully replace the middle i-lines by straight wall-to-outer-edge
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block6_middle_radial_straighten_relax = 1.0D0

    ! Final operation after all smoothing/C1/best-state rollback.
    ! This makes straightening the last geometry operation before final output.
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: block6_middle_radial_straighten_final_step_enabled = .TRUE.

    ! Same straightening logic for Block 8.
    ! straight, especially near the Block 8--9 interface.
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: block8_middle_radial_straighten_each_iter_enabled = .TRUE.
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: block8_middle_radial_protect_i_lines = 6
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block8_middle_radial_straighten_relax = 1.0D0
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: block8_middle_radial_straighten_final_step_enabled = .TRUE.

    ! Final global smoothing of the entire Block 9 interior after Blocks 6 and
    ! layer correction.  It relaxes every Block 9 interior node while keeping
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: block9_global_smooth_after_68_enabled = .TRUE.
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: block9_global_smooth_after_68_passes = 600
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block9_global_smooth_after_68_relax = 0.12D0

    ! Bias the smoother slightly in the cross-interface direction.  Keep this
    ! modest; too large can fold cells.  1.0 gives an ordinary isotropic
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block9_global_smooth_cross_weight = 1.35D0

    ! Whole-Block-9 line blending:
    ! after global smoothing, rebuild the Block 9 cross-lines between the
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: block9_whole_line_blend_68_enabled = .TRUE.

    ! 0.5D0 = halfway between current Block 9 and Hermite blend.
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block9_whole_line_blend_68_relax = 1.0D0

    ! Tangent scale relative to the distance between the two Block 9 edges.
    ! Larger = stronger endpoint radial-direction matching.
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block9_whole_line_blend_tangent_scale = 0.35D0

    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: block89_b8_ref_depth = 4
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block89_b8_ref_relax = 0.22D0
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: block89_b8_ref_retries = 6
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: c1_match_tolerance_fraction = 0.20D0
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: c1_match_relaxation = 0.35D0
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: c1_angle_max_degrees = 12.0D0
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: c1_reverse_spline_nfit = 5
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: c1_skewness_limit = 0.40D0
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: c1_skewness_retry_count = 5

    ! Track the best global C1-angle state during the smoothing loop and
    ! restore it if later iterations oversmooth/drift and make the C1 angle
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: c1_best_state_rollback_enabled = .TRUE.

    ! Do not enforce or track C1 continuity at the Block 6--9 or Block 8--9 interfaces.
    ! Position continuity is still kept by normal block connectivity, but the
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: disable_c1_at_block6_block9_interface = .TRUE.  ! also skips Block 8--9

    ! Restore the best saved state at the end if the current C1 angle is more
    ! than this many degrees worse than the best state seen.
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: c1_best_state_restore_tolerance_degrees = 1.0D0

    ! Stop the main smoothing loop if this many consecutive iterations fail
    ! to improve the best global C1 angle.
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: c1_best_state_patience = 12
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: global_skewness_repair_limit = 0.40D0
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: global_skewness_stagnation_tol = 1.0D-12
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: global_skewness_stagnation_limit = 8
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: global_skewness_repair_passes = 40
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: global_skewness_repair_relax = 0.12D0
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: block89_patch_depth = 12
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: block89_patch_passes = 80
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block89_patch_relax = 0.10D0
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: block89_patch_skew_limit = 0.40D0

    ! 3D extrusion controls.  If enabled, the 2D blocks are extruded in z.
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: extrude_to_3d_enabled = .TRUE.

    ! Output option:
    ! .FALSE. = write the current structured hex zones.
    LOGICAL, PARAMETER :: write_tetrahedral_grid_enabled = .FALSE.
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: extrusion_z_spacing_factor = 8.0D0
    REAL(8), PARAMETER :: extrusion_edge_percentile = 0.20D0
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: extrusion_min_nplanes = 2
    INTEGER, PARAMETER :: extrusion_max_nplanes = 129
    REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xprof(:), yprof(:)

    INTEGER :: npts
    INTEGER :: nwall
    INTEGER :: nnormal

    TYPE(block_type) :: blk(18)

    ! meshing_iw-style topology tables.
    ! side: 1=i-min, 2=i-max, 3=j-min, 4=j-max.
    INTEGER :: next_block(9,4), next_patch(9,4)

    CHARACTER(LEN=*), PARAMETER :: input_file  = "geometry_blade.txt"
    CHARACTER(LEN=*), PARAMETER :: output_file = "nine_block.cgns"

    CALL read_blade_geometry(input_file, pitch, xprof, yprof, npts)

    CALL initialise_nine_block_topology(next_block, next_patch)

    ! Default spanwise extrusion length: one blade chord.
    extrusion_span = 0.25D0*(MAXVAL(xprof)-MINVAL(xprof))

    ! Normal extrusion distance from the blade.
    ! Keep this modest; large normal offsets are the main cause of folded cells.
    Lo = 0.075D0

    ! Target first-cell wall-normal spacing used by the
    ! Smaller values cluster more j-lines close to the blade.
    ywall = 0.0025D0*Lo

    ! Number of surface points per blade block.
    nwall = 181

    ! Number of points away from the blade.
    nnormal = 61

    ! Larger value clusters more points in high-curvature regions.
    curvature_strength = 0.10D0

    CALL make_four_normal_blade_blocks( &
        xprof, yprof, npts, Lo, nwall, nnormal, &
        curvature_strength, blk &
    )

    ! Choose the spanwise grid spacing from a small representative 2D edge length.
    ! This avoids using one tiny near-wall/corner edge and creating thousands
    CALL compute_small_2d_cell_edge_length(blk, small_2d_cell_edge_length)

    IF (small_2d_cell_edge_length > 1.0D-14) THEN
        extrusion_nplanes_actual = CEILING( &
            extrusion_span/(extrusion_z_spacing_factor*small_2d_cell_edge_length) &
        ) + 1
    ELSE
        extrusion_nplanes_actual = extrusion_min_nplanes
    END IF

    extrusion_nplanes_actual = MAX(extrusion_min_nplanes, extrusion_nplanes_actual)
    extrusion_nplanes_actual = MIN(extrusion_max_nplanes, extrusion_nplanes_actual)

    CALL write_blade_and_four_blocks_to_cgns( &
        output_file, pitch, xprof, yprof, npts, blk, &
        extrude_to_3d_enabled, extrusion_span, extrusion_nplanes_actual &
    )

    PRINT *, "3D extrusion enabled = ", extrude_to_3d_enabled
    PRINT *, "Extrusion span length = ", extrusion_span
    PRINT *, "Small representative 2D edge length = ", small_2d_cell_edge_length
    PRINT *, "2D edge percentile used for z spacing = ", extrusion_edge_percentile
    PRINT *, "Extrusion dz target factor = ", extrusion_z_spacing_factor
    PRINT *, "Extrusion planes = ", extrusion_nplanes_actual
    PRINT *, "Target wall spacing ywall = ", ywall
    PRINT *, "Wall profile projection enabled for blade blocks 5-8."
    PRINT *, "Finished."
    PRINT *, "Wrote: ", TRIM(output_file)

CONTAINS

    ! Active path retained: mesh construction, MIW-style smoothing, movement limiter,
    ! equal-angle corner shrink, wall-profile enforcement, topology matching, diagnostics,
    ! Read blade geometry.
    !     pitch
    SUBROUTINE read_blade_geometry(filename, pitch, x, y, npts)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        CHARACTER(LEN=*), INTENT(IN) :: filename

        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: pitch
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE, INTENT(OUT) :: x(:), y(:)

        INTEGER, INTENT(OUT) :: npts

        INTEGER :: unit, ios, i
        REAL(8) :: xtmp, ytmp

        OPEN(NEWUNIT=unit, FILE=filename, STATUS="OLD", ACTION="READ", IOSTAT=ios)

        IF (ios /= 0) THEN
            PRINT *, "Could not open: ", TRIM(filename)
            STOP
        END IF

        READ(unit, *, IOSTAT=ios) pitch

        IF (ios /= 0) THEN
            PRINT *, "Could not read pitch."
            STOP
        END IF

        npts = 0

        DO
            READ(unit, *, IOSTAT=ios) xtmp, ytmp
            IF (ios /= 0) EXIT
            npts = npts + 1
        END DO

        CLOSE(unit)

        IF (npts < 4) THEN
            PRINT *, "Too few blade points."
            STOP
        END IF

        ALLOCATE(x(npts), y(npts))

        OPEN(NEWUNIT=unit, FILE=filename, STATUS="OLD", ACTION="READ", IOSTAT=ios)

        READ(unit, *, IOSTAT=ios) pitch

        IF (ios /= 0) THEN
            PRINT *, "Could not reread pitch."
            STOP
        END IF

        DO i = 1, npts
            READ(unit, *, IOSTAT=ios) x(i), y(i)

            IF (ios /= 0) THEN
                PRINT *, "Error reading point ", i
                STOP
            END IF
        END DO

        CLOSE(unit)

        ! Remove a repeated final point. The blade is closed explicitly later.
        IF (npts > 2) THEN
            IF (ABS(x(1)-x(npts)) < 1.0D-12 .AND. &
                ABS(y(1)-y(npts)) < 1.0D-12) THEN
                npts = npts - 1
            END IF
        END IF

        PRINT *, "Blade points = ", npts
        PRINT *, "Pitch        = ", pitch

    END SUBROUTINE read_blade_geometry

    ! Create the four blocks surrounding the blade.
    ! Block numbering in the CGNS file:
    SUBROUTINE make_four_normal_blade_blocks( &
        xprof, yprof, npts, Lo, nwall, nnormal, &
        curvature_strength, blk &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: npts
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nwall
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nnormal

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xprof(npts), yprof(npts)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: Lo
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: curvature_strength

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)

        INTEGER :: i, j
        INTEGER :: iLE, iTE
        INTEGER :: nupper, nlower
        INTEGER :: iuf, iur, ilf, ilr
        INTEGER :: nring
        INTEGER :: nclosed
        INTEGER :: i0

        INTEGER :: n5wall, n6wall, n7wall, n8wall
        INTEGER :: nseg5, nseg6, nseg7, nseg8

        INTEGER :: split_index(4)
        REAL(8) :: edge_coeff(4,5)

        ! Blade-block cap fractions.
        ! These control where Blocks 5, 6, 7, and 8 split along
        REAL(8), PARAMETER :: leading_edge_top_cap_fraction      = 0.025D0
        REAL(8), PARAMETER :: leading_edge_bottom_cap_fraction   = 0.002D0
        REAL(8), PARAMETER :: trailing_edge_top_cap_fraction     = 0.008D0
        REAL(8), PARAMETER :: trailing_edge_bottom_cap_fraction  = 0.010D0

        REAL(8) :: xc, yc
        REAL(8) :: ex, ey, nx_chord, ny_chord
        REAL(8) :: signed_area
        REAL(8) :: eta, distance

        LOGICAL :: contour_is_ccw

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xu(:), yu(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xl(:), yl(:)

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x5raw(:), y5raw(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x6raw(:), y6raw(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x7raw(:), y7raw(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x8raw(:), y8raw(:)

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x5(:), y5(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x6(:), y6(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x7(:), y7(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x8(:), y8(:)

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xclosed(:), yclosed(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: nxclosed(:), nyclosed(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xgrid(:,:), ygrid(:,:)

        CALL find_chord_axis_and_edges( &
            xprof, yprof, npts, xc, yc, ex, ey, &
            nx_chord, ny_chord, iLE, iTE &
        )

        CALL split_profile_upper_lower( &
            xprof, yprof, npts, iLE, iTE, &
            nx_chord, ny_chord, xu, yu, xl, yl &
        )

        nupper = SIZE(xu)
        nlower = SIZE(xl)

        ! Surface split points.
        ! Blocks are still:
        iuf = MAX(2, NINT(leading_edge_top_cap_fraction*REAL(nupper-1,8)) + 1)
        iur = MIN(nupper-1, &
              NINT((1.0D0-trailing_edge_top_cap_fraction)*REAL(nupper-1,8)) + 1)

        ilf = MAX(2, NINT(leading_edge_bottom_cap_fraction*REAL(nlower-1,8)) + 1)
        ilr = MIN(nlower-1, &
              NINT((1.0D0-trailing_edge_bottom_cap_fraction)*REAL(nlower-1,8)) + 1)

        IF (iuf >= iur .OR. ilf >= ilr) THEN
            PRINT *, "Bad surface split indices."
            PRINT *, "iuf, iur = ", iuf, iur
            PRINT *, "ilf, ilr = ", ilf, ilr
            STOP
        END IF

        ! Block 5 wall: lower-front -> LE -> upper-front.
        CALL join_two_curves( &
            xl(ilf:1:-1), yl(ilf:1:-1), &
            xu(2:iuf), yu(2:iuf), &
            x5raw, y5raw &
        )

        ! Block 6 wall: upper-front -> upper-rear.
        ALLOCATE(x6raw(iur-iuf+1), y6raw(iur-iuf+1))
        x6raw = xu(iuf:iur)
        y6raw = yu(iuf:iur)

        ! Block 7 wall: upper-rear -> TE -> lower-rear.
        CALL join_two_curves( &
            xu(iur:nupper), yu(iur:nupper), &
            xl(nlower-1:ilr:-1), yl(nlower-1:ilr:-1), &
            x7raw, y7raw &
        )

        ! Block 8 wall: lower-rear -> lower-front.
        ALLOCATE(x8raw(ilr-ilf+1), y8raw(ilr-ilf+1))
        x8raw = xl(ilr:ilf:-1)
        y8raw = yl(ilr:ilf:-1)

        CALL choose_blade_block_point_counts( &
            x5raw, y5raw, SIZE(x5raw), &
            x6raw, y6raw, SIZE(x6raw), &
            x7raw, y7raw, SIZE(x7raw), &
            x8raw, y8raw, SIZE(x8raw), &
            nwall, curvature_strength, &
            n5wall, n6wall, n7wall, n8wall &
        )

        ALLOCATE(x5(n5wall), y5(n5wall))
        ALLOCATE(x6(n6wall), y6(n6wall))
        ALLOCATE(x7(n7wall), y7(n7wall))
        ALLOCATE(x8(n8wall), y8(n8wall))

        CALL resample_curve_curvature(x5raw, y5raw, SIZE(x5raw), n5wall, curvature_strength, x5, y5)
        CALL resample_curve_curvature(x6raw, y6raw, SIZE(x6raw), n6wall, curvature_strength, x6, y6)
        CALL resample_curve_curvature(x7raw, y7raw, SIZE(x7raw), n7wall, curvature_strength, x7, y7)
        CALL resample_curve_curvature(x8raw, y8raw, SIZE(x8raw), n8wall, curvature_strength, x8, y8)

        ! Force shared wall endpoints to be exactly identical.
        x6(1) = x5(n5wall)
        y6(1) = y5(n5wall)

        x7(1) = x6(n6wall)
        y7(1) = y6(n6wall)

        x8(1) = x7(n7wall)
        y8(1) = y7(n7wall)

        x5(1) = x8(n8wall)
        y5(1) = y8(n8wall)

        ! nseg = number of intervals in each final block.
        !   Block 5: 1              -> 1+nseg
        nseg5 = n5wall - 1
        nseg6 = n6wall - 1
        nseg7 = n7wall - 1
        nseg8 = n8wall - 1

        nring = nseg5 + nseg6 + nseg7 + nseg8
        nclosed = nring + 1

        ! Shared block-interface indices in the single continuous ring.
        ! A = Block 8/5 interface
        split_index(1) = 1
        split_index(2) = nseg5 + 1
        split_index(3) = nseg5 + nseg6 + 1
        split_index(4) = nseg5 + nseg6 + nseg7 + 1

        ! Coefficient-controlled shared block edges.
        ! edge_coeff(edge_id, coefficient_id)
        CALL initialise_edge_coefficients(edge_coeff)

        ALLOCATE(xclosed(nclosed), yclosed(nclosed))
        ALLOCATE(nxclosed(nclosed), nyclosed(nclosed))
        ALLOCATE(xgrid(nclosed,nnormal), ygrid(nclosed,nnormal))

        ! Segment 5, including A and B.
        DO i = 1, n5wall
            xclosed(i) = x5(i)
            yclosed(i) = y5(i)
        END DO

        ! Segment 6, skip duplicate B.
        i0 = nseg5
        DO i = 2, n6wall
            xclosed(i0+i) = x6(i)
            yclosed(i0+i) = y6(i)
        END DO

        ! Segment 7, skip duplicate C.
        i0 = nseg5 + nseg6
        DO i = 2, n7wall
            xclosed(i0+i) = x7(i)
            yclosed(i0+i) = y7(i)
        END DO

        ! Segment 8, skip duplicate D, include final A.
        i0 = nseg5 + nseg6 + nseg7
        DO i = 2, n8wall
            xclosed(i0+i) = x8(i)
            yclosed(i0+i) = y8(i)
        END DO

        ! Final point must close exactly.
        xclosed(nclosed) = xclosed(1)
        yclosed(nclosed) = yclosed(1)

        CALL compute_closed_polyline_signed_area(xclosed, yclosed, nclosed, signed_area)
        contour_is_ccw = signed_area > 0.0D0

        PRINT *, "Closed O-grid ring signed area = ", signed_area

        ! Compute normals segment-by-segment from the actual wall curves
        ! of Blocks 5, 6, 7, and 8.
        CALL compute_segmented_wall_normals( &
            x5, y5, x6, y6, x7, y7, x8, y8, &
            nclosed, split_index, contour_is_ccw, &
            nxclosed, nyclosed &
        )

        ! Build the whole near-blade O-grid at once.
        ! No separate block interface construction happens here.
        DO j = 1, nnormal

            eta = REAL(j-1, KIND=8)/REAL(nnormal-1, KIND=8)
            distance = Lo*eta**1.35D0

            DO i = 1, nclosed
                xgrid(i,j) = xclosed(i) + distance*nxclosed(i)
                ygrid(i,j) = yclosed(i) + distance*nyclosed(i)
            END DO

        END DO

        ! Ensure exact periodic closure of the ring.
        xgrid(nclosed,:) = xgrid(1,:)
        ygrid(nclosed,:) = ygrid(1,:)

        CALL apply_shared_edge_coefficients( &
            xgrid, ygrid, nclosed, nnormal, split_index, edge_coeff, Lo &
        )

        ! Allocate and slice the near-blade ring into Blocks 5-8.
        ALLOCATE(blk(5)%x(n5wall,nnormal), blk(5)%y(n5wall,nnormal))
        ALLOCATE(blk(6)%x(n6wall,nnormal), blk(6)%y(n6wall,nnormal))
        ALLOCATE(blk(7)%x(n7wall,nnormal), blk(7)%y(n7wall,nnormal))
        ALLOCATE(blk(8)%x(n8wall,nnormal), blk(8)%y(n8wall,nnormal))

        ! Block 5: A -> B.
        DO j = 1, nnormal
            DO i = 1, n5wall
                blk(5)%x(i,j) = xgrid(i,j)
                blk(5)%y(i,j) = ygrid(i,j)
            END DO
        END DO

        ! Block 6: B -> C.
        DO j = 1, nnormal
            DO i = 1, n6wall
                blk(6)%x(i,j) = xgrid(nseg5+i,j)
                blk(6)%y(i,j) = ygrid(nseg5+i,j)
            END DO
        END DO

        ! Block 7: C -> D.
        DO j = 1, nnormal
            DO i = 1, n7wall
                blk(7)%x(i,j) = xgrid(nseg5+nseg6+i,j)
                blk(7)%y(i,j) = ygrid(nseg5+nseg6+i,j)
            END DO
        END DO

        ! Block 8: D -> A.
        DO j = 1, nnormal
            DO i = 1, n8wall
                blk(8)%x(i,j) = xgrid(nseg5+nseg6+nseg7+i,j)
                blk(8)%y(i,j) = ygrid(nseg5+nseg6+nseg7+i,j)
            END DO
        END DO

        ! Add the rest of the passage blocks around the near-blade ring:
        !   1 = inlet upper block, attached to Block 5 outer edge
        PRINT *, "Curvature-proportional blade wall counts 5,6,7,8 = ", &
                 n5wall, n6wall, n7wall, n8wall

        CALL make_outer_passage_blocks(blk, nwall, nnormal, pitch)

        ! Step 3 constraint projection:
        ! keep the far-left and far-right block boundaries vertical.
        CALL enforce_outer_vertical_boundaries(blk)

        ! apply the two-stage Poisson smoothing loop only to Block 5.
        ! Add extra i-direction lines in far-field blocks 1--4.
        ! These appear as additional vertical grid lines in the inlet/outlet blocks.
        CALL refine_farfield_vertical_lines(blk, farfield_vertical_line_refinement)

        CALL optimise_miw_full_loop(blk, pitch, 260, 0.30D0, ywall, xprof, yprof, npts)

    ! Boundary handling.
        CALL make_lower_pitch_visual_blocks(blk, pitch)

        CALL check_block_jacobian(blk(1), 1)
        CALL check_block_jacobian(blk(2), 2)
        CALL check_block_jacobian(blk(3), 3)
        CALL check_block_jacobian(blk(4), 4)
        CALL check_block_jacobian(blk(5), 5)
        CALL check_block_jacobian(blk(6), 6)
        CALL check_block_jacobian(blk(7), 7)
        CALL check_block_jacobian(blk(8), 8)
        CALL check_block_jacobian(blk(9), 9)

        PRINT *, "Leading edge top cap fraction     = ", leading_edge_top_cap_fraction
        PRINT *, "Leading edge bottom cap fraction  = ", leading_edge_bottom_cap_fraction
        PRINT *, "Trailing edge top cap fraction    = ", trailing_edge_top_cap_fraction
        PRINT *, "Trailing edge bottom cap fraction = ", trailing_edge_bottom_cap_fraction
        PRINT *, "Upper split indices   = ", iuf, iur, " of ", nupper
        PRINT *, "Lower split indices   = ", ilf, ilr, " of ", nlower
        PRINT *, "Leading-edge index    = ", iLE
        PRINT *, "Trailing-edge index   = ", iTE
        PRINT *, "Blade-block wall point counts 5,6,7,8 = ", n5wall, n6wall, n7wall, n8wall
        PRINT *, "Continuous ring points= ", nclosed
        PRINT *, "Edge A coeffs = ", edge_coeff(1,:)
        PRINT *, "Edge B coeffs = ", edge_coeff(2,:)
        PRINT *, "Edge C coeffs = ", edge_coeff(3,:)
        PRINT *, "Edge D coeffs = ", edge_coeff(4,:)

        DEALLOCATE(xu, yu, xl, yl)
        DEALLOCATE(x5raw, y5raw, x6raw, y6raw, x7raw, y7raw, x8raw, y8raw)
        DEALLOCATE(x5, y5, x6, y6, x7, y7, x8, y8)
        DEALLOCATE(xclosed, yclosed, nxclosed, nyclosed, xgrid, ygrid)

    END SUBROUTINE make_four_normal_blade_blocks

    SUBROUTINE compute_closed_polyline_signed_area(x, y, n, area)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: n
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x(n), y(n)
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: area

        INTEGER :: i

        area = 0.0D0

        DO i = 1, n-1
            area = area + x(i)*y(i+1) - x(i+1)*y(i)
        END DO

        area = 0.5D0*area

    END SUBROUTINE compute_closed_polyline_signed_area

    SUBROUTINE compute_segmented_wall_normals( &
        x5, y5, x6, y6, x7, y7, x8, y8, &
        nclosed, split_index, contour_is_ccw, nxclosed, nyclosed &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nclosed
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: split_index(4)

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x5(:), y5(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x6(:), y6(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x7(:), y7(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x8(:), y8(:)

        LOGICAL, INTENT(IN) :: contour_is_ccw

        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: nxclosed(nclosed), nyclosed(nclosed)

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: nx5(:), ny5(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: nx6(:), ny6(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: nx7(:), ny7(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: nx8(:), ny8(:)

        INTEGER :: i, idx
        INTEGER :: n5, n6, n7, n8
        INTEGER :: nseg5, nseg6, nseg7

        n5 = SIZE(x5)
        n6 = SIZE(x6)
        n7 = SIZE(x7)
        n8 = SIZE(x8)

        nseg5 = n5 - 1
        nseg6 = n6 - 1
        nseg7 = n7 - 1

        ALLOCATE(nx5(n5), ny5(n5))
        ALLOCATE(nx6(n6), ny6(n6))
        ALLOCATE(nx7(n7), ny7(n7))
        ALLOCATE(nx8(n8), ny8(n8))

        CALL compute_open_oriented_normals(x5, y5, n5, contour_is_ccw, nx5, ny5)
        CALL compute_open_oriented_normals(x6, y6, n6, contour_is_ccw, nx6, ny6)
        CALL compute_open_oriented_normals(x7, y7, n7, contour_is_ccw, nx7, ny7)
        CALL compute_open_oriented_normals(x8, y8, n8, contour_is_ccw, nx8, ny8)

        DO i = 1, n5
            nxclosed(i) = nx5(i)
            nyclosed(i) = ny5(i)
        END DO

        DO i = 2, n6
            idx = nseg5 + i
            nxclosed(idx) = nx6(i)
            nyclosed(idx) = ny6(i)
        END DO

        DO i = 2, n7
            idx = nseg5 + nseg6 + i
            nxclosed(idx) = nx7(i)
            nyclosed(idx) = ny7(i)
        END DO

        DO i = 2, n8
            idx = nseg5 + nseg6 + nseg7 + i
            nxclosed(idx) = nx8(i)
            nyclosed(idx) = ny8(i)
        END DO

        ! Use cap-wall endpoint normals at the radial split edges.
        nxclosed(split_index(1)) = nx5(1)
        nyclosed(split_index(1)) = ny5(1)

        nxclosed(split_index(2)) = nx5(n5)
        nyclosed(split_index(2)) = ny5(n5)

        nxclosed(split_index(3)) = nx7(1)
        nyclosed(split_index(3)) = ny7(1)

        nxclosed(split_index(4)) = nx7(n7)
        nyclosed(split_index(4)) = ny7(n7)

        nxclosed(nclosed) = nxclosed(1)
        nyclosed(nclosed) = nyclosed(1)

        DEALLOCATE(nx5, ny5, nx6, ny6, nx7, ny7, nx8, ny8)

    END SUBROUTINE compute_segmented_wall_normals

    SUBROUTINE compute_open_oriented_normals(x, y, n, contour_is_ccw, nx, ny)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: n

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x(n), y(n)
        LOGICAL, INTENT(IN) :: contour_is_ccw

        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: nx(n), ny(n)

        INTEGER :: i
        REAL(8) :: tx, ty
        REAL(8) :: mag

        IF (n < 2) THEN
            PRINT *, "Too few points in compute_open_oriented_normals."
            STOP
        END IF

        DO i = 1, n

            IF (i == 1) THEN
                tx = x(2) - x(1)
                ty = y(2) - y(1)
            ELSE IF (i == n) THEN
                tx = x(n) - x(n-1)
                ty = y(n) - y(n-1)
            ELSE
                tx = x(i+1) - x(i-1)
                ty = y(i+1) - y(i-1)
            END IF

            mag = SQRT(tx*tx + ty*ty)

            IF (mag <= 1.0D-14) THEN
                nx(i) = 0.0D0
                ny(i) = 0.0D0
            ELSE
                tx = tx/mag
                ty = ty/mag

                IF (contour_is_ccw) THEN
                    nx(i) =  ty
                    ny(i) = -tx
                ELSE
                    nx(i) = -ty
                    ny(i) =  tx
                END IF
            END IF

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE compute_open_oriented_normals

    SUBROUTINE initialise_edge_coefficients(edge_coeff)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: edge_coeff(4,5)

        !   edge_coeff(edge, 1:5)
        edge_coeff = 0.0D0

        ! edge_coeff(2,3) =  0.05D0
        ! edge_coeff(3,3) = -0.05D0
    END SUBROUTINE initialise_edge_coefficients

    SUBROUTINE apply_shared_edge_coefficients( &
        xgrid, ygrid, nclosed, nj, split_index, edge_coeff, Lo &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nclosed, nj
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: split_index(4)

        REAL(8), INTENT(INOUT) :: xgrid(nclosed,nj), ygrid(nclosed,nj)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: edge_coeff(4,5)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: Lo

        INTEGER :: e, j, idx
        REAL(8) :: s
        REAL(8) :: dx, dy, mag
        REAL(8) :: px, py
        REAL(8) :: amp

        DO e = 1, 4

            idx = split_index(e)

            dx = xgrid(idx,nj) - xgrid(idx,1)
            dy = ygrid(idx,nj) - ygrid(idx,1)

            mag = SQRT(dx*dx + dy*dy)

            IF (mag <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE

            ! Sideways direction for this radial interface.
            ! bends the block edge.
            px = -dy/mag
            py =  dx/mag

            DO j = 2, nj-1

                s = REAL(j-1, KIND=8)/REAL(nj-1, KIND=8)

                amp = Lo * edge_shape_5coeff(s, edge_coeff(e,:))

                xgrid(idx,j) = xgrid(idx,j) + amp*px
                ygrid(idx,j) = ygrid(idx,j) + amp*py

            END DO

        END DO

        ! Keep periodic closure exact.
        xgrid(nclosed,:) = xgrid(1,:)
        ygrid(nclosed,:) = ygrid(1,:)

    END SUBROUTINE apply_shared_edge_coefficients

    REAL(8) FUNCTION edge_shape_5coeff(s, coeff)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: s
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: coeff(5)

        REAL(8) :: b0, b1, b2, b3, b4
        REAL(8) :: p

        ! Five Bernstein basis functions of degree 4.
        ! Multiplication by s*(1-s) keeps both endpoints fixed.
        b0 = (1.0D0 - s)**4
        b1 = 4.0D0*s*(1.0D0 - s)**3
        b2 = 6.0D0*s**2*(1.0D0 - s)**2
        b3 = 4.0D0*s**3*(1.0D0 - s)
        b4 = s**4

        p = coeff(1)*b0 + coeff(2)*b1 + coeff(3)*b2 + &
            coeff(4)*b3 + coeff(5)*b4

        edge_shape_5coeff = s*(1.0D0 - s)*p

    END FUNCTION edge_shape_5coeff

    SUBROUTINE find_chord_axis_and_edges( &
        x, y, n, xc, yc, ex, ey, nx, ny, iLE, iTE &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: n
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x(n), y(n)

        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: xc, yc
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: ex, ey
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: nx, ny

        INTEGER, INTENT(OUT) :: iLE, iTE

        REAL(8) :: cxx, cyy, cxy
        REAL(8) :: trace, root, lambda
        REAL(8) :: mag
        REAL(8) :: s, smin, smax

        INTEGER :: i

        xc = SUM(x)/REAL(n,8)
        yc = SUM(y)/REAL(n,8)

        cxx = 0.0D0
        cyy = 0.0D0
        cxy = 0.0D0

        DO i = 1, n
            cxx = cxx + (x(i)-xc)**2
            cyy = cyy + (y(i)-yc)**2
            cxy = cxy + (x(i)-xc)*(y(i)-yc)
        END DO

        cxx = cxx/REAL(n,8)
        cyy = cyy/REAL(n,8)
        cxy = cxy/REAL(n,8)

        trace = cxx + cyy
        root = SQRT((cxx-cyy)**2 + 4.0D0*cxy*cxy)
        lambda = 0.5D0*(trace + root)

        IF (ABS(cxy) > 1.0D-14) THEN
            ex = lambda - cyy
            ey = cxy
        ELSE IF (cxx >= cyy) THEN
            ex = 1.0D0
            ey = 0.0D0
        ELSE
            ex = 0.0D0
            ey = 1.0D0
        END IF

        mag = SQRT(ex*ex + ey*ey)

        IF (mag <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            PRINT *, "Bad chord axis."
            STOP
        END IF

        ex = ex/mag
        ey = ey/mag

        nx = -ey
        ny =  ex

        smin = HUGE(1.0D0)
        smax = -HUGE(1.0D0)

        DO i = 1, n
            s = (x(i)-xc)*ex + (y(i)-yc)*ey

            IF (s < smin) THEN
                smin = s
                iLE = i
            END IF

            IF (s > smax) THEN
                smax = s
                iTE = i
            END IF
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE find_chord_axis_and_edges

    SUBROUTINE split_profile_upper_lower( &
        x, y, n, iLE, iTE, nx, ny, xu, yu, xl, yl &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: n, iLE, iTE
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x(n), y(n)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: nx, ny

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE, INTENT(OUT) :: xu(:), yu(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE, INTENT(OUT) :: xl(:), yl(:)

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xf(:), yf(:), xb(:), yb(:)
        REAL(8) :: meanf, meanb

        CALL extract_cyclic_path(x, y, n, iLE, iTE, 1, xf, yf)
        CALL extract_cyclic_path(x, y, n, iLE, iTE, -1, xb, yb)

        meanf = SUM(xf*nx + yf*ny)/REAL(SIZE(xf),8)
        meanb = SUM(xb*nx + yb*ny)/REAL(SIZE(xb),8)

        IF (meanf >= meanb) THEN
            CALL move_alloc(xf, xu)
            CALL move_alloc(yf, yu)
            CALL move_alloc(xb, xl)
            CALL move_alloc(yb, yl)
        ELSE
            CALL move_alloc(xb, xu)
            CALL move_alloc(yb, yu)
            CALL move_alloc(xf, xl)
            CALL move_alloc(yf, yl)
        END IF

    END SUBROUTINE split_profile_upper_lower

    SUBROUTINE extract_cyclic_path( &
        x, y, n, istart, iend, direction, xp, yp &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: n, istart, iend, direction
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x(n), y(n)

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE, INTENT(OUT) :: xp(:), yp(:)

        INTEGER :: idx, count, k

        idx = istart
        count = 1

        DO WHILE (idx /= iend)

            idx = idx + direction

            IF (idx < 1) idx = n
            IF (idx > n) idx = 1

            count = count + 1

            IF (count > n+1) THEN
                PRINT *, "Cyclic path error."
                STOP
            END IF

        END DO

        ALLOCATE(xp(count), yp(count))

        idx = istart

        DO k = 1, count

            xp(k) = x(idx)
            yp(k) = y(idx)

            IF (idx == iend) EXIT

            idx = idx + direction

            IF (idx < 1) idx = n
            IF (idx > n) idx = 1

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE extract_cyclic_path

    SUBROUTINE join_two_curves(x1, y1, x2, y2, xo, yo)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x1(:), y1(:), x2(:), y2(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE, INTENT(OUT) :: xo(:), yo(:)

        INTEGER :: n1, n2

        n1 = SIZE(x1)
        n2 = SIZE(x2)

        ALLOCATE(xo(n1+n2), yo(n1+n2))

        xo(1:n1) = x1
        yo(1:n1) = y1

        xo(n1+1:n1+n2) = x2
        yo(n1+1:n1+n2) = y2

    END SUBROUTINE join_two_curves

    SUBROUTINE choose_blade_block_point_counts( &
        x5, y5, n5, x6, y6, n6, x7, y7, n7, x8, y8, n8, &
        nominal_nwall, alpha, n5out, n6out, n7out, n8out &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: n5, n6, n7, n8
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nominal_nwall

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x5(n5), y5(n5)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x6(n6), y6(n6)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x7(n7), y7(n7)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x8(n8), y8(n8)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: alpha

        INTEGER, INTENT(OUT) :: n5out, n6out, n7out, n8out

        INTEGER :: total_intervals
        INTEGER :: min_intervals
        INTEGER :: s5, s6, s7, s8
        INTEGER :: diff, idir, kbest
        REAL(8) :: c5, c6, c7, c8, c68, csum
        REAL(8) :: target5, target6, target7, target8
        REAL(8) :: frac5, frac6, frac7
        REAL(8) :: bestfrac

        min_intervals = 12
        total_intervals = MAX(4*min_intervals, 4*(nominal_nwall-1))

        CALL curve_curvature_complexity(x5, y5, n5, alpha, c5)
        CALL curve_curvature_complexity(x6, y6, n6, alpha, c6)
        CALL curve_curvature_complexity(x7, y7, n7, alpha, c7)
        CALL curve_curvature_complexity(x8, y8, n8, alpha, c8)

        ! Block 9 needs Block 6 and Block 8 to have matching streamwise
        ! point counts, so use one combined complexity for both.
        c68 = 0.5D0*(c6 + c8)
        csum = c5 + c7 + 2.0D0*c68

        IF (csum <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            s5 = nominal_nwall - 1
            s6 = nominal_nwall - 1
            s7 = nominal_nwall - 1
            s8 = nominal_nwall - 1
        ELSE
            target5 = REAL(total_intervals,8)*c5/csum
            target6 = REAL(total_intervals,8)*c68/csum
            target7 = REAL(total_intervals,8)*c7/csum
            target8 = target6

            s5 = MAX(min_intervals, NINT(target5))
            s6 = MAX(min_intervals, NINT(target6))
            s7 = MAX(min_intervals, NINT(target7))
            s8 = s6

            ! Enforce exact total interval count while preserving s6=s8.
            diff = total_intervals - (s5 + s6 + s7 + s8)

            DO WHILE (diff /= 0)

                IF (diff > 0) THEN
                    frac5 = target5 - REAL(s5,8)
                    frac6 = target6 - REAL(s6,8)
                    frac7 = target7 - REAL(s7,8)

                    kbest = 5
                    bestfrac = frac5
                    IF (frac6 > bestfrac) THEN
                        kbest = 6
                        bestfrac = frac6
                    END IF
                    IF (frac7 > bestfrac) THEN
                        kbest = 7
                        bestfrac = frac7
                    END IF

                    IF (kbest == 5) THEN
                        s5 = s5 + 1
                        diff = diff - 1
                    ELSE IF (kbest == 6 .AND. diff >= 2) THEN
                        s6 = s6 + 1
                        s8 = s8 + 1
                        diff = diff - 2
                    ELSE
                        s7 = s7 + 1
                        diff = diff - 1
                    END IF

                ELSE

                    idir = 0
                    IF (s5 > min_intervals) idir = 5
                    IF (s7 > min_intervals .AND. (idir == 0 .OR. s7 > s5)) idir = 7
                    IF (s6 > min_intervals .AND. diff <= -2) THEN
                        IF (idir == 0 .OR. s6 > MAX(s5,s7)) idir = 6
                    END IF

                    IF (idir == 5) THEN
                        s5 = s5 - 1
                        diff = diff + 1
                    ELSE IF (idir == 6) THEN
                        s6 = s6 - 1
                        s8 = s8 - 1
                        diff = diff + 2
                    ELSE IF (idir == 7) THEN
                        s7 = s7 - 1
                        diff = diff + 1
                    ELSE
                        EXIT
                    END IF

                END IF

            END DO

        END IF

        n5out = s5 + 1
        n6out = s6 + 1
        n7out = s7 + 1
        n8out = s8 + 1

    END SUBROUTINE choose_blade_block_point_counts

    SUBROUTINE curve_curvature_complexity(x, y, n, alpha, complexity)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: n
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x(n), y(n)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: alpha
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: complexity

        REAL(8) :: dx1, dy1, dx2, dy2
        REAL(8) :: l1, l2, ds, dotp, angle, kappa
        INTEGER :: i

        complexity = 0.0D0

        IF (n < 2) THEN
            complexity = 1.0D0
            RETURN
        END IF

        DO i = 2, n
            ds = SQRT((x(i)-x(i-1))**2 + (y(i)-y(i-1))**2)

            kappa = 0.0D0
            IF (i > 1 .AND. i < n) THEN
                dx1 = x(i)-x(i-1)
                dy1 = y(i)-y(i-1)
                dx2 = x(i+1)-x(i)
                dy2 = y(i+1)-y(i)

                l1 = SQRT(dx1*dx1 + dy1*dy1)
                l2 = SQRT(dx2*dx2 + dy2*dy2)

                IF (l1 > 1.0D-14 .AND. l2 > 1.0D-14) THEN
                    dotp = (dx1*dx2 + dy1*dy2)/(l1*l2)
                    dotp = MAX(-1.0D0, MIN(1.0D0, dotp))
                    angle = ACOS(dotp)
                    kappa = angle/(0.5D0*(l1+l2))
                END IF
            END IF

            complexity = complexity + (1.0D0 + alpha*kappa)*ds
        END DO

        IF (complexity <= 1.0D-14) complexity = 1.0D0

    END SUBROUTINE curve_curvature_complexity

    SUBROUTINE resample_curve_curvature( &
        xin, yin, nin, nout, alpha, xout, yout &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nin, nout
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xin(nin), yin(nin)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: alpha
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: xout(nout), yout(nout)

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: ds(:), kappa(:), rho(:), w(:)

        REAL(8) :: dx1, dy1, dx2, dy2
        REAL(8) :: l1, l2, dotp, angle
        REAL(8) :: target, f

        INTEGER :: i, k

        IF (nin < 2) THEN
            PRINT *, "Too few points in resample_curve_curvature."
            STOP
        END IF

        ALLOCATE(ds(nin), kappa(nin), rho(nin), w(nin))

        ds = 0.0D0
        kappa = 0.0D0
        rho = 1.0D0
        w = 0.0D0

        DO i = 2, nin
            ds(i) = SQRT((xin(i)-xin(i-1))**2 + (yin(i)-yin(i-1))**2)
        END DO

        IF (nin >= 3) THEN

            DO i = 2, nin-1

                dx1 = xin(i)-xin(i-1)
                dy1 = yin(i)-yin(i-1)

                dx2 = xin(i+1)-xin(i)
                dy2 = yin(i+1)-yin(i)

                l1 = SQRT(dx1*dx1 + dy1*dy1)
                l2 = SQRT(dx2*dx2 + dy2*dy2)

                IF (l1 > 1.0D-14 .AND. l2 > 1.0D-14) THEN
                    dotp = (dx1*dx2 + dy1*dy2)/(l1*l2)
                    dotp = MAX(-1.0D0, MIN(1.0D0, dotp))
                    angle = ACOS(dotp)
                    kappa(i) = angle/(0.5D0*(l1+l2))
                END IF

            END DO

            kappa(1) = kappa(2)
            kappa(nin) = kappa(nin-1)

        END IF

        DO i = 1, nin
            rho(i) = 1.0D0 + alpha*kappa(i)
        END DO

        w(1) = 0.0D0

        DO i = 2, nin
            w(i) = w(i-1) + 0.5D0*(rho(i-1)+rho(i))*ds(i)
        END DO

        IF (w(nin) <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            PRINT *, "Zero weighted curve length."
            STOP
        END IF

        k = 1

        DO i = 1, nout

            target = w(nin)*REAL(i-1,8)/REAL(nout-1,8)

            DO WHILE (k < nin-1 .AND. w(k+1) < target)
                k = k + 1
            END DO

            IF (w(k+1) > w(k)) THEN
                f = (target-w(k))/(w(k+1)-w(k))
            ELSE
                f = 0.0D0
            END IF

            xout(i) = (1.0D0-f)*xin(k) + f*xin(k+1)
            yout(i) = (1.0D0-f)*yin(k) + f*yin(k+1)

        END DO

        xout(1) = xin(1)
        yout(1) = yin(1)

        xout(nout) = xin(nin)
        yout(nout) = yin(nin)

        DEALLOCATE(ds, kappa, rho, w)

    END SUBROUTINE resample_curve_curvature

    SUBROUTINE check_block_jacobian(blk, block_number)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: block_number

        INTEGER :: i, j, ni, nj
        REAL(8) :: dxdi, dydi, dxdj, dydj, jac
        REAL(8) :: jacmin, jacmax

        ni = SIZE(blk%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(blk%x,2)

        jacmin = HUGE(1.0D0)
        jacmax = -HUGE(1.0D0)

        DO j = 1, nj-1
            DO i = 1, ni-1

                dxdi = blk%x(i+1,j)-blk%x(i,j)
                dydi = blk%y(i+1,j)-blk%y(i,j)

                dxdj = blk%x(i,j+1)-blk%x(i,j)
                dydj = blk%y(i,j+1)-blk%y(i,j)

                jac = dxdi*dydj-dydi*dxdj

                jacmin = MIN(jacmin, jac)
                jacmax = MAX(jacmax, jac)

            END DO
        END DO

        PRINT *, "Block ", block_number, " Jacobian min/max = ", jacmin, jacmax

        IF (jacmin*jacmax <= 0.0D0) THEN
            PRINT *, "WARNING: Block ", block_number, &
                     " contains folded or degenerate cells."
            PRINT *, "         Reduce Lo, increase nwall, or adjust split fractions."
        END IF

    END SUBROUTINE check_block_jacobian

    ! Write:
    !   Base 1: BladeCurveBase, containing the blade curve
    SUBROUTINE enforce_outer_vertical_boundaries(blk)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)

        INTEGER :: n1j, n2j, n3j, n4j
        INTEGER :: n3i, n4i

        REAL(8) :: xL
        REAL(8) :: xR

    ! Boundary handling.
        n1j = SIZE(blk(1)%x, 2)
        n2j = SIZE(blk(2)%x, 2)
        n3j = SIZE(blk(3)%x, 2)
        n4j = SIZE(blk(4)%x, 2)

        n3i = SIZE(blk(3)%x, 1)
        n4i = SIZE(blk(4)%x, 1)

        ! Leftmost boundary: Block 1 left edge and Block 2 left edge.
        ! Use the average x-value of both existing edges as the vertical
        xL = 0.5D0*( &
            SUM(blk(1)%x(1,:))/REAL(n1j,8) + &
            SUM(blk(2)%x(1,:))/REAL(n2j,8) &
        )

        blk(1)%x(1,:) = xL
        blk(2)%x(1,:) = xL

        ! Rightmost boundary: Block 3 right edge and Block 4 right edge.
        xR = 0.5D0*( &
            SUM(blk(3)%x(n3i,:))/REAL(n3j,8) + &
            SUM(blk(4)%x(n4i,:))/REAL(n4j,8) &
        )

        blk(3)%x(n3i,:) = xR
        blk(4)%x(n4i,:) = xR

    END SUBROUTINE enforce_outer_vertical_boundaries

    SUBROUTINE make_lower_pitch_visual_blocks(blk, pitch)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch

        INTEGER :: ib, ib2
        INTEGER :: ni, nj

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 18) THEN
            PRINT *, "make_lower_pitch_visual_blocks requires at least 18 blocks."
            STOP
        END IF

        ! Blocks 10--18 are exact copies of Blocks 1--9 shifted down by
        ! one pitch.  These are not a new mesh topology; they are only
        DO ib = 1, 9

            ib2 = ib + 9

            ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x, 1)
            nj = SIZE(blk(ib)%x, 2)

            IF (ALLOCATED(blk(ib2)%x)) DEALLOCATE(blk(ib2)%x)
            IF (ALLOCATED(blk(ib2)%y)) DEALLOCATE(blk(ib2)%y)

            ALLOCATE(blk(ib2)%x(ni,nj))
            ALLOCATE(blk(ib2)%y(ni,nj))

            blk(ib2)%x = blk(ib)%x
            blk(ib2)%y = blk(ib)%y - pitch

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE make_lower_pitch_visual_blocks

                SUBROUTINE smooth_block89_patch_poisson_skew_limited(blk, pitch, depth, npass, relax_smooth, skew_limit)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: depth
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: npass
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax_smooth
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: skew_limit

        INTEGER :: side8, side9
        INTEGER :: pass, ib, level, k, nedge
        INTEGER :: inode, jnode
        INTEGER :: nchanged, nbad
        REAL(8) :: max_patch_skew
        LOGICAL :: changed
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xe(:), ye(:)

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 9) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(8)%x)) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(9)%x)) RETURN

        side8 = find_connected_side(8, 9)
        side9 = find_connected_side(9, 8)

        IF (side8 <= 0 .OR. side9 <= 0) THEN
            PRINT *, "Warning: could not find Block 8--9 sides for patch smoothing."
            RETURN
        END IF

        ! Re-match the actual interface first.  Routine then keeps
        ! the edge fixed and diffuses the correction into a 2D patch.
        CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, 8)
        CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, 9)

        DO pass = 1, npass

            nchanged = 0
            nbad = 0
            max_patch_skew = 0.0D0

            DO ib = 8, 9

                IF (ib == 8) THEN
                    CALL get_edge_line(blk(8), side8, xe, ye)
                ELSE
                    CALL get_edge_line(blk(9), side9, xe, ye)
                END IF

                nedge = SIZE(xe)
                DEALLOCATE(xe, ye)

                ! Skip corner-controlled end stations.  Move a whole
                ! interior band, not just the first line beside the edge.
                DO k = 4, nedge-3
                    DO level = depth, 1, -1

                        IF (ib == 8) THEN
                            CALL get_patch_indices(blk(8), side8, k, level, inode, jnode)
                            CALL try_patch_poisson_node_move(blk(8), inode, jnode, relax_smooth, skew_limit, changed)
                            max_patch_skew = MAX(max_patch_skew, local_node_max_skewness_any(blk(8), inode, jnode))
                        ELSE
                            CALL get_patch_indices(blk(9), side9, k, level, inode, jnode)
                            CALL try_patch_poisson_node_move(blk(9), inode, jnode, relax_smooth, skew_limit, changed)
                            max_patch_skew = MAX(max_patch_skew, local_node_max_skewness_any(blk(9), inode, jnode))
                        END IF

                        IF (max_patch_skew > skew_limit) nbad = nbad + 1
                        IF (changed) nchanged = nchanged + 1

                    END DO
                END DO

            END DO

            ! Keep the interface C0 matched while the 2D patch relaxes.
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, 8)
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, 9)

            PRINT *, "Block 8--9 patch smoother pass, bad, moved, max patch skew = ", &
                     pass, nbad, nchanged, max_patch_skew

            IF (nbad == 0 .OR. nchanged == 0) EXIT

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE smooth_block89_patch_poisson_skew_limited

    SUBROUTINE try_patch_poisson_node_move(b, inode, jnode, relax_smooth, skew_limit, changed)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: inode, jnode
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax_smooth
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: skew_limit
        LOGICAL, INTENT(OUT) :: changed

        INTEGER :: ni, nj, retry
        REAL(8) :: xold, yold, xlap, ylap, xtry, ytry
        REAL(8) :: skew_before, skew_after
        REAL(8) :: min_area_before, min_area_after
        REAL(8) :: fac

        changed = .FALSE.

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        ! Do not move block boundaries.  This prevents the smoother from
        ! cheating by hiding the issue on either side of the interface.
        IF (inode <= 1 .OR. inode >= ni) RETURN
        IF (jnode <= 1 .OR. jnode >= nj) RETURN

        xold = b%x(inode,jnode)
        yold = b%y(inode,jnode)

        skew_before = local_node_max_skewness_any(b, inode, jnode)
        min_area_before = local_node_min_signed_area_trial(b, inode, jnode, xold, yold)

        ! Standard local Poisson/Laplace target: move towards the average
        ! of surrounding nodes, not towards a forced interface line.
        xlap = 0.25D0*(b%x(inode-1,jnode) + b%x(inode+1,jnode) + &
                       b%x(inode,jnode-1) + b%x(inode,jnode+1))
        ylap = 0.25D0*(b%y(inode-1,jnode) + b%y(inode+1,jnode) + &
                       b%y(inode,jnode-1) + b%y(inode,jnode+1))

        fac = relax_smooth

        DO retry = 1, 8

            xtry = xold + fac*(xlap - xold)
            ytry = yold + fac*(ylap - yold)

            skew_after = local_node_max_skewness_any_trial(b, inode, jnode, xtry, ytry)
            min_area_after = local_node_min_signed_area_trial(b, inode, jnode, xtry, ytry)

            ! Accept only if skewness improves and no local cell area is
            ! to worsen already bad cells.
            IF (skew_after < skew_before .AND. min_area_after > 0.0D0) THEN
                b%x(inode,jnode) = xtry
                b%y(inode,jnode) = ytry
                changed = .TRUE.
                RETURN
            END IF

            IF (skew_before <= skew_limit .AND. skew_after <= skew_limit .AND. &
                min_area_after >= 0.75D0*min_area_before) THEN
                b%x(inode,jnode) = xtry
                b%y(inode,jnode) = ytry
                changed = .TRUE.
                RETURN
            END IF

            fac = 0.5D0*fac

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE try_patch_poisson_node_move

    REAL(8) FUNCTION local_node_min_signed_area_trial(b, inode, jnode, xtrial, ytrial)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: inode, jnode
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xtrial, ytrial

        INTEGER :: ni, nj, i, j
        REAL(8) :: area

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        local_node_min_signed_area_trial = HUGE(1.0D0)

        DO j = MAX(1,jnode-1), MIN(nj-1,jnode)
            DO i = MAX(1,inode-1), MIN(ni-1,inode)

                area = quad_signed_area_with_trial_node(b, i, j, inode, jnode, xtrial, ytrial)
                local_node_min_signed_area_trial = MIN(local_node_min_signed_area_trial, area)

            END DO
        END DO

        IF (local_node_min_signed_area_trial == HUGE(1.0D0)) THEN
            local_node_min_signed_area_trial = 0.0D0
        END IF

    END FUNCTION local_node_min_signed_area_trial

    REAL(8) FUNCTION quad_signed_area_with_trial_node(b, i, j, inode, jnode, xtrial, ytrial)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: i, j
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: inode, jnode
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xtrial, ytrial

        REAL(8) :: x1, y1, x2, y2, x3, y3, x4, y4

        x1 = b%x(i,j)
        y1 = b%y(i,j)
        x2 = b%x(i+1,j)
        y2 = b%y(i+1,j)
        x3 = b%x(i+1,j+1)
        y3 = b%y(i+1,j+1)
        x4 = b%x(i,j+1)
        y4 = b%y(i,j+1)

        IF (i == inode .AND. j == jnode) THEN
            x1 = xtrial
            y1 = ytrial
        END IF
        IF (i+1 == inode .AND. j == jnode) THEN
            x2 = xtrial
            y2 = ytrial
        END IF
        IF (i+1 == inode .AND. j+1 == jnode) THEN
            x3 = xtrial
            y3 = ytrial
        END IF
        IF (i == inode .AND. j+1 == jnode) THEN
            x4 = xtrial
            y4 = ytrial
        END IF

        quad_signed_area_with_trial_node = 0.5D0*( &
            x1*y2 - x2*y1 + &
            x2*y3 - x3*y2 + &
            x3*y4 - x4*y3 + &
            x4*y1 - x1*y4 )

    END FUNCTION quad_signed_area_with_trial_node

    INTEGER FUNCTION find_connected_side(ib, jb)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ib, jb
        INTEGER :: side

        find_connected_side = 0

        DO side = 1, 4
            IF (next_block(ib,side) == jb) THEN
                find_connected_side = side
                RETURN
            END IF
        END DO

    END FUNCTION find_connected_side

    SUBROUTINE get_patch_indices(b, side, kedge, offset, inode, jnode)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: kedge
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: offset
        INTEGER, INTENT(OUT) :: inode, jnode

        INTEGER :: ni, nj

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        SELECT CASE (side)
        CASE (1)
            inode = 1 + offset
            jnode = kedge
        CASE (2)
            inode = ni - offset
            jnode = kedge
        CASE (3)
            inode = kedge
            jnode = 1 + offset
        CASE (4)
            inode = kedge
            jnode = nj - offset
        CASE DEFAULT
            PRINT *, "Bad side in get_patch_indices: ", side
            STOP
        END SELECT

        inode = MAX(1, MIN(ni, inode))
        jnode = MAX(1, MIN(nj, jnode))

    END SUBROUTINE get_patch_indices

SUBROUTINE improve_high_skewness_cells(blk, nblock_report, skew_limit, max_passes, relax_smooth)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8) :: previous_max_skew
        INTEGER :: stagnant_count
        INTEGER :: worst_block, worst_i, worst_j

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nblock_report
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: skew_limit
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: max_passes
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax_smooth

        INTEGER :: pass, ib, i, j, ni, nj
        INTEGER :: nchanged, nbad
        REAL(8) :: skew, max_skew
        LOGICAL :: changed_node

        ! This is a skewness repair smoother, not a replacement for
        ! Boundary/interface nodes stay fixed, so block connectivity and
        previous_max_skew = -1.0D0
        stagnant_count = 0

        DO pass = 1, max_passes

            nchanged = 0
            nbad = 0
            max_skew = 0.0D0

            DO ib = 1, MIN(nblock_report, SIZE(blk))

                IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE

                ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1)
                nj = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,2)

                DO j = 1, nj-1
                    DO i = 1, ni-1

                        skew = quad_cell_equiangular_skewness( &
                            blk(ib)%x(i,j),     blk(ib)%y(i,j), &
                            blk(ib)%x(i+1,j),   blk(ib)%y(i+1,j), &
                            blk(ib)%x(i+1,j+1), blk(ib)%y(i+1,j+1), &
                            blk(ib)%x(i,j+1),   blk(ib)%y(i,j+1) )

                        max_skew = MAX(max_skew, skew)

                        IF (skew > skew_limit) THEN

                            nbad = nbad + 1

                            CALL try_skewness_laplace_node_move( &
                                blk(ib), i, j, relax_smooth, changed_node &
                            )
                            IF (changed_node) nchanged = nchanged + 1

                            CALL try_skewness_laplace_node_move( &
                                blk(ib), i+1, j, relax_smooth, changed_node &
                            )
                            IF (changed_node) nchanged = nchanged + 1

                            CALL try_skewness_laplace_node_move( &
                                blk(ib), i, j+1, relax_smooth, changed_node &
                            )
                            IF (changed_node) nchanged = nchanged + 1

                            CALL try_skewness_laplace_node_move( &
                                blk(ib), i+1, j+1, relax_smooth, changed_node &
                            )
                            IF (changed_node) nchanged = nchanged + 1

                        END IF

                    END DO
                END DO

            END DO

            PRINT *, "Skewness repair pass, bad cells, node moves, max skew = ", &
                     pass, nbad, nchanged, max_skew

            CALL find_global_worst_skewness_cell(blk, 9, max_skew, worst_block, worst_i, worst_j)
            PRINT *, "Worst skewness cell location = block, i, j = ", worst_block, worst_i, worst_j

            IF (previous_max_skew > 0.0D0) THEN
                IF (ABS(max_skew - previous_max_skew) <= global_skewness_stagnation_tol) THEN
                    stagnant_count = stagnant_count + 1
                ELSE
                    stagnant_count = 0
                END IF
            END IF

            previous_max_skew = max_skew

            IF (stagnant_count >= global_skewness_stagnation_limit) THEN
                PRINT *, "Stopping global skewness repair: max skewness is stagnant."
                EXIT
            END IF

            IF (nbad == 0 .OR. nchanged == 0) EXIT

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE improve_high_skewness_cells

    SUBROUTINE try_skewness_laplace_node_move(b, inode, jnode, relax_smooth, changed_node)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: inode, jnode
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax_smooth
        LOGICAL, INTENT(OUT) :: changed_node

        INTEGER :: ni, nj, retry
        REAL(8) :: xold, yold, xlap, ylap, xtry, ytry
        REAL(8) :: skew_before, skew_after
        REAL(8) :: fac

        changed_node = .FALSE.

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        ! Do not move block boundaries.  C0 matching, blade wall
        ! projection, and periodic matching own those nodes.
        IF (inode <= 1 .OR. inode >= ni) RETURN
        IF (jnode <= 1 .OR. jnode >= nj) RETURN

        xold = b%x(inode,jnode)
        yold = b%y(inode,jnode)

        skew_before = local_node_max_skewness_any(b, inode, jnode)

        xlap = 0.25D0*(b%x(inode-1,jnode) + b%x(inode+1,jnode) + &
                       b%x(inode,jnode-1) + b%x(inode,jnode+1))
        ylap = 0.25D0*(b%y(inode-1,jnode) + b%y(inode+1,jnode) + &
                       b%y(inode,jnode-1) + b%y(inode,jnode+1))

        fac = relax_smooth

        DO retry = 1, 6

            xtry = xold + fac*(xlap - xold)
            ytry = yold + fac*(ylap - yold)

            skew_after = local_node_max_skewness_any_trial(b, inode, jnode, xtry, ytry)

            IF (skew_after < skew_before) THEN
                b%x(inode,jnode) = xtry
                b%y(inode,jnode) = ytry
                changed_node = .TRUE.
                RETURN
            END IF

            fac = 0.5D0*fac

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE try_skewness_laplace_node_move

    REAL(8) FUNCTION local_node_max_skewness_any(b, inode, jnode)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: inode, jnode

        local_node_max_skewness_any = local_node_max_skewness_any_trial( &
            b, inode, jnode, b%x(inode,jnode), b%y(inode,jnode) &
        )

    END FUNCTION local_node_max_skewness_any

    REAL(8) FUNCTION local_node_max_skewness_any_trial(b, inode, jnode, xtrial, ytrial)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: inode, jnode
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xtrial, ytrial

        INTEGER :: ni, nj, i, j
        REAL(8) :: skew

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        local_node_max_skewness_any_trial = 0.0D0

        DO j = MAX(1,jnode-1), MIN(nj-1,jnode)
            DO i = MAX(1,inode-1), MIN(ni-1,inode)

                skew = quad_cell_skewness_with_trial_node_any( &
                    b, i, j, inode, jnode, xtrial, ytrial &
                )

                local_node_max_skewness_any_trial = MAX(local_node_max_skewness_any_trial, skew)

            END DO
        END DO

    END FUNCTION local_node_max_skewness_any_trial

    REAL(8) FUNCTION quad_cell_skewness_with_trial_node_any(b, i, j, inode, jnode, xtrial, ytrial)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: i, j
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: inode, jnode
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xtrial, ytrial

        REAL(8) :: x1, y1, x2, y2, x3, y3, x4, y4

        x1 = b%x(i,j)
        y1 = b%y(i,j)
        x2 = b%x(i+1,j)
        y2 = b%y(i+1,j)
        x3 = b%x(i+1,j+1)
        y3 = b%y(i+1,j+1)
        x4 = b%x(i,j+1)
        y4 = b%y(i,j+1)

        IF (i == inode .AND. j == jnode) THEN
            x1 = xtrial
            y1 = ytrial
        END IF

        IF (i+1 == inode .AND. j == jnode) THEN
            x2 = xtrial
            y2 = ytrial
        END IF

        IF (i+1 == inode .AND. j+1 == jnode) THEN
            x3 = xtrial
            y3 = ytrial
        END IF

        IF (i == inode .AND. j+1 == jnode) THEN
            x4 = xtrial
            y4 = ytrial
        END IF

        quad_cell_skewness_with_trial_node_any = quad_cell_equiangular_skewness( &
            x1, y1, x2, y2, x3, y3, x4, y4 &
        )

    END FUNCTION quad_cell_skewness_with_trial_node_any

SUBROUTINE report_worst_cell_skewness(blk, nblock_report, nworst)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nblock_report
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nworst

        INTEGER :: ib, i, j, ni, nj, k, pos, nstored
        REAL(8) :: skew
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: worst_skew(:)
        INTEGER, ALLOCATABLE :: worst_blk(:), worst_i(:), worst_j(:)

        ALLOCATE(worst_skew(nworst))
        ALLOCATE(worst_blk(nworst))
        ALLOCATE(worst_i(nworst))
        ALLOCATE(worst_j(nworst))

        worst_skew = -1.0D0
        worst_blk = 0
        worst_i = 0
        worst_j = 0
        nstored = 0

        DO ib = 1, MIN(nblock_report, SIZE(blk))

            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE

            ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1)
            nj = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,2)

            DO j = 1, nj-1
                DO i = 1, ni-1

                    skew = quad_cell_equiangular_skewness( &
                        blk(ib)%x(i,j),     blk(ib)%y(i,j), &
                        blk(ib)%x(i+1,j),   blk(ib)%y(i+1,j), &
                        blk(ib)%x(i+1,j+1), blk(ib)%y(i+1,j+1), &
                        blk(ib)%x(i,j+1),   blk(ib)%y(i,j+1) )

                    pos = 0

                    IF (nstored < nworst) THEN
                        nstored = nstored + 1
                        pos = nstored
                    ELSE IF (skew > worst_skew(nworst)) THEN
                        pos = nworst
                    END IF

                    IF (pos > 0) THEN

                        worst_skew(pos) = skew
                        worst_blk(pos) = ib
                        worst_i(pos) = i
                        worst_j(pos) = j

                        DO k = pos, 2, -1
                            IF (worst_skew(k) <= worst_skew(k-1)) EXIT
                            CALL swap_real8(worst_skew(k), worst_skew(k-1))
                            CALL swap_integer(worst_blk(k), worst_blk(k-1))
                            CALL swap_integer(worst_i(k), worst_i(k-1))
                            CALL swap_integer(worst_j(k), worst_j(k-1))
                        END DO

                    END IF

                END DO
            END DO

        END DO

        PRINT *, " "
        PRINT *, "Worst cell equiangular skewness values:"
        PRINT *, "  skewness = max((theta_max-90)/90, (90-theta_min)/90)"
        PRINT *, "  0 is ideal orthogonal quad, values near 1 are very poor/degenerate."
        PRINT *, "  rank  block      i      j        skewness"

        DO k = 1, nstored
            WRITE(*,'(I6,3I8,ES18.8)') k, worst_blk(k), worst_i(k), worst_j(k), worst_skew(k)
        END DO

        PRINT *, " "

        DEALLOCATE(worst_skew)
        DEALLOCATE(worst_blk)
        DEALLOCATE(worst_i)
        DEALLOCATE(worst_j)

    END SUBROUTINE report_worst_cell_skewness

    REAL(8) FUNCTION quad_cell_equiangular_skewness(x1, y1, x2, y2, x3, y3, x4, y4)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x1, y1, x2, y2, x3, y3, x4, y4

        REAL(8) :: theta1, theta2, theta3, theta4
        REAL(8) :: theta_min, theta_max
        REAL(8), PARAMETER :: pi = 3.1415926535897932384626433832795D0

        theta1 = angle_between_vectors(x2-x1, y2-y1, x4-x1, y4-y1)
        theta2 = angle_between_vectors(x1-x2, y1-y2, x3-x2, y3-y2)
        theta3 = angle_between_vectors(x2-x3, y2-y3, x4-x3, y4-y3)
        theta4 = angle_between_vectors(x1-x4, y1-y4, x3-x4, y3-y4)

        theta_min = MIN(theta1, theta2, theta3, theta4)
        theta_max = MAX(theta1, theta2, theta3, theta4)

        ! Equiangular skewness for quadrilateral cells.
        ! Uses radians internally.  Ideal angle = pi/2.
        quad_cell_equiangular_skewness = MAX( &
            (theta_max - 0.5D0*pi)/(0.5D0*pi), &
            (0.5D0*pi - theta_min)/(0.5D0*pi) )

        quad_cell_equiangular_skewness = MAX(0.0D0, quad_cell_equiangular_skewness)

    END FUNCTION quad_cell_equiangular_skewness

    REAL(8) FUNCTION angle_between_vectors(ax, ay, bx, by)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: ax, ay, bx, by

        REAL(8) :: amag, bmag, cang

        amag = SQRT(ax*ax + ay*ay)
        bmag = SQRT(bx*bx + by*by)

        IF (amag <= 1.0D-14 .OR. bmag <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            angle_between_vectors = 0.0D0
            RETURN
        END IF

        cang = (ax*bx + ay*by)/(amag*bmag)
        cang = MIN(1.0D0, MAX(-1.0D0, cang))

        angle_between_vectors = ACOS(cang)

    END FUNCTION angle_between_vectors

    SUBROUTINE swap_real8(a, b)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(INOUT) :: a, b
        REAL(8) :: tmp

        tmp = a
        a = b
        b = tmp

    END SUBROUTINE swap_real8

    SUBROUTINE swap_integer(a, b)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(INOUT) :: a, b
        INTEGER :: tmp

        tmp = a
        a = b
        b = tmp

    END SUBROUTINE swap_integer

    LOGICAL FUNCTION is_block9_excluded_c1_pair(ib, jb)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ib
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: jb

        is_block9_excluded_c1_pair = &
            ((ib == 6 .AND. jb == 9) .OR. (ib == 9 .AND. jb == 6) .OR. &
             (ib == 8 .AND. jb == 9) .OR. (ib == 9 .AND. jb == 8))

    END FUNCTION is_block9_excluded_c1_pair

    SUBROUTINE enforce_interface_c1_tolerance(blk, pitch, tol_frac, relax_c1)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: tol_frac
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax_c1

        INTEGER :: ib, side, b2, p2
        INTEGER :: nedge, k, k2
        REAL(8) :: yoff
        LOGICAL :: rev
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xe1(:), ye1(:), xi1(:), yi1(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xe2(:), ye2(:), xi2(:), yi2(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xi1_new(:), yi1_new(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xi2_new(:), yi2_new(:)
        REAL(8) :: t1x, t1y, t2x, t2y
        REAL(8) :: d1mag, d2mag, t1mag, t2mag
        REAL(8) :: angle_deg, max_angle
        REAL(8) :: bisx, bisy, bmag
        REAL(8) :: target1x, target1y, target2x, target2y
        REAL(8) :: new1x, new1y, new2x, new2y
        REAL(8) :: max_angle_before, max_angle_after
        REAL(8) :: old1x, old1y, old2x, old2y
        REAL(8) :: skew_before, skew_after
        REAL(8) :: try_relax
        INTEGER :: retry
        LOGICAL :: accepted
        INTEGER :: nchanged
        INTEGER :: nrejected

        max_angle = c1_angle_max_degrees
        max_angle_before = 0.0D0
        max_angle_after  = 0.0D0
        nchanged = 0
        nrejected = 0

        ! based.  At a block interface, the important skewness driver is
        ! whether the two grid-line families enter the shared edge with a
        DO ib = 1, 9

            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE

            DO side = 1, 4

                b2 = next_block(ib,side)
                p2 = next_patch(ib,side)

                IF (b2 <= 0 .OR. p2 <= 0) CYCLE

                IF (disable_c1_at_block6_block9_interface) THEN
                    IF (is_block9_excluded_c1_pair(ib, b2)) CYCLE
                END IF

                ! Do not smooth both sides of the Block 6--9 or Block 9--8
                ! interfaces. Keep Blocks 6 and 8 fixed; only Block 9 is
                IF (block9_only_safe_interface_smoothing_enabled) THEN
                    IF ((ib == 9 .AND. (b2 == 6 .OR. b2 == 8)) .OR. &
                        (b2 == 9 .AND. (ib == 6 .OR. ib == 8))) CYCLE
                END IF
                IF (b2 < ib) CYCLE
                IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(b2)%x)) CYCLE

                CALL get_edge_line(blk(ib), side, xe1, ye1)
                CALL get_edge_line(blk(b2), p2, xe2, ye2)

                IF (SIZE(xe1) /= SIZE(xe2)) THEN
                    DEALLOCATE(xe1, ye1, xe2, ye2)
                    CYCLE
                END IF

                nedge = SIZE(xe1)

                IF (nedge < 3) THEN
                    DEALLOCATE(xe1, ye1, xe2, ye2)
                    CYCLE
                END IF

                CALL get_interior_line_next_to_patch(blk(ib), side, xi1, yi1)
                CALL get_interior_line_next_to_patch(blk(b2), p2, xi2, yi2)

                ALLOCATE(xi1_new(nedge), yi1_new(nedge))
                ALLOCATE(xi2_new(nedge), yi2_new(nedge))

                xi1_new(:) = xi1(:)
                yi1_new(:) = yi1(:)
                xi2_new(:) = xi2(:)
                yi2_new(:) = yi2(:)

                CALL periodic_match_endpoints( &
                    xe1(1), ye1(1), xe1(nedge), ye1(nedge), &
                    xe2(1), ye2(1), xe2(nedge), ye2(nedge), &
                    pitch, rev, yoff &
                )

                DO k = 2, nedge-1

                    IF (rev) THEN
                        k2 = nedge - k + 1
                    ELSE
                        k2 = k
                    END IF

                    ! Use a five-point reverse spline/fit to estimate the
                    ! derivative at the boundary from inside each block.
                    CALL reverse_spline_boundary_tangent(blk(ib), side, k, t1x, t1y, d1mag)
                    CALL reverse_spline_boundary_tangent(blk(b2), p2, k2, t2x, t2y, d2mag)

                    t1mag = SQRT(t1x*t1x + t1y*t1y)
                    t2mag = SQRT(t2x*t2x + t2y*t2y)

                    IF (t1mag <= 1.0D-14 .OR. t2mag <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE
                    IF (d1mag <= 1.0D-14 .OR. d2mag <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE

                    t1x = t1x/t1mag
                    t1y = t1y/t1mag
                    t2x = t2x/t2mag
                    t2y = t2y/t2mag

                    ! Across a smooth interface, the two reverse tangents
                    ! point towards the same boundary point from opposite
                    angle_deg = vector_angle_degrees(t1x, t1y, -t2x, -t2y)
                    max_angle_before = MAX(max_angle_before, angle_deg)

                    IF (angle_deg <= max_angle) CYCLE

                    ! Common inward normal/tangent direction.  This limits
                    ! each side, so the two blocks can still have different
                    bisx = t1x - t2x
                    bisy = t1y - t2y
                    bmag = SQRT(bisx*bisx + bisy*bisy)

                    IF (bmag <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE

                    bisx = bisx/bmag
                    bisy = bisy/bmag

                    target1x = bisx*d1mag
                    target1y = bisy*d1mag
                    target2x = -bisx*d2mag
                    target2y = -bisy*d2mag

                    old1x = xi1(k)
                    old1y = yi1(k)
                    old2x = xi2(k2)
                    old2y = yi2(k2)

                    skew_before = local_interface_node_max_skewness( &
                        blk(ib), side, k, old1x, old1y &
                    )
                    skew_before = MAX(skew_before, local_interface_node_max_skewness( &
                        blk(b2), p2, k2, old2x, old2y &
                    ))

                    accepted = .FALSE.
                    try_relax = relax_c1

                    DO retry = 1, c1_skewness_retry_count

                        new1x = (1.0D0-try_relax)*(old1x - xe1(k)) + &
                                try_relax*target1x
                        new1y = (1.0D0-try_relax)*(old1y - ye1(k)) + &
                                try_relax*target1y

                        new2x = (1.0D0-try_relax)*(old2x - xe2(k2)) + &
                                try_relax*target2x
                        new2y = (1.0D0-try_relax)*(old2y - ye2(k2)) + &
                                try_relax*target2y

                        skew_after = local_interface_node_max_skewness( &
                            blk(ib), side, k, xe1(k)+new1x, ye1(k)+new1y &
                        )
                        skew_after = MAX(skew_after, local_interface_node_max_skewness( &
                            blk(b2), p2, k2, xe2(k2)+new2x, ye2(k2)+new2y &
                        ))

                        IF (skew_after <= skew_before .OR. skew_after <= c1_skewness_limit) THEN

                            xi1_new(k)  = xe1(k)  + new1x
                            yi1_new(k)  = ye1(k)  + new1y

                            xi2_new(k2) = xe2(k2) + new2x
                            yi2_new(k2) = ye2(k2) + new2y

                            angle_deg = vector_angle_degrees(new1x, new1y, -new2x, -new2y)
                            max_angle_after = MAX(max_angle_after, angle_deg)
                            nchanged = nchanged + 1
                            accepted = .TRUE.
                            EXIT

                        END IF

                        try_relax = 0.5D0*try_relax

                    END DO

                    IF (.NOT. accepted) THEN
                        nrejected = nrejected + 1
                    END IF

                END DO

                CALL set_interior_line_next_to_patch(blk(ib), side, xi1_new, yi1_new)
                CALL set_interior_line_next_to_patch(blk(b2), p2, xi2_new, yi2_new)

                DEALLOCATE(xe1, ye1, xi1, yi1, xe2, ye2, xi2, yi2)
                DEALLOCATE(xi1_new, yi1_new, xi2_new, yi2_new)

            END DO

        END DO

        IF (nchanged > 0 .OR. nrejected > 0) THEN
            PRINT *, "C1 angle/skewness correction: changed, rejected, max angle before/after = ", &
                     nchanged, nrejected, max_angle_before, max_angle_after
        END IF

    END SUBROUTINE enforce_interface_c1_tolerance

    SUBROUTINE compute_global_c1_max_angle(blk, pitch, max_angle_out)

        ! Compute the same global C1 interface angle metric used by the
        ! C1 state and then later oversmooths away from it, we restore the best
        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: max_angle_out

        INTEGER :: ib
        INTEGER :: side
        INTEGER :: b2
        INTEGER :: p2
        INTEGER :: nedge
        INTEGER :: k
        INTEGER :: k2
        REAL(8) :: yoff
        LOGICAL :: rev
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xe1(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: ye1(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xe2(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: ye2(:)
        REAL(8) :: t1x
        REAL(8) :: t1y
        REAL(8) :: t2x
        REAL(8) :: t2y
        REAL(8) :: d1mag
        REAL(8) :: d2mag
        REAL(8) :: t1mag
        REAL(8) :: t2mag
        REAL(8) :: angle_deg

        max_angle_out = 0.0D0

        DO ib = 1, 9

            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE

            DO side = 1, 4

                b2 = next_block(ib,side)
                p2 = next_patch(ib,side)

                IF (b2 <= 0 .OR. p2 <= 0) CYCLE

                IF (disable_c1_at_block6_block9_interface) THEN
                    IF (is_block9_excluded_c1_pair(ib, b2)) CYCLE
                END IF

                ! Match the same interface exclusions used by the correction
                ! routine, so the tracking metric describes the interfaces
                IF (block9_only_safe_interface_smoothing_enabled) THEN
                    IF ((ib == 9 .AND. (b2 == 6 .OR. b2 == 8)) .OR. &
                        (b2 == 9 .AND. (ib == 6 .OR. ib == 8))) CYCLE
                END IF

                IF (b2 < ib) CYCLE
                IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(b2)%x)) CYCLE

                CALL get_edge_line(blk(ib), side, xe1, ye1)
                CALL get_edge_line(blk(b2), p2, xe2, ye2)

                IF (SIZE(xe1) /= SIZE(xe2)) THEN
                    DEALLOCATE(xe1, ye1, xe2, ye2)
                    CYCLE
                END IF

                nedge = SIZE(xe1)

                IF (nedge < 3) THEN
                    DEALLOCATE(xe1, ye1, xe2, ye2)
                    CYCLE
                END IF

                CALL periodic_match_endpoints( &
                    xe1(1), ye1(1), xe1(nedge), ye1(nedge), &
                    xe2(1), ye2(1), xe2(nedge), ye2(nedge), &
                    pitch, rev, yoff &
                )

                DO k = 2, nedge-1

                    IF (rev) THEN
                        k2 = nedge - k + 1
                    ELSE
                        k2 = k
