                    END IF

                    CALL reverse_spline_boundary_tangent(blk(ib), side, k, t1x, t1y, d1mag)
                    CALL reverse_spline_boundary_tangent(blk(b2), p2, k2, t2x, t2y, d2mag)

                    t1mag = SQRT(t1x*t1x + t1y*t1y)
                    t2mag = SQRT(t2x*t2x + t2y*t2y)

                    IF (t1mag <= 1.0D-14 .OR. t2mag <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE
                    IF (d1mag <= 1.0D-14 .OR. d2mag <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE

                    t1x = t1x/t1mag
                    t1y = t1y/t1mag
                    t2x = t2x/t2mag
                    t2y = t2y/t2mag

                    angle_deg = vector_angle_degrees(t1x, t1y, -t2x, -t2y)
                    max_angle_out = MAX(max_angle_out, angle_deg)

                END DO

                DEALLOCATE(xe1, ye1, xe2, ye2)

            END DO

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE compute_global_c1_max_angle

        SUBROUTINE reverse_spline_boundary_tangent(b, side, kedge, tx, ty, dmag)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: kedge
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: tx, ty, dmag

        INTEGER :: nfit, p, ni, nj
        REAL(8) :: x0, y0
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: qx(:), qy(:), s(:)

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        nfit = c1_reverse_spline_nfit

        tx = 0.0D0
        ty = 0.0D0
        dmag = 0.0D0

        IF (nfit < 2) RETURN

        SELECT CASE (side)
        CASE (1)
            IF (ni < nfit + 1) RETURN
        CASE (2)
            IF (ni < nfit + 1) RETURN
        CASE (3)
            IF (nj < nfit + 1) RETURN
        CASE (4)
            IF (nj < nfit + 1) RETURN
        END SELECT

        ALLOCATE(qx(0:nfit), qy(0:nfit), s(0:nfit))

        CALL get_patch_node(b, side, kedge, 0, x0, y0)
        qx(0) = x0
        qy(0) = y0
        s(0) = 0.0D0

        DO p = 1, nfit
            CALL get_patch_node(b, side, kedge, p, qx(p), qy(p))
            s(p) = s(p-1) + SQRT((qx(p)-qx(p-1))**2 + (qy(p)-qy(p-1))**2)
        END DO

        IF (s(nfit) <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            DEALLOCATE(qx, qy, s)
            RETURN
        END IF

        ! Reverse-spline interpretation:
        ! q(0) is the boundary point, q(1..5) are the five points leading
        CALL cubic_fit_boundary_derivative(s, qx, nfit, tx)
        CALL cubic_fit_boundary_derivative(s, qy, nfit, ty)

        dmag = SQRT((qx(1)-qx(0))**2 + (qy(1)-qy(0))**2)

        IF (SQRT(tx*tx + ty*ty) <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            tx = qx(1) - qx(0)
            ty = qy(1) - qy(0)
        END IF

        DEALLOCATE(qx, qy, s)

    END SUBROUTINE reverse_spline_boundary_tangent

    SUBROUTINE get_patch_node(b, side, kedge, offset, xnode, ynode)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: kedge
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: offset
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: xnode, ynode

        INTEGER :: ni, nj
        INTEGER :: i, j

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        SELECT CASE (side)
        CASE (1)
            i = 1 + offset
            j = kedge
        CASE (2)
            i = ni - offset
            j = kedge
        CASE (3)
            i = kedge
            j = 1 + offset
        CASE (4)
            i = kedge
            j = nj - offset
        CASE DEFAULT
            PRINT *, "Bad side in get_patch_node: ", side
            STOP
        END SELECT

        i = MAX(1, MIN(ni, i))
        j = MAX(1, MIN(nj, j))

        xnode = b%x(i,j)
        ynode = b%y(i,j)

    END SUBROUTINE get_patch_node

    SUBROUTINE cubic_fit_boundary_derivative(s, q, nfit, dqds0)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nfit
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: s(0:nfit)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: q(0:nfit)
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: dqds0

        REAL(8) :: a(4,4), rhs(4), coeff(4)
        REAL(8) :: phi(4)
        INTEGER :: p, r, c

        a = 0.0D0
        rhs = 0.0D0

        DO p = 0, nfit
            phi(1) = 1.0D0
            phi(2) = s(p)
            phi(3) = s(p)*s(p)
            phi(4) = s(p)*s(p)*s(p)

            DO r = 1, 4
                rhs(r) = rhs(r) + phi(r)*q(p)
                DO c = 1, 4
                    a(r,c) = a(r,c) + phi(r)*phi(c)
                END DO
            END DO
        END DO

        CALL solve_4x4_linear_system(a, rhs, coeff)

        ! q(s) = coeff(1) + coeff(2)*s + coeff(3)*s^2 + coeff(4)*s^3
        ! derivative at the boundary s=0 is coeff(2).
        dqds0 = coeff(2)

    END SUBROUTINE cubic_fit_boundary_derivative

    SUBROUTINE solve_4x4_linear_system(a, b, x)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(INOUT) :: a(4,4)
        REAL(8), INTENT(INOUT) :: b(4)
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: x(4)

        INTEGER :: i, j, k, ipiv
        REAL(8) :: piv, fac, tmp
        REAL(8) :: rowtmp(4)

        DO k = 1, 4

            ipiv = k
            piv = ABS(a(k,k))

            DO i = k+1, 4
                IF (ABS(a(i,k)) > piv) THEN
                    piv = ABS(a(i,k))
                    ipiv = i
                END IF
            END DO

            IF (piv <= 1.0D-30) THEN
                x = 0.0D0
                RETURN
            END IF

            IF (ipiv /= k) THEN
                rowtmp(:) = a(k,:)
                a(k,:) = a(ipiv,:)
                a(ipiv,:) = rowtmp(:)

                tmp = b(k)
                b(k) = b(ipiv)
                b(ipiv) = tmp
            END IF

            DO i = k+1, 4
                fac = a(i,k)/a(k,k)
                a(i,k) = 0.0D0

                DO j = k+1, 4
                    a(i,j) = a(i,j) - fac*a(k,j)
                END DO

                b(i) = b(i) - fac*b(k)

            END DO

        END DO

        DO i = 4, 1, -1

            tmp = b(i)

            DO j = i+1, 4
                tmp = tmp - a(i,j)*x(j)
            END DO

            x(i) = tmp/a(i,i)

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE solve_4x4_linear_system

    REAL(8) FUNCTION vector_angle_degrees(ax, ay, bx, by)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: ax, ay, bx, by

        REAL(8) :: amag, bmag, cang
        REAL(8), PARAMETER :: pi = 3.1415926535897932384626433832795D0

        amag = SQRT(ax*ax + ay*ay)
        bmag = SQRT(bx*bx + by*by)

        IF (amag <= 1.0D-14 .OR. bmag <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            vector_angle_degrees = 0.0D0
            RETURN
        END IF

        cang = (ax*bx + ay*by)/(amag*bmag)
        cang = MIN(1.0D0, MAX(-1.0D0, cang))

        vector_angle_degrees = ACOS(cang)*180.0D0/pi

    END FUNCTION vector_angle_degrees

    REAL(8) FUNCTION local_interface_node_max_skewness(b, side, kedge, xnew, ynew)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: kedge
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xnew, ynew

        INTEGER :: ni, nj, i, j
        INTEGER :: inode, jnode
        REAL(8) :: skew

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        local_interface_node_max_skewness = -1.0D0

        CALL first_interior_node_indices_for_patch(b, side, kedge, inode, jnode)

        DO j = MAX(1,jnode-1), MIN(nj-1,jnode)
            DO i = MAX(1,inode-1), MIN(ni-1,inode)

                skew = quad_cell_skewness_with_trial_node( &
                    b, i, j, inode, jnode, xnew, ynew &
                )

                local_interface_node_max_skewness = MAX(local_interface_node_max_skewness, skew)

            END DO
        END DO

        IF (local_interface_node_max_skewness < 0.0D0) THEN
            local_interface_node_max_skewness = 0.0D0
        END IF

    END FUNCTION local_interface_node_max_skewness

    SUBROUTINE first_interior_node_indices_for_patch(b, side, kedge, inode, jnode)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: kedge
        INTEGER, INTENT(OUT) :: inode, jnode

        INTEGER :: ni, nj

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        SELECT CASE (side)
        CASE (1)
            inode = MIN(2,ni)
            jnode = kedge
        CASE (2)
            inode = MAX(ni-1,1)
            jnode = kedge
        CASE (3)
            inode = kedge
            jnode = MIN(2,nj)
        CASE (4)
            inode = kedge
            jnode = MAX(nj-1,1)
        CASE DEFAULT
            PRINT *, "Bad side in first_interior_node_indices_for_patch: ", side
            STOP
        END SELECT

    END SUBROUTINE first_interior_node_indices_for_patch

    REAL(8) FUNCTION quad_cell_skewness_with_trial_node(b, i, j, inode, jnode, xnew, ynew)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: i, j
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: inode, jnode
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xnew, ynew

        REAL(8) :: x1, y1, x2, y2, x3, y3, x4, y4

        x1 = b%x(i,j)
        y1 = b%y(i,j)
        x2 = b%x(i+1,j)
        y2 = b%y(i+1,j)
        x3 = b%x(i+1,j+1)
        y3 = b%y(i+1,j+1)
        x4 = b%x(i,j+1)
        y4 = b%y(i,j+1)

        IF (i == inode .AND. j == jnode) THEN
            x1 = xnew
            y1 = ynew
        END IF

        IF (i+1 == inode .AND. j == jnode) THEN
            x2 = xnew
            y2 = ynew
        END IF

        IF (i+1 == inode .AND. j+1 == jnode) THEN
            x3 = xnew
            y3 = ynew
        END IF

        IF (i == inode .AND. j+1 == jnode) THEN
            x4 = xnew
            y4 = ynew
        END IF

        quad_cell_skewness_with_trial_node = quad_cell_equiangular_skewness( &
            x1, y1, x2, y2, x3, y3, x4, y4 &
        )

    END FUNCTION quad_cell_skewness_with_trial_node

SUBROUTINE set_interior_line_next_to_patch(b, side, xline, yline)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xline(:), yline(:)

        INTEGER :: ni, nj, n, k
        INTEGER :: ii, jj

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)
        n = SIZE(xline)

        SELECT CASE (side)
        CASE (1)
            ii = MIN(2,ni)
            DO k = 1, MIN(n,nj)
                b%x(ii,k) = xline(k)
                b%y(ii,k) = yline(k)
            END DO
        CASE (2)
            ii = MAX(ni-1,1)
            DO k = 1, MIN(n,nj)
                b%x(ii,k) = xline(k)
                b%y(ii,k) = yline(k)
            END DO
        CASE (3)
            jj = MIN(2,nj)
            DO k = 1, MIN(n,ni)
                b%x(k,jj) = xline(k)
                b%y(k,jj) = yline(k)
            END DO
        CASE (4)
            jj = MAX(nj-1,1)
            DO k = 1, MIN(n,ni)
                b%x(k,jj) = xline(k)
                b%y(k,jj) = yline(k)
            END DO
        CASE DEFAULT
            PRINT *, "Bad side in set_interior_line_next_to_patch: ", side
            STOP
        END SELECT

    END SUBROUTINE set_interior_line_next_to_patch

    SUBROUTINE save_initial_blade_wall_points(blk, fixed_wall)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: fixed_wall(4)

        INTEGER :: ib, iw, ni

        DO iw = 1, 4
            IF (ALLOCATED(fixed_wall(iw)%x)) DEALLOCATE(fixed_wall(iw)%x)
            IF (ALLOCATED(fixed_wall(iw)%y)) DEALLOCATE(fixed_wall(iw)%y)
        END DO

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 8) RETURN

        DO ib = 5, 8

            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE

            iw = ib - 4
            ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1)

            ALLOCATE(fixed_wall(iw)%x(ni,1))
            ALLOCATE(fixed_wall(iw)%y(ni,1))

            fixed_wall(iw)%x(:,1) = blk(ib)%x(:,1)
            fixed_wall(iw)%y(:,1) = blk(ib)%y(:,1)

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE save_initial_blade_wall_points

    SUBROUTINE restore_initial_blade_wall_points(blk, fixed_wall)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: fixed_wall(4)

        INTEGER :: ib, iw, ni

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 8) RETURN

        DO ib = 5, 8

            iw = ib - 4

            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE
            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(fixed_wall(iw)%x)) CYCLE

            ni = MIN(SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1), SIZE(fixed_wall(iw)%x,1))

            blk(ib)%x(1:ni,1) = fixed_wall(iw)%x(1:ni,1)
            blk(ib)%y(1:ni,1) = fixed_wall(iw)%y(1:ni,1)

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE restore_initial_blade_wall_points

    SUBROUTINE free_blade_wall_storage(fixed_wall)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: fixed_wall(4)

        INTEGER :: iw

        DO iw = 1, 4
            IF (ALLOCATED(fixed_wall(iw)%x)) DEALLOCATE(fixed_wall(iw)%x)
            IF (ALLOCATED(fixed_wall(iw)%y)) DEALLOCATE(fixed_wall(iw)%y)
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE free_blade_wall_storage

    SUBROUTINE refine_farfield_vertical_lines(blk, refine_factor)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: refine_factor

        INTEGER :: ib

        IF (refine_factor <= 1) RETURN
        IF (SIZE(blk) < 4) RETURN

        DO ib = 1, 4
            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE
            CALL refine_block_i_direction(blk(ib), refine_factor)
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE refine_farfield_vertical_lines

    SUBROUTINE refine_block_i_direction(b, refine_factor)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: refine_factor

        INTEGER :: ni_old, nj_old, ni_new
        INTEGER :: i_new, j
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xold(:,:), yold(:,:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xnew(:,:), ynew(:,:)
        REAL(8) :: xi_old
        REAL(8) :: t
        INTEGER :: i0

        IF (refine_factor <= 1) RETURN

        ni_old = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj_old = SIZE(b%x,2)

        IF (ni_old < 2 .OR. nj_old < 1) RETURN

        ni_new = (ni_old - 1)*refine_factor + 1

        ALLOCATE(xold(ni_old,nj_old), yold(ni_old,nj_old))
        ALLOCATE(xnew(ni_new,nj_old), ynew(ni_new,nj_old))

        xold(:,:) = b%x(:,:)
        yold(:,:) = b%y(:,:)

        DO j = 1, nj_old

            DO i_new = 1, ni_new

                xi_old = 1.0D0 + REAL(i_new-1,8)/REAL(refine_factor,8)

                i0 = INT(FLOOR(xi_old))
                IF (i0 < 1) i0 = 1
                IF (i0 > ni_old-1) i0 = ni_old-1

                t = xi_old - REAL(i0,8)

                xnew(i_new,j) = (1.0D0-t)*xold(i0,j) + t*xold(i0+1,j)
                ynew(i_new,j) = (1.0D0-t)*yold(i0,j) + t*yold(i0+1,j)

            END DO

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(b%x)
        DEALLOCATE(b%y)

        ALLOCATE(b%x(ni_new,nj_old))
        ALLOCATE(b%y(ni_new,nj_old))

        b%x(:,:) = xnew(:,:)
        b%y(:,:) = ynew(:,:)

        DEALLOCATE(xold, yold, xnew, ynew)

    END SUBROUTINE refine_block_i_direction

    SUBROUTINE smooth_blade_tangential_lines_once(blk, nlines, relax_smooth)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nlines
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax_smooth

        INTEGER :: ib, ni, nj, j, i
        INTEGER :: jmax
        INTEGER :: nchanged
        LOGICAL :: changed

        ! Smooths constant-j tangential lines around the blade in blocks
        !   j = 1       blade wall line
        IF (SIZE(blk) < 8) RETURN

        nchanged = 0

        DO ib = 5, 8

            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE

            ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1)
            nj = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,2)

            IF (ni < 5 .OR. nj < 4) CYCLE

            jmax = MIN(nj-1, 1 + nlines)

            ! Start at j=2 to keep the blade wall itself fixed.
            DO j = 2, jmax

                ! Do not move i=1 or i=ni, because those are block
                ! interface/radial edge lines.
                DO i = 2, ni-1

                    CALL try_tangential_line_node_move( &
                        blk(ib), i, j, relax_smooth, changed &
                    )

                    IF (changed) nchanged = nchanged + 1

                END DO

            END DO

        END DO

        PRINT *, "Blade tangential line smoother node moves = ", nchanged

    END SUBROUTINE smooth_blade_tangential_lines_once

    SUBROUTINE try_tangential_line_node_move(b, inode, jnode, relax_smooth, changed)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: inode, jnode
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax_smooth
        LOGICAL, INTENT(OUT) :: changed

        INTEGER :: ni, nj, retry
        REAL(8) :: xold, yold
        REAL(8) :: xtan, ytan
        REAL(8) :: xtry, ytry
        REAL(8) :: skew_before, skew_after
        REAL(8) :: area_before, area_after
        REAL(8) :: fac

        changed = .FALSE.

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        IF (inode <= 1 .OR. inode >= ni) RETURN
        IF (jnode <= 1 .OR. jnode >= nj) RETURN

        xold = b%x(inode,jnode)
        yold = b%y(inode,jnode)

        ! Tangential smoothing target: only average along the same
        ! toward/away from the blade wall.
        xtan = 0.5D0*(b%x(inode-1,jnode) + b%x(inode+1,jnode))
        ytan = 0.5D0*(b%y(inode-1,jnode) + b%y(inode+1,jnode))

        skew_before = local_node_max_skewness_any(b, inode, jnode)
        area_before = local_node_min_signed_area_trial(b, inode, jnode, xold, yold)

        fac = relax_smooth

        DO retry = 1, 6

            xtry = xold + fac*(xtan - xold)
            ytry = yold + fac*(ytan - yold)

            skew_after = local_node_max_skewness_any_trial(b, inode, jnode, xtry, ytry)
            area_after = local_node_min_signed_area_trial(b, inode, jnode, xtry, ytry)

            ! Accept only true local improvement, and never accept a
            ! move that folds neighbouring cells.
            IF (skew_after < skew_before .AND. area_after > 0.0D0) THEN
                b%x(inode,jnode) = xtry
                b%y(inode,jnode) = ytry
                changed = .TRUE.
                RETURN
            END IF

            ! If the local region was already acceptable, permit a tiny
            ! smoothing move only if it remains safely acceptable.
            IF (skew_before <= 0.40D0 .AND. skew_after <= 0.40D0 .AND. &
                area_after >= 0.90D0*area_before) THEN
                b%x(inode,jnode) = xtry
                b%y(inode,jnode) = ytry
                changed = .TRUE.
                RETURN
            END IF

            fac = 0.5D0*fac

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE try_tangential_line_node_move

    SUBROUTINE radialise_block6_interior_fixed_edges(blk)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)

        INTEGER :: ni
        INTEGER :: nj
        INTEGER :: i
        INTEGER :: j
        INTEGER :: im
        INTEGER :: ip

        REAL(8) :: xw
        REAL(8) :: yw
        REAL(8) :: xtop
        REAL(8) :: ytop

        REAL(8) :: tx
        REAL(8) :: ty
        REAL(8) :: tmag

        REAL(8) :: nxv
        REAL(8) :: nyv

        REAL(8) :: avx
        REAL(8) :: avy
        REAL(8) :: dnormal
        REAL(8) :: tangx
        REAL(8) :: tangy

        REAL(8) :: old_len_total
        REAL(8) :: cumlen
        REAL(8) :: seglen
        REAL(8) :: eta
        REAL(8) :: tang_blend
        REAL(8) :: p

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xold(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: yold(:)

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 6) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(6)%x)) RETURN

        ni = SIZE(blk(6)%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(blk(6)%x,2)

        IF (ni < 4 .OR. nj < 4) RETURN

        ALLOCATE(xold(nj))
        ALLOCATE(yold(nj))

        p = MAX(1.0D0, block6_fixed_edge_tangent_recovery_power)

        ! Do not touch the side edges i=1 and i=ni.
        DO i = 2, ni-1

            DO j = 1, nj
                xold(j) = blk(6)%x(i,j)
                yold(j) = blk(6)%y(i,j)
            END DO

            xw = xold(1)
            yw = yold(1)
            xtop = xold(nj)
            ytop = yold(nj)

            ! Smooth blade-wall tangent.
            im = i - 1
            ip = i + 1

            tx = blk(6)%x(ip,1) - blk(6)%x(im,1)
            ty = blk(6)%y(ip,1) - blk(6)%y(im,1)

            tmag = SQRT(tx*tx + ty*ty)

            IF (tmag <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE

            tx = tx/tmag
            ty = ty/tmag

            ! Candidate normal.
            nxv = -ty
            nyv =  tx

            avx = xtop - xw
            avy = ytop - yw

            ! Point normal toward the fixed top edge.
            IF (avx*nxv + avy*nyv < 0.0D0) THEN
                nxv = -nxv
                nyv = -nyv
            END IF

            dnormal = avx*nxv + avy*nyv

            IF (dnormal <= 1.0D-14) THEN
                ! If projection is bad, keep this line unchanged.
                CYCLE
            END IF

            tangx = avx - dnormal*nxv
            tangy = avy - dnormal*nyv

            old_len_total = 0.0D0

            DO j = 2, nj
                old_len_total = old_len_total + SQRT( &
                    (xold(j)-xold(j-1))**2 + &
                    (yold(j)-yold(j-1))**2 )
            END DO

            IF (old_len_total <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE

            ! Keep wall and top fixed.
            blk(6)%x(i,1) = xold(1)
            blk(6)%y(i,1) = yold(1)
            blk(6)%x(i,nj) = xold(nj)
            blk(6)%y(i,nj) = yold(nj)

            cumlen = 0.0D0

            DO j = 2, nj-1

                seglen = SQRT( &
                    (xold(j)-xold(j-1))**2 + &
                    (yold(j)-yold(j-1))**2 )

                cumlen = cumlen + seglen
                eta = cumlen/old_len_total
                eta = MAX(0.0D0, MIN(1.0D0, eta))

                ! Delayed tangential recovery.  Since p > 1, derivative of
                ! this term is zero at the blade wall.
                tang_blend = eta**p

                blk(6)%x(i,j) = xw + eta*dnormal*nxv + tang_blend*tangx
                blk(6)%y(i,j) = yw + eta*dnormal*nyv + tang_blend*tangy

            END DO

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(xold)
        DEALLOCATE(yold)

        PRINT *, "Block 6 interior radialised with all four Block 6 edges fixed."
        PRINT *, "  Block 6--9 discontinuity allowed."
        PRINT *, "  tangent recovery power = ", p

    END SUBROUTINE radialise_block6_interior_fixed_edges

    SUBROUTINE straighten_block6_middle_radial_lines(blk)

    ! Block 6 line straightening.
        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)

        INTEGER :: ni
        INTEGER :: nj
        INTEGER :: i
        INTEGER :: j
        INTEGER :: i_start
        INTEGER :: i_end

        REAL(8) :: xw
        REAL(8) :: yw
        REAL(8) :: xo
        REAL(8) :: yo
        REAL(8) :: dx
        REAL(8) :: dy

        REAL(8) :: old_len_total
        REAL(8) :: cumlen
        REAL(8) :: seglen
        REAL(8) :: eta
        REAL(8) :: relax

        REAL(8) :: xline
        REAL(8) :: yline

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xold(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: yold(:)

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 6) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(6)%x)) RETURN

        ni = SIZE(blk(6)%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(blk(6)%x,2)

        IF (ni < 4 .OR. nj < 4) RETURN

        i_start = 1 + block6_middle_radial_protect_i_lines
        i_end   = ni - block6_middle_radial_protect_i_lines

        IF (i_start >= i_end) RETURN

        relax = MAX(0.0D0, MIN(1.0D0, block6_middle_radial_straighten_relax))

        ALLOCATE(xold(nj))
        ALLOCATE(yold(nj))

        DO i = i_start, i_end

            DO j = 1, nj
                xold(j) = blk(6)%x(i,j)
                yold(j) = blk(6)%y(i,j)
            END DO

            xw = xold(1)
            yw = yold(1)
            xo = xold(nj)
            yo = yold(nj)

            dx = xo - xw
            dy = yo - yw

            old_len_total = 0.0D0

            DO j = 2, nj
                old_len_total = old_len_total + SQRT( &
                    (xold(j)-xold(j-1))**2 + &
                    (yold(j)-yold(j-1))**2 )
            END DO

            IF (old_len_total <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE

            ! Keep endpoints fixed.
            blk(6)%x(i,1) = xold(1)
            blk(6)%y(i,1) = yold(1)
            blk(6)%x(i,nj) = xold(nj)
            blk(6)%y(i,nj) = yold(nj)

            cumlen = 0.0D0

            DO j = 2, nj-1

                seglen = SQRT( &
                    (xold(j)-xold(j-1))**2 + &
                    (yold(j)-yold(j-1))**2 )

                cumlen = cumlen + seglen

                eta = cumlen/old_len_total
                eta = MAX(0.0D0, MIN(1.0D0, eta))

                xline = xw + eta*dx
                yline = yw + eta*dy

                blk(6)%x(i,j) = (1.0D0-relax)*xold(j) + relax*xline
                blk(6)%y(i,j) = (1.0D0-relax)*yold(j) + relax*yline

            END DO

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(xold)
        DEALLOCATE(yold)

        PRINT *, "Block 6 middle radial lines straightened."
        PRINT *, "  protected side i-lines = ", block6_middle_radial_protect_i_lines
        PRINT *, "  straighten relax = ", relax

    END SUBROUTINE straighten_block6_middle_radial_lines

    SUBROUTINE straighten_block8_middle_radial_lines(blk)

    ! Block 8 line straightening.
        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)

        INTEGER :: ni
        INTEGER :: nj
        INTEGER :: i
        INTEGER :: j
        INTEGER :: i_start
        INTEGER :: i_end

        REAL(8) :: xw
        REAL(8) :: yw
        REAL(8) :: xo
        REAL(8) :: yo
        REAL(8) :: dx
        REAL(8) :: dy

        REAL(8) :: old_len_total
        REAL(8) :: cumlen
        REAL(8) :: seglen
        REAL(8) :: eta
        REAL(8) :: relax

        REAL(8) :: xline
        REAL(8) :: yline

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xold(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: yold(:)

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 8) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(8)%x)) RETURN

        ni = SIZE(blk(8)%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(blk(8)%x,2)

        IF (ni < 4 .OR. nj < 4) RETURN

        i_start = 1 + block8_middle_radial_protect_i_lines
        i_end   = ni - block8_middle_radial_protect_i_lines

        IF (i_start >= i_end) RETURN

        relax = MAX(0.0D0, MIN(1.0D0, block8_middle_radial_straighten_relax))

        ALLOCATE(xold(nj))
        ALLOCATE(yold(nj))

        DO i = i_start, i_end

            DO j = 1, nj
                xold(j) = blk(8)%x(i,j)
                yold(j) = blk(8)%y(i,j)
            END DO

            xw = xold(1)
            yw = yold(1)
            xo = xold(nj)
            yo = yold(nj)

            dx = xo - xw
            dy = yo - yw

            old_len_total = 0.0D0

            DO j = 2, nj
                old_len_total = old_len_total + SQRT( &
                    (xold(j)-xold(j-1))**2 + &
                    (yold(j)-yold(j-1))**2 )
            END DO

            IF (old_len_total <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE

            blk(8)%x(i,1) = xold(1)
            blk(8)%y(i,1) = yold(1)
            blk(8)%x(i,nj) = xold(nj)
            blk(8)%y(i,nj) = yold(nj)

            cumlen = 0.0D0

            DO j = 2, nj-1

                seglen = SQRT( &
                    (xold(j)-xold(j-1))**2 + &
                    (yold(j)-yold(j-1))**2 )

                cumlen = cumlen + seglen

                eta = cumlen/old_len_total
                eta = MAX(0.0D0, MIN(1.0D0, eta))

                xline = xw + eta*dx
                yline = yw + eta*dy

                blk(8)%x(i,j) = (1.0D0-relax)*xold(j) + relax*xline
                blk(8)%y(i,j) = (1.0D0-relax)*yold(j) + relax*yline

            END DO

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(xold)
        DEALLOCATE(yold)

        PRINT *, "Block 8 middle radial lines straightened."
        PRINT *, "  protected side i-lines = ", block8_middle_radial_protect_i_lines
        PRINT *, "  straighten relax = ", relax

    END SUBROUTINE straighten_block8_middle_radial_lines

    SUBROUTINE smooth_block9_global_fixed_edges_after_68(blk, pitch)

        ! Smooth the entire interior of Block 9 after Blocks 6 and 8 have
        ! This is global smoothing:
        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch

        INTEGER :: ni
        INTEGER :: nj
        INTEGER :: i
        INTEGER :: j
        INTEGER :: pass
        INTEGER :: changed
        INTEGER :: total_changed

        REAL(8) :: relax
        REAL(8) :: cross_w
        REAL(8) :: denom
        REAL(8) :: xtarget
        REAL(8) :: ytarget
        REAL(8) :: xtry
        REAL(8) :: ytry
        REAL(8) :: area_after

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 9) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(9)%x)) RETURN

        ni = SIZE(blk(9)%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(blk(9)%x,2)

        IF (ni < 4 .OR. nj < 4) RETURN

        relax = MAX(0.0D0, MIN(1.0D0, block9_global_smooth_after_68_relax))
        cross_w = MAX(0.10D0, block9_global_smooth_cross_weight)

        ! Keep edges geometrically conformed before the final global smoother.
        CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, 9)

        total_changed = 0

        DO pass = 1, block9_global_smooth_after_68_passes

            changed = 0

            DO j = 2, nj-1
                DO i = 2, ni-1

                    ! Block 9, not just near the 6--9 or 8--9 interfaces.
                    ! The j-direction is usually the cross-interface direction
                    denom = 2.0D0 + 2.0D0*cross_w

                    xtarget = (blk(9)%x(i-1,j) + blk(9)%x(i+1,j) + &
                               cross_w*blk(9)%x(i,j-1) + &
                               cross_w*blk(9)%x(i,j+1)) / denom

                    ytarget = (blk(9)%y(i-1,j) + blk(9)%y(i+1,j) + &
                               cross_w*blk(9)%y(i,j-1) + &
                               cross_w*blk(9)%y(i,j+1)) / denom

                    xtry = (1.0D0-relax)*blk(9)%x(i,j) + relax*xtarget
                    ytry = (1.0D0-relax)*blk(9)%y(i,j) + relax*ytarget

                    area_after = local_node_min_signed_area_trial(blk(9), i, j, xtry, ytry)

                    IF (area_after > 0.0D0) THEN
                        blk(9)%x(i,j) = xtry
                        blk(9)%y(i,j) = ytry
                        changed = changed + 1
                    END IF

                END DO
            END DO

            total_changed = total_changed + changed

            ! Re-lock only the edges.  The interior remains free to smooth.
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, 9)

            IF (changed == 0) EXIT

        END DO

        PRINT *, "Block 9 final global smoother: passes requested = ", &
                 block9_global_smooth_after_68_passes
        PRINT *, "Block 9 final global smoother: total accepted moves = ", total_changed
        PRINT *, "Block 9 final global smoother: relax, cross weight = ", relax, cross_w

    END SUBROUTINE smooth_block9_global_fixed_edges_after_68

    SUBROUTINE blend_block9_lines_between_block6_block8_radials(blk, pitch)

        ! Blend whole Block 9 cross-lines smoothly between the straightened
        ! radial-line directions of Blocks 6 and 8.
        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch

        INTEGER :: side9_to_6
        INTEGER :: side9_to_8

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 9) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(9)%x)) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(6)%x)) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(8)%x)) RETURN

        CALL find_block9_side_closest_to_block_edge_simple(blk, pitch, 6, 4, side9_to_6)
        CALL find_block9_side_closest_to_block_edge_simple(blk, pitch, 8, 4, side9_to_8)

        PRINT *, "Whole-line Block 9 blend: side to Block 6 = ", side9_to_6
        PRINT *, "Whole-line Block 9 blend: side to Block 8 = ", side9_to_8

        IF (side9_to_6 <= 0 .OR. side9_to_8 <= 0) THEN
            PRINT *, "WARNING: Block 9 whole-line blend skipped; side detection failed."
            RETURN
        END IF

        IF (.NOT. are_opposite_sides(side9_to_6, side9_to_8)) THEN
            PRINT *, "WARNING: Block 9 whole-line blend skipped; detected sides are not opposite."
            RETURN
        END IF

        IF ((side9_to_6 == 3 .AND. side9_to_8 == 4) .OR. &
            (side9_to_6 == 4 .AND. side9_to_8 == 3)) THEN

            CALL blend_block9_j_lines_between_6_8(blk, pitch, side9_to_6, side9_to_8)

        ELSE IF ((side9_to_6 == 1 .AND. side9_to_8 == 2) .OR. &
                 (side9_to_6 == 2 .AND. side9_to_8 == 1)) THEN

            CALL blend_block9_i_lines_between_6_8(blk, pitch, side9_to_6, side9_to_8)

        ELSE
            PRINT *, "WARNING: unexpected opposite side combination in Block 9 whole-line blend."
        END IF

    END SUBROUTINE blend_block9_lines_between_block6_block8_radials

    LOGICAL FUNCTION are_opposite_sides(side_a, side_b)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side_a
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side_b

        are_opposite_sides = &
            ((side_a == 1 .AND. side_b == 2) .OR. &
             (side_a == 2 .AND. side_b == 1) .OR. &
             (side_a == 3 .AND. side_b == 4) .OR. &
             (side_a == 4 .AND. side_b == 3))

    END FUNCTION are_opposite_sides

    SUBROUTINE blend_block9_j_lines_between_6_8(blk, pitch, side6, side8)

        ! Handles the case where Block 9 connects Blocks 6 and 8 across j.
        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side6
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side8

        INTEGER :: ni9, nj9
        INTEGER :: i, j
        INTEGER :: k6, k8
        INTEGER :: im6, im8
        INTEGER :: kedge
        LOGICAL :: rev6, rev8
        REAL(8) :: yoff6, yoff8
        REAL(8) :: p0x, p0y, p1x, p1y
        REAL(8) :: t0x, t0y, t1x, t1y
        REAL(8) :: rx, ry, rmag
        REAL(8) :: chord_len, scale
        REAL(8) :: s, h00, h10, h01, h11
        REAL(8) :: xh, yh
        REAL(8) :: relax
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x6(:), y6(:), x8(:), y8(:), x9a(:), y9a(:), x9b(:), y9b(:)

        ni9 = SIZE(blk(9)%x,1)
        nj9 = SIZE(blk(9)%x,2)

        CALL get_edge_line(blk(6), 4, x6, y6)
        CALL get_edge_line(blk(8), 4, x8, y8)
        CALL get_edge_line(blk(9), side6, x9a, y9a)
        CALL get_edge_line(blk(9), side8, x9b, y9b)

        IF (SIZE(x6) /= SIZE(x9a) .OR. SIZE(x8) /= SIZE(x9b)) THEN
            PRINT *, "WARNING: Block 9 j-line blend skipped; edge sizes do not match."
            DEALLOCATE(x6,y6,x8,y8,x9a,y9a,x9b,y9b)
            RETURN
        END IF

        CALL periodic_match_endpoints(x6(1), y6(1), x6(SIZE(x6)), y6(SIZE(y6)), &
                                      x9a(1), y9a(1), x9a(SIZE(x9a)), y9a(SIZE(y9a)), &
                                      pitch, rev6, yoff6)

        CALL periodic_match_endpoints(x8(1), y8(1), x8(SIZE(x8)), y8(SIZE(y8)), &
                                      x9b(1), y9b(1), x9b(SIZE(x9b)), y9b(SIZE(y9b)), &
                                      pitch, rev8, yoff8)

        relax = MAX(0.0D0, MIN(1.0D0, block9_whole_line_blend_68_relax))

        DO i = 2, ni9-1

            ! edge index on each detected side
            kedge = i

            IF (rev6) THEN
                k6 = SIZE(x6) - kedge + 1
            ELSE
                k6 = kedge
            END IF

            IF (rev8) THEN
                k8 = SIZE(x8) - kedge + 1
            ELSE
                k8 = kedge
            END IF

            IF (k6 < 2 .OR. k6 > SIZE(x6)-1) CYCLE
            IF (k8 < 2 .OR. k8 > SIZE(x8)-1) CYCLE

            ! P0/P1 are fixed Block 9 edge points.
            IF (side6 == 3) THEN
                p0x = blk(9)%x(i,1)
                p0y = blk(9)%y(i,1)
                p1x = blk(9)%x(i,nj9)
                p1y = blk(9)%y(i,nj9)
            ELSE
                p0x = blk(9)%x(i,nj9)
                p0y = blk(9)%y(i,nj9)
                p1x = blk(9)%x(i,1)
                p1y = blk(9)%y(i,1)
            END IF

            chord_len = SQRT((p1x-p0x)**2 + (p1y-p0y)**2)
            IF (chord_len <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE
            scale = block9_whole_line_blend_tangent_scale*chord_len

            ! Direction from Block 6 interior to its outer edge.
            im6 = SIZE(blk(6)%y,2) - 1
            rx = blk(6)%x(k6,SIZE(blk(6)%y,2)) - blk(6)%x(k6,im6)
            ry = blk(6)%y(k6,SIZE(blk(6)%y,2)) - blk(6)%y(k6,im6)
            rmag = SQRT(rx*rx + ry*ry)
            IF (rmag <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE
            rx = rx/rmag
            ry = ry/rmag

            ! Orient from p0 into the line.
            IF (((p1x-p0x)*rx + (p1y-p0y)*ry) < 0.0D0) THEN
                rx = -rx
                ry = -ry
            END IF
            t0x = scale*rx
            t0y = scale*ry

            ! Direction from Block 8 interior to its outer edge.
            im8 = SIZE(blk(8)%y,2) - 1
            rx = blk(8)%x(k8,SIZE(blk(8)%y,2)) - blk(8)%x(k8,im8)
            ry = blk(8)%y(k8,SIZE(blk(8)%y,2)) - blk(8)%y(k8,im8)
            rmag = SQRT(rx*rx + ry*ry)
            IF (rmag <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE
            rx = rx/rmag
            ry = ry/rmag

            IF (((p0x-p1x)*rx + (p0y-p1y)*ry) < 0.0D0) THEN
                rx = -rx
                ry = -ry
            END IF
            t1x = -scale*rx
            t1y = -scale*ry

            DO j = 2, nj9-1

                IF (side6 == 3) THEN
                    s = REAL(j-1,8)/REAL(nj9-1,8)
                ELSE
                    s = REAL(nj9-j,8)/REAL(nj9-1,8)
                END IF

                s = MAX(0.0D0, MIN(1.0D0, s))

                h00 =  2.0D0*s**3 - 3.0D0*s**2 + 1.0D0
                h10 =        s**3 - 2.0D0*s**2 + s
                h01 = -2.0D0*s**3 + 3.0D0*s**2
                h11 =        s**3 -       s**2

                xh = h00*p0x + h10*t0x + h01*p1x + h11*t1x
                yh = h00*p0y + h10*t0y + h01*p1y + h11*t1y

                blk(9)%x(i,j) = (1.0D0-relax)*blk(9)%x(i,j) + relax*xh
                blk(9)%y(i,j) = (1.0D0-relax)*blk(9)%y(i,j) + relax*yh

            END DO

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(x6,y6,x8,y8,x9a,y9a,x9b,y9b)

    END SUBROUTINE blend_block9_j_lines_between_6_8

    SUBROUTINE blend_block9_i_lines_between_6_8(blk, pitch, side6, side8)

        ! Handles the case where Block 9 connects Blocks 6 and 8 across i.
        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side6
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side8

        INTEGER :: ni9, nj9
        INTEGER :: i, j
        INTEGER :: k6, k8
        INTEGER :: im6, im8
        INTEGER :: kedge
        LOGICAL :: rev6, rev8
        REAL(8) :: yoff6, yoff8
        REAL(8) :: p0x, p0y, p1x, p1y
        REAL(8) :: t0x, t0y, t1x, t1y
        REAL(8) :: rx, ry, rmag
        REAL(8) :: chord_len, scale
        REAL(8) :: s, h00, h10, h01, h11
        REAL(8) :: xh, yh
        REAL(8) :: relax
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x6(:), y6(:), x8(:), y8(:), x9a(:), y9a(:), x9b(:), y9b(:)

        ni9 = SIZE(blk(9)%x,1)
        nj9 = SIZE(blk(9)%x,2)

        CALL get_edge_line(blk(6), 4, x6, y6)
        CALL get_edge_line(blk(8), 4, x8, y8)
        CALL get_edge_line(blk(9), side6, x9a, y9a)
        CALL get_edge_line(blk(9), side8, x9b, y9b)

        IF (SIZE(x6) /= SIZE(x9a) .OR. SIZE(x8) /= SIZE(x9b)) THEN
            PRINT *, "WARNING: Block 9 i-line blend skipped; edge sizes do not match."
            DEALLOCATE(x6,y6,x8,y8,x9a,y9a,x9b,y9b)
            RETURN
        END IF

        CALL periodic_match_endpoints(x6(1), y6(1), x6(SIZE(x6)), y6(SIZE(y6)), &
                                      x9a(1), y9a(1), x9a(SIZE(x9a)), y9a(SIZE(y9a)), &
                                      pitch, rev6, yoff6)

        CALL periodic_match_endpoints(x8(1), y8(1), x8(SIZE(x8)), y8(SIZE(y8)), &
                                      x9b(1), y9b(1), x9b(SIZE(x9b)), y9b(SIZE(y9b)), &
                                      pitch, rev8, yoff8)

        relax = MAX(0.0D0, MIN(1.0D0, block9_whole_line_blend_68_relax))

        DO j = 2, nj9-1

            kedge = j

            IF (rev6) THEN
                k6 = SIZE(x6) - kedge + 1
            ELSE
                k6 = kedge
            END IF

            IF (rev8) THEN
                k8 = SIZE(x8) - kedge + 1
            ELSE
                k8 = kedge
            END IF

            IF (k6 < 2 .OR. k6 > SIZE(x6)-1) CYCLE
            IF (k8 < 2 .OR. k8 > SIZE(x8)-1) CYCLE

            IF (side6 == 1) THEN
                p0x = blk(9)%x(1,j)
                p0y = blk(9)%y(1,j)
                p1x = blk(9)%x(ni9,j)
                p1y = blk(9)%y(ni9,j)
            ELSE
                p0x = blk(9)%x(ni9,j)
                p0y = blk(9)%y(ni9,j)
                p1x = blk(9)%x(1,j)
                p1y = blk(9)%y(1,j)
            END IF

            chord_len = SQRT((p1x-p0x)**2 + (p1y-p0y)**2)
            IF (chord_len <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE
            scale = block9_whole_line_blend_tangent_scale*chord_len

            im6 = SIZE(blk(6)%y,2) - 1
            rx = blk(6)%x(k6,SIZE(blk(6)%y,2)) - blk(6)%x(k6,im6)
            ry = blk(6)%y(k6,SIZE(blk(6)%y,2)) - blk(6)%y(k6,im6)
            rmag = SQRT(rx*rx + ry*ry)
            IF (rmag <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE
            rx = rx/rmag
            ry = ry/rmag

            IF (((p1x-p0x)*rx + (p1y-p0y)*ry) < 0.0D0) THEN
                rx = -rx
                ry = -ry
            END IF
            t0x = scale*rx
            t0y = scale*ry

            im8 = SIZE(blk(8)%y,2) - 1
            rx = blk(8)%x(k8,SIZE(blk(8)%y,2)) - blk(8)%x(k8,im8)
            ry = blk(8)%y(k8,SIZE(blk(8)%y,2)) - blk(8)%y(k8,im8)
            rmag = SQRT(rx*rx + ry*ry)
            IF (rmag <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE
            rx = rx/rmag
            ry = ry/rmag

            IF (((p0x-p1x)*rx + (p0y-p1y)*ry) < 0.0D0) THEN
                rx = -rx
                ry = -ry
            END IF
            t1x = -scale*rx
            t1y = -scale*ry

            DO i = 2, ni9-1

                IF (side6 == 1) THEN
                    s = REAL(i-1,8)/REAL(ni9-1,8)
                ELSE
                    s = REAL(ni9-i,8)/REAL(ni9-1,8)
                END IF

                s = MAX(0.0D0, MIN(1.0D0, s))

                h00 =  2.0D0*s**3 - 3.0D0*s**2 + 1.0D0
                h10 =        s**3 - 2.0D0*s**2 + s
                h01 = -2.0D0*s**3 + 3.0D0*s**2
                h11 =        s**3 -       s**2

                xh = h00*p0x + h10*t0x + h01*p1x + h11*t1x
                yh = h00*p0y + h10*t0y + h01*p1y + h11*t1y

                blk(9)%x(i,j) = (1.0D0-relax)*blk(9)%x(i,j) + relax*xh
                blk(9)%y(i,j) = (1.0D0-relax)*blk(9)%y(i,j) + relax*yh

            END DO

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(x6,y6,x8,y8,x9a,y9a,x9b,y9b)

    END SUBROUTINE blend_block9_i_lines_between_6_8

    SUBROUTINE find_block9_side_closest_to_block_edge_simple(blk, pitch, master_block, master_side, side_out)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: master_block, master_side
        INTEGER, INTENT(OUT) :: side_out

        INTEGER :: side, nm, n9, k, k9
        LOGICAL :: rev
        REAL(8) :: yoff, dsum, dmean, best_dmean, dx, dy
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xm(:), ym(:), x9(:), y9(:)

        side_out = 0
        best_dmean = HUGE(1.0D0)

        CALL get_edge_line(blk(master_block), master_side, xm, ym)
        nm = SIZE(xm)

        DO side = 1, 4
            CALL get_edge_line(blk(9), side, x9, y9)
            n9 = SIZE(x9)

            IF (n9 == nm) THEN
                CALL periodic_match_endpoints(xm(1), ym(1), xm(nm), ym(nm), &
                                              x9(1), y9(1), x9(n9), y9(n9), &
                                              pitch, rev, yoff)
                dsum = 0.0D0

                DO k = 1, nm
                    IF (rev) THEN
                        k9 = nm-k+1
                    ELSE
                        k9 = k
                    END IF

                    dx = x9(k9) - xm(k)
                    dy = (y9(k9)-yoff) - ym(k)
                    dsum = dsum + SQRT(dx*dx + dy*dy)
                END DO

                dmean = dsum/REAL(nm,8)

                IF (dmean < best_dmean) THEN
                    best_dmean = dmean
                    side_out = side
                END IF
            END IF

            DEALLOCATE(x9,y9)
        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(xm,ym)

        PRINT *, "Block 9 side detection simple: master block/side, side_out, mean distance = ", &
                 master_block, master_side, side_out, best_dmean

    END SUBROUTINE find_block9_side_closest_to_block_edge_simple

    SUBROUTINE find_block9_side_closest_to_block6_outer_edge(blk, pitch, side_out)

        ! Find which Block 9 edge is geometrically closest to the Block 6
        ! outer edge. This is more robust than relying on next_block(9,side)=6.
        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        INTEGER, INTENT(OUT) :: side_out

        INTEGER :: side
        INTEGER :: n6
        INTEGER :: n9
        INTEGER :: k
        INTEGER :: k9
        LOGICAL :: rev
        REAL(8) :: yoff
        REAL(8) :: dsum
        REAL(8) :: dmean
        REAL(8) :: best_dmean
        REAL(8) :: dx
        REAL(8) :: dy
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x6(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: y6(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x9(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: y9(:)

        side_out = 0
        best_dmean = HUGE(1.0D0)

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 9) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(6)%x)) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(9)%x)) RETURN

        ! Block 6 outer edge should normally be j-max = side 4.
        CALL get_edge_line(blk(6), 4, x6, y6)
        n6 = SIZE(x6)

        IF (n6 < 2) THEN
            DEALLOCATE(x6, y6)
            RETURN
        END IF

        DO side = 1, 4

            CALL get_edge_line(blk(9), side, x9, y9)
            n9 = SIZE(x9)

            IF (n9 == n6) THEN

                CALL periodic_match_endpoints( &
                    x6(1), y6(1), x6(n6), y6(n6), &
                    x9(1), y9(1), x9(n9), y9(n9), &
                    pitch, rev, yoff &
                )

                dsum = 0.0D0

                DO k = 1, n6
                    IF (rev) THEN
                        k9 = n6 - k + 1
                    ELSE
                        k9 = k
                    END IF

                    dx = x9(k9) - x6(k)
                    dy = (y9(k9) - yoff) - y6(k)
                    dsum = dsum + SQRT(dx*dx + dy*dy)
                END DO

                dmean = dsum/REAL(n6,8)

                IF (dmean < best_dmean) THEN
                    best_dmean = dmean
                    side_out = side
                END IF

            END IF

            DEALLOCATE(x9, y9)

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(x6, y6)

        PRINT *, "Geometric 6--9 edge detection mean distance = ", best_dmean

    END SUBROUTINE find_block9_side_closest_to_block6_outer_edge

    SUBROUTINE smooth_block9_only_fixed_edges(blk, pitch, n_passes, relax)

    ! Block 9 smoothing.
        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: n_passes
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax

        INTEGER :: pass
        INTEGER :: i
        INTEGER :: j
        INTEGER :: ni
        INTEGER :: nj
        INTEGER :: changed
        INTEGER :: total_changed
        REAL(8) :: xtarget
        REAL(8) :: ytarget
        REAL(8) :: xtry
        REAL(8) :: ytry
        REAL(8) :: area_after
        REAL(8) :: local_relax
        REAL(8) :: layer_weight
        INTEGER :: side6_on_block9_geom
        INTEGER :: dist_to_block6

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 9) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(9)%x)) RETURN

        ni = SIZE(blk(9)%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(blk(9)%x,2)

        IF (ni < 4 .OR. nj < 4) RETURN

        ! Geometrically detect which side of Block 9 faces Block 6.
        ! Do not rely on next_block(9,side), because the topology table may not
        CALL find_block9_side_closest_to_block6_outer_edge(blk, pitch, side6_on_block9_geom)

        total_changed = 0

        PRINT *, "Block 9 geometric side closest to Block 6 outer edge = ", side6_on_block9_geom
        PRINT *, "Block 9 smoothing near Block 6 relaxation factor = ", &
                 block9_smooth_relax_near_block6_factor
        PRINT *, "Block 9 smoothing near Block 6 protected layers = ", &
                 block9_smooth_near_block6_layers

        ! Ensure Block 9 edges remain exactly conformed to neighbours before
        ! smoothing starts.
        CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, 9)

        DO pass = 1, n_passes

            changed = 0

            ! Skip all edges.  Only Block 9 interior changes.
            DO j = 2, nj-1
                DO i = 2, ni-1

                    xtarget = (blk(9)%x(i-1,j) + blk(9)%x(i+1,j) + &
                               1.35D0*blk(9)%x(i,j-1) + 1.35D0*blk(9)%x(i,j+1)) / 4.70D0

                    ytarget = (blk(9)%y(i-1,j) + blk(9)%y(i+1,j) + &
                               1.35D0*blk(9)%y(i,j-1) + 1.35D0*blk(9)%y(i,j+1)) / 4.70D0

                    local_relax = relax

                    ! Weaken or freeze smoothing in Block 9 close to the
                    ! geometrically detected Block 6-facing edge.
                    IF (side6_on_block9_geom > 0) THEN
                        SELECT CASE (side6_on_block9_geom)
                        CASE (1)
                            dist_to_block6 = i - 1
                        CASE (2)
                            dist_to_block6 = ni - i
                        CASE (3)
                            dist_to_block6 = j - 1
                        CASE (4)
                            dist_to_block6 = nj - j
                        CASE DEFAULT
                            dist_to_block6 = 999999
                        END SELECT

                        IF (dist_to_block6 <= block9_smooth_near_block6_layers) THEN
                            layer_weight = REAL(dist_to_block6,8) / &
                                           REAL(MAX(1, block9_smooth_near_block6_layers),8)
                            layer_weight = MAX(0.0D0, MIN(1.0D0, layer_weight))

                            local_relax = relax * ( &
                                block9_smooth_relax_near_block6_factor + &
                                (1.0D0 - block9_smooth_relax_near_block6_factor)*layer_weight &
                            )
                        END IF
                    END IF

                    xtry = (1.0D0-local_relax)*blk(9)%x(i,j) + local_relax*xtarget
                    ytry = (1.0D0-local_relax)*blk(9)%y(i,j) + local_relax*ytarget

                    area_after = local_node_min_signed_area_trial(blk(9), i, j, xtry, ytry)

                    IF (area_after > 0.0D0) THEN
                        blk(9)%x(i,j) = xtry
                        blk(9)%y(i,j) = ytry
                        changed = changed + 1
                    END IF

                END DO
            END DO

            total_changed = total_changed + changed

            ! Re-lock only Block 9 edges every pass.  This prevents slow edge
            ! drift while keeping Blocks 6/8 untouched.
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, 9)

            IF (changed == 0) EXIT

        END DO

        PRINT *, "Block9-only fixed-edge smoothing total accepted moves = ", total_changed

    END SUBROUTINE smooth_block9_only_fixed_edges

    SUBROUTINE smooth_block8_against_block9_reference(blk, pitch, depth, relax_smooth, nretry)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: depth
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax_smooth
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nretry

        INTEGER :: side8, side9
        INTEGER :: nedge, k, k9, level
        INTEGER :: i8, j8, i9, j9
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xe8(:), ye8(:), xe9(:), ye9(:)
        LOGICAL :: rev
        REAL(8) :: yoff
        REAL(8) :: ex8, ey8, ex9, ey9
        REAL(8) :: d9x, d9y
        REAL(8) :: xtarget, ytarget
        REAL(8) :: xold, yold, xtry, ytry
        REAL(8) :: skew_before, skew_after
        REAL(8) :: area_before, area_after
        REAL(8) :: fac, decay
        INTEGER :: retry
        INTEGER :: ni8, nj8
        INTEGER :: nchanged, nrejected

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 9) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(8)%x)) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(9)%x)) RETURN

        side8 = find_connected_side(8, 9)
        side9 = find_connected_side(9, 8)

        IF (side8 <= 0 .OR. side9 <= 0) THEN
            PRINT *, "Warning: could not find Block 8--9 sides for reference smoother."
            RETURN
        END IF

        ni8 = SIZE(blk(8)%x,1)
        nj8 = SIZE(blk(8)%x,2)

        CALL get_edge_line(blk(8), side8, xe8, ye8)
        CALL get_edge_line(blk(9), side9, xe9, ye9)

        IF (SIZE(xe8) /= SIZE(xe9)) THEN
            DEALLOCATE(xe8, ye8, xe9, ye9)
            PRINT *, "Warning: Block 8--9 edge counts differ; skipping reference smoother."
            RETURN
        END IF

        nedge = SIZE(xe8)

        CALL periodic_match_endpoints( &
            xe8(1), ye8(1), xe8(nedge), ye8(nedge), &
            xe9(1), ye9(1), xe9(nedge), ye9(nedge), &
            pitch, rev, yoff &
        )

        nchanged = 0
        nrejected = 0

        ! Skip corner-controlled end region.
        DO k = 4, nedge-3

            IF (rev) THEN
                k9 = nedge - k + 1
            ELSE
                k9 = k
            END IF

            CALL get_patch_node(blk(8), side8, k, 0, ex8, ey8)
            CALL get_patch_node(blk(9), side9, k9, 0, ex9, ey9)

            ! Use Block 9 as the reference side.
            ! d9 points from the interface into Block 9.
            CALL get_patch_node(blk(9), side9, k9, 1, xtarget, ytarget)
            d9x = xtarget - ex9
            d9y = ytarget - ey9

            ! For C1 across the interface, the Block 8 interior should go
            ! in the opposite direction to Block 9's inward derivative.
            DO level = 1, depth

                CALL get_patch_indices(blk(8), side8, k, level, i8, j8)

                IF (i8 <= 1 .OR. i8 >= ni8) CYCLE
                IF (j8 <= 1 .OR. j8 >= nj8) CYCLE

                xold = blk(8)%x(i8,j8)
                yold = blk(8)%y(i8,j8)

                xtarget = ex8 - REAL(level,8)*d9x
                ytarget = ey8 - REAL(level,8)*d9y

                decay = 1.0D0 - REAL(level-1,8)/REAL(MAX(1,depth),8)
                fac = relax_smooth*decay

                skew_before = local_node_max_skewness_any(blk(8), i8, j8)
                area_before = local_node_min_signed_area_trial(blk(8), i8, j8, xold, yold)

                DO retry = 1, nretry

                    xtry = xold + fac*(xtarget - xold)
                    ytry = yold + fac*(ytarget - yold)

                    skew_after = local_node_max_skewness_any_trial(blk(8), i8, j8, xtry, ytry)
                    area_after = local_node_min_signed_area_trial(blk(8), i8, j8, xtry, ytry)

                    ! Strict: accept only if Block 8 local skewness improves
                    ! and no local cell is folded.
                    IF (skew_after < skew_before .AND. area_after > 0.0D0) THEN
                        blk(8)%x(i8,j8) = xtry
                        blk(8)%y(i8,j8) = ytry
                        nchanged = nchanged + 1
                        EXIT
                    END IF

                    ! Mild safe acceptance if both before/after are already below target.
                    IF (skew_before <= 0.40D0 .AND. skew_after <= 0.40D0 .AND. &
                        area_after >= 0.90D0*area_before) THEN
                        blk(8)%x(i8,j8) = xtry
                        blk(8)%y(i8,j8) = ytry
                        nchanged = nchanged + 1
                        EXIT
                    END IF

                    fac = 0.5D0*fac

                END DO

                IF (retry > nretry) nrejected = nrejected + 1

            END DO

        END DO

        PRINT *, "Block 8 against Block 9 reference smoother changed/rejected = ", &
                 nchanged, nrejected

        DEALLOCATE(xe8, ye8, xe9, ye9)

    END SUBROUTINE smooth_block8_against_block9_reference
    SUBROUTINE reset_and_smooth_block9_interior(blk, pitch, do_tfi_reset, npass, relax_smooth, skew_limit)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        LOGICAL, INTENT(IN) :: do_tfi_reset
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: npass
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax_smooth
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: skew_limit

        INTEGER :: pass, i, j, ni, nj
        INTEGER :: nbad, nchanged
        REAL(8) :: max_skew, node_skew
        LOGICAL :: changed

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 9) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(9)%x)) RETURN

        ni = SIZE(blk(9)%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(blk(9)%x,2)

        IF (ni < 4 .OR. nj < 4) RETURN

        CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, 9)

        IF (do_tfi_reset) THEN
            CALL reset_block9_interior_tfi(blk(9))
        END IF

        DO pass = 1, npass

            nbad = 0
            nchanged = 0
            max_skew = 0.0D0

            DO j = 2, nj-1
                DO i = 2, ni-1

                    node_skew = local_node_max_skewness_any(blk(9), i, j)
                    max_skew = MAX(max_skew, node_skew)

                    IF (node_skew > skew_limit) nbad = nbad + 1

                    CALL try_block9_poisson_node_move( &
                        blk(9), i, j, relax_smooth, skew_limit, changed &
                    )

                    IF (changed) nchanged = nchanged + 1

                END DO
            END DO

            PRINT *, "Block 9 TFI/Poisson repair pass, bad nodes, moved, max skew = ", &
                     pass, nbad, nchanged, max_skew

            IF (nbad == 0 .OR. nchanged == 0) EXIT

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE reset_and_smooth_block9_interior

    SUBROUTINE reset_block9_interior_tfi(b)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b

        INTEGER :: i, j, ni, nj
        REAL(8) :: xi, eta
        REAL(8) :: xleft, yleft, xright, yright
        REAL(8) :: xbot, ybot, xtop, ytop
        REAL(8) :: xbl, ybl, xbr, ybr, xtl, ytl, xtr, ytr

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        IF (ni < 4 .OR. nj < 4) RETURN

        xbl = b%x(1,1)
        ybl = b%y(1,1)
        xbr = b%x(ni,1)
        ybr = b%y(ni,1)
        xtl = b%x(1,nj)
        ytl = b%y(1,nj)
        xtr = b%x(ni,nj)
        ytr = b%y(ni,nj)

        DO j = 2, nj-1

            eta = REAL(j-1,8)/REAL(nj-1,8)

            DO i = 2, ni-1

                xi = REAL(i-1,8)/REAL(ni-1,8)

                xleft  = b%x(1,j)
                yleft  = b%y(1,j)
                xright = b%x(ni,j)
                yright = b%y(ni,j)

                xbot = b%x(i,1)
                ybot = b%y(i,1)
                xtop = b%x(i,nj)
                ytop = b%y(i,nj)

                b%x(i,j) = (1.0D0-xi)*xleft + xi*xright + &
                           (1.0D0-eta)*xbot + eta*xtop - &
                           ((1.0D0-xi)*(1.0D0-eta)*xbl + &
                            xi*(1.0D0-eta)*xbr + &
                            (1.0D0-xi)*eta*xtl + &
                            xi*eta*xtr)

                b%y(i,j) = (1.0D0-xi)*yleft + xi*yright + &
                           (1.0D0-eta)*ybot + eta*ytop - &
                           ((1.0D0-xi)*(1.0D0-eta)*ybl + &
                            xi*(1.0D0-eta)*ybr + &
                            (1.0D0-xi)*eta*ytl + &
                            xi*eta*ytr)

            END DO
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE reset_block9_interior_tfi

    SUBROUTINE try_block9_poisson_node_move(b, inode, jnode, relax_smooth, skew_limit, changed)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: inode, jnode
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax_smooth
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: skew_limit
        LOGICAL, INTENT(OUT) :: changed

        INTEGER :: ni, nj, retry
        REAL(8) :: xold, yold, xlap, ylap, xtry, ytry
        REAL(8) :: skew_before, skew_after
        REAL(8) :: area_after
        REAL(8) :: fac

        changed = .FALSE.

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        IF (inode <= 1 .OR. inode >= ni) RETURN
        IF (jnode <= 1 .OR. jnode >= nj) RETURN

        xold = b%x(inode,jnode)
        yold = b%y(inode,jnode)

        skew_before = local_node_max_skewness_any(b, inode, jnode)

        xlap = 0.25D0*(b%x(inode-1,jnode) + b%x(inode+1,jnode) + &
                       b%x(inode,jnode-1) + b%x(inode,jnode+1))
        ylap = 0.25D0*(b%y(inode-1,jnode) + b%y(inode+1,jnode) + &
                       b%y(inode,jnode-1) + b%y(inode,jnode+1))

        fac = relax_smooth

        DO retry = 1, 8

            xtry = xold + fac*(xlap - xold)
            ytry = yold + fac*(ylap - yold)

            skew_after = local_node_max_skewness_any_trial(b, inode, jnode, xtry, ytry)
            area_after = local_node_min_signed_area_trial(b, inode, jnode, xtry, ytry)

            IF (skew_after < skew_before .AND. area_after > 0.0D0) THEN
                b%x(inode,jnode) = xtry
                b%y(inode,jnode) = ytry
                changed = .TRUE.
                RETURN
            END IF

            IF (skew_before <= skew_limit .AND. skew_after <= skew_limit .AND. area_after > 0.0D0) THEN
                b%x(inode,jnode) = xtry
                b%y(inode,jnode) = ytry
                changed = .TRUE.
                RETURN
            END IF

            fac = 0.5D0*fac

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE try_block9_poisson_node_move

    SUBROUTINE force_rebuild_block9_interior(blk, pitch, npass, relax_smooth, skew_limit, boundary_bias)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: npass
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax_smooth
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: skew_limit
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: boundary_bias

        INTEGER :: pass, i, j, ni, nj
        INTEGER :: nbad
        REAL(8) :: max_skew, min_area

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 9) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(9)%x)) RETURN

        ni = SIZE(blk(9)%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(blk(9)%x,2)

        IF (ni < 4 .OR. nj < 4) RETURN

        ! Lock Block 9 boundaries from its neighbours first.
        CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, 9)

        ! Step 1: overwrite the Block 9 interior with a clean TFI field
        ! from its current four edges.  This is  not an
        CALL reset_block9_interior_tfi(blk(9))

        ! boundary-biased smoothing.  This directly targets cells adjacent
        ! to the fixed edges, which the previous interior-only skewness
        CALL block9_boundary_layer_bias_smooth(blk(9), boundary_bias)

        DO pass = 1, npass

            DO j = 2, nj-1
                DO i = 2, ni-1
                    CALL force_block9_laplace_node_move(blk(9), i, j, relax_smooth)
                END DO
            END DO

            IF (MOD(pass,25) == 0 .OR. pass == 1) THEN
                CALL block9_quality_summary(blk(9), max_skew, min_area, nbad, skew_limit)
                PRINT *, "Forced Block 9 smoother pass, bad cells, max skew, min area = ", &
                         pass, nbad, max_skew, min_area
            END IF

        END DO

        ! Final conservative cleanup using the original local skewness-improve rule.
        DO pass = 1, 40
            nbad = 0
            DO j = 2, nj-1
                DO i = 2, ni-1
                    IF (local_node_max_skewness_any(blk(9), i, j) > skew_limit) THEN
                        nbad = nbad + 1
                        CALL force_block9_laplace_node_move(blk(9), i, j, 0.18D0)
                    END IF
                END DO
            END DO
            IF (nbad == 0) EXIT
        END DO

        CALL block9_quality_summary(blk(9), max_skew, min_area, nbad, skew_limit)
        PRINT *, "Final Block 9-only repair quality: bad cells, max skew, min area = ", &
                 nbad, max_skew, min_area

        CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, 9)

    END SUBROUTINE force_rebuild_block9_interior

    SUBROUTINE block9_boundary_layer_bias_smooth(b, bias)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: bias

        INTEGER :: ni, nj, i, j

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        IF (ni < 5 .OR. nj < 5) RETURN

        ! Pull the first interior layer toward a smooth interpolation of
        ! the second and third interior layers, while keeping the edge fixed.
        DO i = 2, ni-1
            b%x(i,2)    = (1.0D0-bias)*b%x(i,2)    + bias*(2.0D0*b%x(i,3)    - b%x(i,4))
            b%y(i,2)    = (1.0D0-bias)*b%y(i,2)    + bias*(2.0D0*b%y(i,3)    - b%y(i,4))
            b%x(i,nj-1) = (1.0D0-bias)*b%x(i,nj-1) + bias*(2.0D0*b%x(i,nj-2) - b%x(i,nj-3))
            b%y(i,nj-1) = (1.0D0-bias)*b%y(i,nj-1) + bias*(2.0D0*b%y(i,nj-2) - b%y(i,nj-3))
        END DO

        DO j = 2, nj-1
            b%x(2,j)    = (1.0D0-bias)*b%x(2,j)    + bias*(2.0D0*b%x(3,j)    - b%x(4,j))
            b%y(2,j)    = (1.0D0-bias)*b%y(2,j)    + bias*(2.0D0*b%y(3,j)    - b%y(4,j))
            b%x(ni-1,j) = (1.0D0-bias)*b%x(ni-1,j) + bias*(2.0D0*b%x(ni-2,j) - b%x(ni-3,j))
            b%y(ni-1,j) = (1.0D0-bias)*b%y(ni-1,j) + bias*(2.0D0*b%y(ni-2,j) - b%y(ni-3,j))
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE block9_boundary_layer_bias_smooth

    SUBROUTINE force_block9_laplace_node_move(b, inode, jnode, relax_smooth)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: inode, jnode
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax_smooth

        INTEGER :: ni, nj, retry
        REAL(8) :: xold, yold, xtry, ytry, xlap, ylap
        REAL(8) :: area_after
        REAL(8) :: fac

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        IF (inode <= 1 .OR. inode >= ni) RETURN
        IF (jnode <= 1 .OR. jnode >= nj) RETURN

        xold = b%x(inode,jnode)
        yold = b%y(inode,jnode)

        xlap = 0.25D0*(b%x(inode-1,jnode) + b%x(inode+1,jnode) + &
                       b%x(inode,jnode-1) + b%x(inode,jnode+1))
        ylap = 0.25D0*(b%y(inode-1,jnode) + b%y(inode+1,jnode) + &
                       b%y(inode,jnode-1) + b%y(inode,jnode+1))

        fac = relax_smooth

        DO retry = 1, 8

            xtry = xold + fac*(xlap - xold)
            ytry = yold + fac*(ylap - yold)

            area_after = local_node_min_signed_area_trial(b, inode, jnode, xtry, ytry)

            IF (area_after > 0.0D0) THEN
                b%x(inode,jnode) = xtry
                b%y(inode,jnode) = ytry
                RETURN
            END IF

            fac = 0.5D0*fac

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE force_block9_laplace_node_move

    SUBROUTINE block9_quality_summary(b, max_skew, min_area, nbad, skew_limit)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: max_skew
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: min_area
        INTEGER, INTENT(OUT) :: nbad
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: skew_limit

        INTEGER :: i, j, ni, nj
        REAL(8) :: skew, area

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        max_skew = 0.0D0
        min_area = HUGE(1.0D0)
        nbad = 0

        DO j = 1, nj-1
            DO i = 1, ni-1

                skew = quad_cell_equiangular_skewness( &
                    b%x(i,j),     b%y(i,j), &
                    b%x(i+1,j),   b%y(i+1,j), &
                    b%x(i+1,j+1), b%y(i+1,j+1), &
                    b%x(i,j+1),   b%y(i,j+1) )

                area = 0.5D0*( &
                    b%x(i,j)*b%y(i+1,j) - b%x(i+1,j)*b%y(i,j) + &
                    b%x(i+1,j)*b%y(i+1,j+1) - b%x(i+1,j+1)*b%y(i+1,j) + &
                    b%x(i+1,j+1)*b%y(i,j+1) - b%x(i,j+1)*b%y(i+1,j+1) + &
                    b%x(i,j+1)*b%y(i,j) - b%x(i,j)*b%y(i,j+1) )

                max_skew = MAX(max_skew, skew)
                min_area = MIN(min_area, area)
                IF (skew > skew_limit) nbad = nbad + 1

            END DO
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE block9_quality_summary

    SUBROUTINE find_global_worst_skewness_cell(blk, nb, worst_skew, worst_block, worst_i, worst_j)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nb
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: worst_skew
        INTEGER, INTENT(OUT) :: worst_block, worst_i, worst_j

        INTEGER :: ib, i, j, ni, nj
        REAL(8) :: skew

        worst_skew = -1.0D0
        worst_block = -1
        worst_i = -1
        worst_j = -1

        DO ib = 1, MIN(nb, SIZE(blk))

            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE

            ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1)
            nj = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,2)

            DO j = 1, nj-1
                DO i = 1, ni-1

                    skew = quad_cell_equiangular_skewness( &
                        blk(ib)%x(i,j),     blk(ib)%y(i,j), &
                        blk(ib)%x(i+1,j),   blk(ib)%y(i+1,j), &
                        blk(ib)%x(i+1,j+1), blk(ib)%y(i+1,j+1), &
                        blk(ib)%x(i,j+1),   blk(ib)%y(i,j+1) )

                    IF (skew > worst_skew) THEN
                        worst_skew = skew
                        worst_block = ib
                        worst_i = i
                        worst_j = j
                    END IF

                END DO
            END DO

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE find_global_worst_skewness_cell

    SUBROUTINE realign_first_offwall_line_to_wall_normals(blk, npass, relax_move)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: npass
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax_move

        INTEGER :: pass, ib, ni, i
        INTEGER :: istart, iend, iskip_te
        INTEGER :: nchanged
        LOGICAL :: changed

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 8) RETURN

        DO pass = 1, npass

            nchanged = 0

            ! The current limiting cells reported by the diagnostic are
            ! in Block 6, j=1, and Block 8, j=1.  These are the upper and
            DO ib = 6, 8, 2

                IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE

                ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1)
                IF (SIZE(blk(ib)%x,2) < 3) CYCLE
                IF (ni < 5) CYCLE

                ! Skip the TE transition.  Block 6 reaches the trailing
                ! edge at high i; Block 8 reaches it at low i.  The TE cap
                iskip_te = MAX(8, INT(wall_first_offline_te_skip_fraction*REAL(ni,8)))

                IF (ib == 6) THEN
                    istart = 4
                    iend   = ni - iskip_te
                ELSE
                    istart = 1 + iskip_te
                    iend   = ni - 3
                END IF

                DO i = istart, iend

                    CALL try_first_offwall_normal_move(blk(ib), i, relax_move, changed)
                    IF (changed) nchanged = nchanged + 1

                END DO

            END DO

            PRINT *, "First off-wall normal realignment pass/moves = ", pass, nchanged

            IF (nchanged == 0) EXIT

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE realign_first_offwall_line_to_wall_normals

    SUBROUTINE try_first_offwall_normal_move(b, inode, relax_move, changed)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: inode
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax_move
        LOGICAL, INTENT(OUT) :: changed

        INTEGER :: ni, nj, retry
        REAL(8) :: xw, yw, tx, ty, tmag
        REAL(8) :: nx, ny
        REAL(8) :: xold, yold, dx, dy, spacing
        REAL(8) :: xnormal, ynormal
        REAL(8) :: xtan, ytan
        REAL(8) :: xtarget, ytarget
        REAL(8) :: xtry, ytry, fac
        REAL(8) :: skew_before, skew_after
        REAL(8) :: area_after

        changed = .FALSE.

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        IF (nj < 3) RETURN
        IF (inode <= 2 .OR. inode >= ni-1) RETURN

        ! Wall node and first off-wall node.
        xw = b%x(inode,1)
        yw = b%y(inode,1)

        xold = b%x(inode,2)
        yold = b%y(inode,2)

        ! Local blade-wall tangent from neighbouring wall points.
        tx = b%x(inode+1,1) - b%x(inode-1,1)
        ty = b%y(inode+1,1) - b%y(inode-1,1)
        tmag = SQRT(tx*tx + ty*ty)

        IF (tmag <= 1.0D-14) RETURN

        tx = tx/tmag
        ty = ty/tmag

        ! Candidate normal orientation.  Flip it so it points toward the
        ! current first off-wall point, not through the blade wall.
        nx = -ty
        ny =  tx

        dx = xold - xw
        dy = yold - yw

        IF (dx*nx + dy*ny < 0.0D0) THEN
            nx = -nx
            ny = -ny
        END IF

        spacing = SQRT(dx*dx + dy*dy)
        IF (spacing <= 1.0D-14) RETURN

        ! Normal target preserves the local first-cell height but makes
        ! the wall-normal grid line perpendicular to the fixed wall.
        xnormal = xw + spacing*nx
        ynormal = yw + spacing*ny

        ! Mild tangential smoothing of the first off-wall line as well.
        xtan = 0.5D0*(b%x(inode-1,2) + b%x(inode+1,2))
        ytan = 0.5D0*(b%y(inode-1,2) + b%y(inode+1,2))

        xtarget = 0.70D0*xnormal + 0.30D0*xtan
        ytarget = 0.70D0*ynormal + 0.30D0*ytan

        skew_before = local_node_max_skewness_any(b, inode, 2)

        fac = relax_move

        DO retry = 1, 8

            xtry = xold + fac*(xtarget - xold)
            ytry = yold + fac*(ytarget - yold)

            skew_after = local_node_max_skewness_any_trial(b, inode, 2, xtry, ytry)
            area_after = local_node_min_signed_area_trial(b, inode, 2, xtry, ytry)

            ! Primary rule: improve the actual local worst cell quality.
            IF (skew_after < skew_before - 1.0D-5 .AND. area_after > 0.0D0) THEN
                b%x(inode,2) = xtry
                b%y(inode,2) = ytry
                changed = .TRUE.
                RETURN
            END IF
            fac = 0.5D0*fac

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE try_first_offwall_normal_move

SUBROUTINE optimise_miw_full_loop(blk, pitch, nsmooth, relax, ywall, xprof, yprof, nprof)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nsmooth
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: ywall
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xprof(:), yprof(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nprof

        TYPE(block_type), ALLOCATABLE :: blk_old(:)
        TYPE(block_type), ALLOCATABLE :: blk_best_c1(:)
        TYPE(block_type) :: fixed_blade_wall(4)

        INTEGER :: iter
        INTEGER :: ib
        INTEGER :: best_c1_iter
        INTEGER :: c1_no_improve_count
        LOGICAL :: have_best_c1_state
        REAL(8) :: block9_max_skew_before, block9_max_skew_after
        REAL(8) :: block9_min_area_tmp
        INTEGER :: block9_nbad_tmp
        REAL(8) :: min_jac
        REAL(8) :: current_c1_angle
        REAL(8) :: best_c1_angle

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 9) THEN
            PRINT *, "optimise_miw_full_loop requires at least 9 blocks."
            STOP
        END IF

        ALLOCATE(blk_old(SIZE(blk)))
        ALLOCATE(blk_best_c1(SIZE(blk)))

        best_c1_angle = HUGE(1.0D0)
        current_c1_angle = HUGE(1.0D0)
        best_c1_iter = 0
        c1_no_improve_count = 0
        have_best_c1_state = .FALSE.

        ! Lock the original j=1 blade-wall coordinates forever.
        ! These are the blade boundary points created during the initial
        IF (fixed_initial_blade_wall_enabled) THEN
            CALL save_initial_blade_wall_points(blk, fixed_blade_wall)
        END IF

        !   1. copy old blocks
        !   2. smooth every block against frozen neighbours
        CALL validate_next_patch_topology(blk, next_block, next_patch, pitch)

        IF (c1_best_state_rollback_enabled) THEN
            CALL compute_global_c1_max_angle(blk, pitch, current_c1_angle)
            best_c1_angle = current_c1_angle
            best_c1_iter = 0
            have_best_c1_state = .TRUE.
            CALL copy_blocks_xy(blk, blk_best_c1)
            PRINT *, "C1 best-state tracking initial max angle = ", best_c1_angle
        END IF

        DO iter = 1, nsmooth

            CALL copy_blocks_xy(blk, blk_old)

            DO ib = 1, 9
                CALL smooth_block_miw_style(blk, blk_old, ib, pitch, relax)
            END DO

            CALL enforce_blade_wall_profile_and_spacing_jm( &
                blk, xprof, yprof, nprof, ywall &
            )

            IF (fixed_initial_blade_wall_enabled) THEN
                CALL restore_initial_blade_wall_points(blk, fixed_blade_wall)
            END IF

            CALL enforce_shared_blade_radial_edge_full_average(blk, pitch)

            DO ib = 1, 9
                CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
            END DO

            ! Match first-interior-line derivatives across connected block
            ! interfaces.  This reduces C1 jumps at block intersections
            CALL enforce_interface_c1_tolerance( &
                blk, pitch, c1_match_tolerance_fraction, c1_match_relaxation &
            )

            CALL corner_patch_our_topology(blk, pitch, corner_equal_angle_force, .FALSE.)

            DO ib = 1, 9
                CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
            END DO

            CALL enforce_miw_hard_boundaries(blk)

            IF (block6_middle_radial_straighten_each_iter_enabled) THEN
                CALL straighten_block6_middle_radial_lines(blk)
                CALL enforce_miw_hard_boundaries(blk)
            END IF

            IF (block8_middle_radial_straighten_each_iter_enabled) THEN
                CALL straighten_block8_middle_radial_lines(blk)
                CALL enforce_miw_hard_boundaries(blk)
            END IF

            IF (c1_best_state_rollback_enabled) THEN
                CALL compute_global_c1_max_angle(blk, pitch, current_c1_angle)

                IF (current_c1_angle < best_c1_angle) THEN
                    best_c1_angle = current_c1_angle
                    best_c1_iter = iter
                    c1_no_improve_count = 0
                    have_best_c1_state = .TRUE.
                    CALL copy_blocks_xy(blk, blk_best_c1)
                    PRINT *, "C1 best-state updated: iter, max angle = ", &
                             iter, best_c1_angle
                ELSE
                    c1_no_improve_count = c1_no_improve_count + 1
                END IF

                IF (c1_no_improve_count >= c1_best_state_patience) THEN
                    PRINT *, "C1 best-state early stop: no improvement for iterations = ", &
                             c1_no_improve_count
                    PRINT *, "Restoring best C1 state from iter, max angle = ", &
                             best_c1_iter, best_c1_angle
                    CALL copy_blocks_xy(blk_best_c1, blk)
                    EXIT
                END IF
            END IF

            IF (MOD(iter,25) == 0 .OR. iter == 1) THEN
                CALL compute_global_min_jacobian(blk, 9, min_jac)
                PRINT *, "iter, min signed cell area/J = ", iter, min_jac
                CALL diagnose_all_connected_interface_mismatch(blk, pitch)
            END IF

        END DO

        DO ib = 1, 9
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
        END DO

        ! corner collapse while still applying the meshing_iw-style
        ! local corner cleanup once the field has converged.
