        CALL corner_patch_our_topology(blk, pitch, corner_equal_angle_force, .FALSE.)

        DO ib = 1, 9
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
        END DO

        ! Final corner-edge smoothing: fit the first ten points
        ! into each non-wall corner edge with a cubic Hermite spline.
        CALL enforce_corner_ten_point_splines(blk, pitch)

        DO ib = 1, 9
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
        END DO

        CALL fix_cap_corner_nodes_like_miw(blk)

        DO ib = 1, 9
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
        END DO

        CALL enforce_interface_c1_tolerance( &
            blk, pitch, c1_match_tolerance_fraction, c1_match_relaxation &
        )

        ! Dedicated Block 8--9 repair.  This is a patch/Poisson-style
        ! smoother, not a line-only interface correction, so it cannot
        IF (fixed_initial_blade_wall_enabled) THEN
            CALL restore_initial_blade_wall_points(blk, fixed_blade_wall)
        END IF

        IF (block9_only_safe_interface_smoothing_enabled) THEN
            PRINT *, "Skipping two-sided Block 8--9 patch smoother; smoothing Block 9 interior only."
            CALL smooth_block9_only_fixed_edges( &
                blk, pitch, block9_only_interface_smooth_passes, &
                block9_only_interface_smooth_relax &
            )
        ELSE
            CALL smooth_block89_patch_poisson_skew_limited( &
                blk, pitch, block89_patch_depth, block89_patch_passes, &
                block89_patch_relax, block89_patch_skew_limit &
            )
        END IF

        DO ib = 1, 9
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
        END DO

        CALL improve_high_skewness_cells( &
            blk, 9, global_skewness_repair_limit, &
            global_skewness_repair_passes, global_skewness_repair_relax &
        )

        DO ib = 1, 9
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
        END DO

        ! Single post-optimisation smoother for tangential grid lines
        ! close to the blade surface in blocks 5--8.  This smooths
        CALL smooth_blade_tangential_lines_once( &
            blk, blade_tangential_smooth_nlines, blade_tangential_smooth_relax &
        )

        IF (fixed_initial_blade_wall_enabled) THEN
            CALL restore_initial_blade_wall_points(blk, fixed_blade_wall)
        END IF

        DO ib = 1, 9
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
        END DO

        ! Block 9-only targeted repair:
        ! do not move Block 6 or Block 8.  Smooth the entire transition inside
        IF (block9_only_safe_interface_smoothing_enabled) THEN
            CALL smooth_block9_only_fixed_edges( &
                blk, pitch, block9_only_interface_smooth_passes, &
                block9_only_interface_smooth_relax &
            )
        ELSE
            CALL smooth_block8_against_block9_reference( &
                blk, pitch, block89_b8_ref_depth, block89_b8_ref_relax, &
                block89_b8_ref_retries &
            )
        END IF

        IF (fixed_initial_blade_wall_enabled) THEN
            CALL restore_initial_blade_wall_points(blk, fixed_blade_wall)
        END IF

        DO ib = 1, 9
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
        END DO

        ! Block 9-only repair: rebuild the interior from its four fixed
        ! boundaries using TFI, then smooth only Block 9 interior nodes with
        CALL block9_quality_summary(blk(9), block9_max_skew_before, block9_min_area_tmp, &
            block9_nbad_tmp, block9_skewness_limit)
        PRINT *, "Block 9 max skew before forced rebuild = ", block9_max_skew_before

        CALL force_rebuild_block9_interior( &
            blk, pitch, block9_force_smooth_passes, block9_force_smooth_relax, &
            block9_skewness_limit, block9_boundary_line_bias &
        )

        IF (block9_only_safe_interface_smoothing_enabled) THEN
            CALL smooth_block9_only_fixed_edges( &
                blk, pitch, block9_only_interface_smooth_passes, &
                block9_only_interface_smooth_relax &
            )
        END IF

        CALL block9_quality_summary(blk(9), block9_max_skew_after, block9_min_area_tmp, &
            block9_nbad_tmp, block9_skewness_limit)
        PRINT *, "Block 9 max skew after forced rebuild = ", block9_max_skew_after

        DO ib = 1, 9
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
        END DO

        ! The worst cells are wall-adjacent cells in blocks 6/8.
        ! Smooth/realign the first off-wall line j=2 against the fixed
        IF (wall_first_offline_normalise_enabled) THEN
            CALL realign_first_offwall_line_to_wall_normals( &
                blk, wall_first_offline_normalise_passes, &
                wall_first_offline_normalise_relax &
            )
        END IF

        IF (fixed_initial_blade_wall_enabled) THEN
            CALL restore_initial_blade_wall_points(blk, fixed_blade_wall)
        END IF

        DO ib = 1, 9
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
        END DO

        ! Make Block 6 interior lines more radial while keeping every Block 6
        ! edge fixed.  This  allows the Block 6--9 derivative
        IF (block6_fixed_edge_radialise_enabled) THEN
            CALL radialise_block6_interior_fixed_edges(blk)
        END IF

        IF (c1_best_state_rollback_enabled .AND. have_best_c1_state) THEN
            CALL compute_global_c1_max_angle(blk, pitch, current_c1_angle)

            IF (current_c1_angle > best_c1_angle + c1_best_state_restore_tolerance_degrees) THEN
                PRINT *, "C1 best-state rollback at final output."
                PRINT *, "  current max angle = ", current_c1_angle
                PRINT *, "  restored max angle = ", best_c1_angle
                PRINT *, "  restored best iteration = ", best_c1_iter

                CALL copy_blocks_xy(blk_best_c1, blk)

                IF (fixed_initial_blade_wall_enabled) THEN
                    CALL restore_initial_blade_wall_points(blk, fixed_blade_wall)
                END IF

                DO ib = 1, 9
                    CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
                END DO
            ELSE
                PRINT *, "C1 best-state rollback not needed."
                PRINT *, "  current max angle = ", current_c1_angle
                PRINT *, "  best max angle = ", best_c1_angle
                PRINT *, "  best iteration = ", best_c1_iter
            END IF
        END IF

        IF (block6_middle_radial_straighten_final_step_enabled) THEN
            PRINT *, "FINAL STEP: straightening middle Block 6 radial lines."
            CALL straighten_block6_middle_radial_lines(blk)

            ! Do NOT call enforce_miw_hard_boundaries here.
            PRINT *, "FINAL STEP Block 6 complete."
        END IF

        IF (block8_middle_radial_straighten_final_step_enabled) THEN
            PRINT *, "FINAL STEP: straightening middle Block 8 radial lines."
            CALL straighten_block8_middle_radial_lines(blk)

            ! Do NOT call enforce_miw_hard_boundaries here.
            PRINT *, "FINAL STEP Block 8 complete."
        END IF

        IF (block9_global_smooth_after_68_enabled) THEN
            PRINT *, "FINAL STEP: globally smoothing all Block 9 interior nodes."
            CALL smooth_block9_global_fixed_edges_after_68(blk, pitch)
            PRINT *, "FINAL STEP Block 9 global smoothing complete."
        END IF

        IF (block9_whole_line_blend_68_enabled) THEN
            PRINT *, "FINAL STEP: blending whole Block 9 lines between Blocks 6 and 8."
            CALL blend_block9_lines_between_block6_block8_radials(blk, pitch)
            PRINT *, "FINAL STEP Block 9 whole-line blend complete."
        END IF

        PRINT *, "FINAL STEP complete: no boundary enforcement after this point."

        CALL compute_global_min_jacobian(blk, 9, min_jac)
        PRINT *, "Final min signed cell area/J = ", min_jac
        CALL diagnose_all_connected_interface_mismatch(blk, pitch)

        CALL report_worst_cell_skewness(blk, 9, 20)

        IF (fixed_initial_blade_wall_enabled) THEN
            CALL restore_initial_blade_wall_points(blk, fixed_blade_wall)
        END IF

        CALL free_blade_wall_storage(fixed_blade_wall)
        CALL free_blocks_xy(blk_old)
        CALL free_blocks_xy(blk_best_c1)
        DEALLOCATE(blk_old)
        DEALLOCATE(blk_best_c1)

    END SUBROUTINE optimise_miw_full_loop

    SUBROUTINE smooth_block_miw_style(blk, blk_old, ib, pitch, relax)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk_old(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ib
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax

        ! This is our version of meshing_iw's smooth_block.
        ! alpha/beta/gamma elliptic update to the whole block.
        CALL elliptic_poisson_any_block_free_edges( &
            blk, blk_old, ib, pitch, 1, relax &
        )

    END SUBROUTINE smooth_block_miw_style

    SUBROUTINE enforce_shared_blade_radial_edge_full_average(blk, pitch)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch

        ! Direct full-edge averaging at blade-block radial interfaces.
        ! For the whole shared edge, the interface line is set from the
        IF (SIZE(blk) < 8) RETURN

        CALL enforce_one_shared_blade_radial_edge_full_average(blk, 5, 2, 6, 1, pitch)
        CALL enforce_one_shared_blade_radial_edge_full_average(blk, 6, 2, 7, 1, pitch)
        CALL enforce_one_shared_blade_radial_edge_full_average(blk, 7, 2, 8, 1, pitch)
        CALL enforce_one_shared_blade_radial_edge_full_average(blk, 8, 2, 5, 1, pitch)

    END SUBROUTINE enforce_shared_blade_radial_edge_full_average

    SUBROUTINE enforce_one_shared_blade_radial_edge_full_average(blk, ib1, side1, ib2, side2, pitch)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ib1, side1, ib2, side2
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch

        INTEGER :: n1, n2, n
        INTEGER :: k, k2
        LOGICAL :: rev
        REAL(8) :: yoff
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xe1(:), ye1(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xe2(:), ye2(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xe2r(:), ye2r(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xe2m(:), ye2m(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xi1(:), yi1(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xi2(:), yi2(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xi2r(:), yi2r(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xi2m(:), yi2m(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xmaster(:), ymaster(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xb(:), yb(:)

        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib1)%x)) RETURN
        IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib2)%x)) RETURN

        CALL get_edge_line(blk(ib1), side1, xe1, ye1)
        CALL get_edge_line(blk(ib2), side2, xe2, ye2)

        n1 = SIZE(xe1)
        n2 = SIZE(xe2)

        IF (n1 < 3 .OR. n2 < 3) THEN
            DEALLOCATE(xe1, ye1, xe2, ye2)
            RETURN
        END IF

        n = n1

        CALL periodic_match_endpoints( &
            xe1(1), ye1(1), xe1(n1), ye1(n1), &
            xe2(1), ye2(1), xe2(n2), ye2(n2), &
            pitch, rev, yoff &
        )

        ALLOCATE(xe2r(n), ye2r(n), xe2m(n), ye2m(n))
        ALLOCATE(xi1(n), yi1(n), xi2(n2), yi2(n2), xi2r(n), yi2r(n), xi2m(n), yi2m(n))
        ALLOCATE(xmaster(n), ymaster(n), xb(n), yb(n))

        ! Put block-2 edge into block-1's point count.
        IF (n2 == n) THEN
            xe2r(:) = xe2(:)
            ye2r(:) = ye2(:)
        ELSE
            CALL resample_line(xe2, ye2, n2, xe2r, ye2r, n)
        END IF

        DO k = 1, n
            IF (rev) THEN
                k2 = n - k + 1
            ELSE
                k2 = k
            END IF

            xe2m(k) = xe2r(k2)
            ye2m(k) = ye2r(k2) + yoff
        END DO

        CALL get_adjacent_i_side_line_full_average(blk(ib1), side1, xi1, yi1)
        CALL get_adjacent_i_side_line_full_average(blk(ib2), side2, xi2, yi2)

        IF (n2 == n) THEN
            xi2r(:) = xi2(:)
            yi2r(:) = yi2(:)
        ELSE
            CALL resample_line(xi2, yi2, n2, xi2r, yi2r, n)
        END IF

        DO k = 1, n
            IF (rev) THEN
                k2 = n - k + 1
            ELSE
                k2 = k
            END IF

            xi2m(k) = xi2r(k2)
            yi2m(k) = yi2r(k2) + yoff
        END DO

        ! Endpoints remain the matched shared-edge endpoints.
        xmaster(1) = 0.5D0*(xe1(1) + xe2m(1))
        ymaster(1) = 0.5D0*(ye1(1) + ye2m(1))

        xmaster(n) = 0.5D0*(xe1(n) + xe2m(n))
        ymaster(n) = 0.5D0*(ye1(n) + ye2m(n))

        ! The whole interior of the interface edge is the average of the
        ! two adjacent same-j interior grid lines.
        DO k = 2, n-1
            xmaster(k) = 0.5D0*(xi1(k) + xi2m(k))
            ymaster(k) = 0.5D0*(yi1(k) + yi2m(k))
        END DO

        CALL set_edge_line(blk(ib1), side1, xmaster, ymaster)

        DO k = 1, n
            IF (rev) THEN
                k2 = n - k + 1
            ELSE
                k2 = k
            END IF

            xb(k2) = xmaster(k)
            yb(k2) = ymaster(k) - yoff
        END DO

        CALL set_edge_line_resampled(blk(ib2), side2, xb, yb)

        DEALLOCATE(xe1, ye1, xe2, ye2)
        DEALLOCATE(xe2r, ye2r, xe2m, ye2m)
        DEALLOCATE(xi1, yi1, xi2, yi2, xi2r, yi2r, xi2m, yi2m)
        DEALLOCATE(xmaster, ymaster, xb, yb)

    END SUBROUTINE enforce_one_shared_blade_radial_edge_full_average

    SUBROUTINE get_adjacent_i_side_line_full_average(b, side, xline, yline)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE, INTENT(OUT) :: xline(:), yline(:)

        INTEGER :: ni, nj
        INTEGER :: j, ii

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        ALLOCATE(xline(nj), yline(nj))

        SELECT CASE (side)
        CASE (1)
            ii = MIN(2, ni)
        CASE (2)
            ii = MAX(1, ni-1)
        CASE DEFAULT
            PRINT *, "get_adjacent_i_side_line_full_average only supports i-side edges."
            STOP
        END SELECT

        DO j = 1, nj
            xline(j) = b%x(ii,j)
            yline(j) = b%y(ii,j)
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE get_adjacent_i_side_line_full_average

    SUBROUTINE make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ib

        ! interface matching.
        ! This follows meshing_iw's make_periodic idea:
        IF (ib < 1 .OR. ib > 9) RETURN

        CALL make_periodic_topology(blk, next_block, next_patch, ib, pitch)

    END SUBROUTINE make_periodic_miw_style

    SUBROUTINE fix_boundary_node_if_collapsed_like_miw(xm, ym, x0, y0, xp, yp)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xm, ym, xp, yp
        REAL(8), INTENT(INOUT) :: x0, y0

        REAL(8) :: dprev, dnext, dspan, dotp

        dprev = SQRT((x0 - xm)**2 + (y0 - ym)**2)
        dnext = SQRT((xp - x0)**2 + (yp - y0)**2)
        dspan = SQRT((xp - xm)**2 + (yp - ym)**2)

        IF (dspan <= 1.0D-14) RETURN

        dotp = (x0 - xm)*(xp - x0) + (y0 - ym)*(yp - y0)

        IF (dotp <= 0.0D0 .OR. dprev <= 1.0D-12*dspan .OR. dnext <= 1.0D-12*dspan) THEN
            x0 = 0.5D0*(xm + xp)
            y0 = 0.5D0*(ym + yp)
        END IF

    END SUBROUTINE fix_boundary_node_if_collapsed_like_miw

    SUBROUTINE fix_cap_corner_nodes_like_miw(blk)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)

        INTEGER :: ib, ni, nj

        ! Generalised version of meshing_iw's fix_cap_corner_nodes for
        ! our block numbering.  It does not try to globally optimise the
        IF (SIZE(blk) < 8) RETURN

        DO ib = 5, 8

            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE

            ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1)
            nj = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,2)

            IF (ni < 4 .OR. nj < 4) CYCLE

            ! Harmonic/Laplace repair of first interior nodes near the
            ! four corners of each blade block.
            CALL laplace_repair_node(blk(ib), 2,     2)
            CALL laplace_repair_node(blk(ib), ni-1,  2)
            CALL laplace_repair_node(blk(ib), 2,     nj-1)
            CALL laplace_repair_node(blk(ib), ni-1,  nj-1)

            ! Boundary-line collapse checks on all four near-corner
            ! edge segments.  This is the direct analogue of
            CALL fix_boundary_node_if_collapsed_like_miw( &
                blk(ib)%x(1,1), blk(ib)%y(1,1), &
                blk(ib)%x(2,1), blk(ib)%y(2,1), &
                blk(ib)%x(3,1), blk(ib)%y(3,1) )

            CALL fix_boundary_node_if_collapsed_like_miw( &
                blk(ib)%x(ni,1), blk(ib)%y(ni,1), &
                blk(ib)%x(ni-1,1), blk(ib)%y(ni-1,1), &
                blk(ib)%x(ni-2,1), blk(ib)%y(ni-2,1) )

            CALL fix_boundary_node_if_collapsed_like_miw( &
                blk(ib)%x(1,nj), blk(ib)%y(1,nj), &
                blk(ib)%x(2,nj), blk(ib)%y(2,nj), &
                blk(ib)%x(3,nj), blk(ib)%y(3,nj) )

            CALL fix_boundary_node_if_collapsed_like_miw( &
                blk(ib)%x(ni,nj), blk(ib)%y(ni,nj), &
                blk(ib)%x(ni-1,nj), blk(ib)%y(ni-1,nj), &
                blk(ib)%x(ni-2,nj), blk(ib)%y(ni-2,nj) )

            CALL fix_boundary_node_if_collapsed_like_miw( &
                blk(ib)%x(1,1), blk(ib)%y(1,1), &
                blk(ib)%x(1,2), blk(ib)%y(1,2), &
                blk(ib)%x(1,3), blk(ib)%y(1,3) )

            CALL fix_boundary_node_if_collapsed_like_miw( &
                blk(ib)%x(1,nj), blk(ib)%y(1,nj), &
                blk(ib)%x(1,nj-1), blk(ib)%y(1,nj-1), &
                blk(ib)%x(1,nj-2), blk(ib)%y(1,nj-2) )

            CALL fix_boundary_node_if_collapsed_like_miw( &
                blk(ib)%x(ni,1), blk(ib)%y(ni,1), &
                blk(ib)%x(ni,2), blk(ib)%y(ni,2), &
                blk(ib)%x(ni,3), blk(ib)%y(ni,3) )

            CALL fix_boundary_node_if_collapsed_like_miw( &
                blk(ib)%x(ni,nj), blk(ib)%y(ni,nj), &
                blk(ib)%x(ni,nj-1), blk(ib)%y(ni,nj-1), &
                blk(ib)%x(ni,nj-2), blk(ib)%y(ni,nj-2) )

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE fix_cap_corner_nodes_like_miw

    SUBROUTINE laplace_repair_node(b, i, j)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: i, j

        INTEGER :: ni, nj

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        IF (i <= 1 .OR. i >= ni) RETURN
        IF (j <= 1 .OR. j >= nj) RETURN

        b%x(i,j) = 0.25D0*(b%x(i-1,j) + b%x(i+1,j) + b%x(i,j-1) + b%x(i,j+1))
        b%y(i,j) = 0.25D0*(b%y(i-1,j) + b%y(i+1,j) + b%y(i,j-1) + b%y(i,j+1))

    END SUBROUTINE laplace_repair_node

    SUBROUTINE enforce_corner_ten_point_splines(blk, pitch)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch

        TYPE(corner_patch_type) :: corner(4)
        INTEGER :: n, m, ib, ni, nj, ic, jc
        INTEGER :: iflag, jflag

        IF (SIZE(blk) < 9) RETURN

        CALL initialise_corner_patch_groups_our_topology(blk, pitch, corner)

        DO n = 1, SIZE(corner)

            IF (corner(n)%Nb <= 0) CYCLE

            DO m = 1, corner(n)%Nb

                ib = corner(n)%block(m)
                IF (ib <= 0) CYCLE
                IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE

                ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1)
                nj = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,2)

                ic = corner(n)%i(m)
                jc = corner(n)%j(m)

                IF (ic /= 1) ic = ni
                IF (jc /= 1) jc = nj

                IF (ic == 1) THEN
                    iflag = 1
                ELSE
                    iflag = -1
                END IF

                IF (jc == 1) THEN
                    jflag = 1
                ELSE
                    jflag = -1
                END IF

                ! Fit/smooth the i-edge incident to this corner, unless
                ! it is the blade wall edge.  For blade blocks 5-8, j=1
                IF (.NOT. is_blade_wall_edge_i(ib, jc)) THEN
                    CALL fit_ten_point_corner_spline_i(blk(ib), ic, jc, iflag)
                END IF

                ! Fit/smooth the j-edge incident to this corner.  This is
                ! allowed even for blade blocks because it is a radial/block
                CALL fit_ten_point_corner_spline_j(blk(ib), ic, jc, jflag)

            END DO

        END DO

        ! Restore C0 matching after edge-spline correction.
        DO ib = 1, 9
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
        END DO

        DO ib = 1, 9
            CALL make_periodic_miw_style(blk, pitch, ib)
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE enforce_corner_ten_point_splines

    LOGICAL FUNCTION is_blade_wall_edge_i(ib, jedge)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ib, jedge

        is_blade_wall_edge_i = .FALSE.

        IF (ib >= 5 .AND. ib <= 8 .AND. jedge == 1) THEN
            is_blade_wall_edge_i = .TRUE.
        END IF

    END FUNCTION is_blade_wall_edge_i

    SUBROUTINE fit_ten_point_corner_spline_i(b, ic, jc, iflag)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ic, jc, iflag

        INTEGER :: ni, nfit, p
        INTEGER :: i0, iend, i11
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: s(:)
        REAL(8) :: total, t, h00, h10, h01, h11
        REAL(8) :: x0, y0, x10, y10
        REAL(8) :: tx0, ty0, tx10, ty10, mag0, mag10

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)

        nfit = 10

        ! Need points 0..10 plus point 11.  Point 0 is the corner.
        IF (ic + nfit*iflag < 1 .OR. ic + nfit*iflag > ni) RETURN
        IF (ic + (nfit+1)*iflag < 1 .OR. ic + (nfit+1)*iflag > ni) RETURN

        ALLOCATE(s(0:nfit))

        s(0) = 0.0D0

        DO p = 1, nfit
            s(p) = s(p-1) + SQRT( &
                (b%x(ic+p*iflag,jc) - b%x(ic+(p-1)*iflag,jc))**2 + &
                (b%y(ic+p*iflag,jc) - b%y(ic+(p-1)*iflag,jc))**2 )
        END DO

        total = s(nfit)

        IF (total <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            DEALLOCATE(s)
            RETURN
        END IF

        i0   = ic
        iend = ic + nfit*iflag
        i11  = ic + (nfit+1)*iflag

        x0  = b%x(i0,jc)
        y0  = b%y(i0,jc)
        x10 = b%x(iend,jc)
        y10 = b%y(iend,jc)

        ! Corner-side tangent: use the direction from point 0 to point 2
        ! for stability instead of the possibly bad final first segment.
        tx0 = b%x(ic+2*iflag,jc) - b%x(ic,jc)
        ty0 = b%y(ic+2*iflag,jc) - b%y(ic,jc)

        mag0 = SQRT(tx0*tx0 + ty0*ty0)

        IF (mag0 <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            tx0 = x10 - x0
            ty0 = y10 - y0
            mag0 = SQRT(tx0*tx0 + ty0*ty0)
        END IF

        IF (mag0 <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            DEALLOCATE(s)
            RETURN
        END IF

        tx0 = tx0/mag0
        ty0 = ty0/mag0

        ! Required condition: derivative at point 10 of the fitted spline
        tx10 = b%x(i11,jc) - b%x(iend,jc)
        ty10 = b%y(i11,jc) - b%y(iend,jc)

        mag10 = SQRT(tx10*tx10 + ty10*ty10)

        IF (mag10 <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            tx10 = x10 - b%x(ic+(nfit-1)*iflag,jc)
            ty10 = y10 - b%y(ic+(nfit-1)*iflag,jc)
            mag10 = SQRT(tx10*tx10 + ty10*ty10)
        END IF

        IF (mag10 <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            tx10 = tx0
            ty10 = ty0
            mag10 = 1.0D0
        ELSE
            tx10 = tx10/mag10
            ty10 = ty10/mag10
        END IF

        ! Replace only points 1..9.  Point 0 is the shared corner,
        DO p = 1, nfit-1

            t = s(p)/total

            h00 =  2.0D0*t**3 - 3.0D0*t**2 + 1.0D0
            h10 =        t**3 - 2.0D0*t**2 + t
            h01 = -2.0D0*t**3 + 3.0D0*t**2
            h11 =        t**3 -       t**2

            b%x(ic+p*iflag,jc) = h00*x0 + h10*total*tx0 + &
                                  h01*x10 + h11*total*tx10

            b%y(ic+p*iflag,jc) = h00*y0 + h10*total*ty0 + &
                                  h01*y10 + h11*total*ty10

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(s)

    END SUBROUTINE fit_ten_point_corner_spline_i

    SUBROUTINE fit_ten_point_corner_spline_j(b, ic, jc, jflag)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ic, jc, jflag

        INTEGER :: nj, nfit, p
        INTEGER :: j0, jend, j11
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: s(:)
        REAL(8) :: total, t, h00, h10, h01, h11
        REAL(8) :: x0, y0, x10, y10
        REAL(8) :: tx0, ty0, tx10, ty10, mag0, mag10

        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        nfit = 10

        IF (jc + nfit*jflag < 1 .OR. jc + nfit*jflag > nj) RETURN
        IF (jc + (nfit+1)*jflag < 1 .OR. jc + (nfit+1)*jflag > nj) RETURN

        ALLOCATE(s(0:nfit))

        s(0) = 0.0D0

        DO p = 1, nfit
            s(p) = s(p-1) + SQRT( &
                (b%x(ic,jc+p*jflag) - b%x(ic,jc+(p-1)*jflag))**2 + &
                (b%y(ic,jc+p*jflag) - b%y(ic,jc+(p-1)*jflag))**2 )
        END DO

        total = s(nfit)

        IF (total <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            DEALLOCATE(s)
            RETURN
        END IF

        j0   = jc
        jend = jc + nfit*jflag
        j11  = jc + (nfit+1)*jflag

        x0  = b%x(ic,j0)
        y0  = b%y(ic,j0)
        x10 = b%x(ic,jend)
        y10 = b%y(ic,jend)

        tx0 = b%x(ic,jc+2*jflag) - b%x(ic,jc)
        ty0 = b%y(ic,jc+2*jflag) - b%y(ic,jc)

        mag0 = SQRT(tx0*tx0 + ty0*ty0)

        IF (mag0 <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            tx0 = x10 - x0
            ty0 = y10 - y0
            mag0 = SQRT(tx0*tx0 + ty0*ty0)
        END IF

        IF (mag0 <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            DEALLOCATE(s)
            RETURN
        END IF

        tx0 = tx0/mag0
        ty0 = ty0/mag0

        tx10 = b%x(ic,j11) - b%x(ic,jend)
        ty10 = b%y(ic,j11) - b%y(ic,jend)

        mag10 = SQRT(tx10*tx10 + ty10*ty10)

        IF (mag10 <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            tx10 = x10 - b%x(ic,jc+(nfit-1)*jflag)
            ty10 = y10 - b%y(ic,jc+(nfit-1)*jflag)
            mag10 = SQRT(tx10*tx10 + ty10*ty10)
        END IF

        IF (mag10 <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            tx10 = tx0
            ty10 = ty0
            mag10 = 1.0D0
        ELSE
            tx10 = tx10/mag10
            ty10 = ty10/mag10
        END IF

        DO p = 1, nfit-1

            t = s(p)/total

            h00 =  2.0D0*t**3 - 3.0D0*t**2 + 1.0D0
            h10 =        t**3 - 2.0D0*t**2 + t
            h01 = -2.0D0*t**3 + 3.0D0*t**2
            h11 =        t**3 -       t**2

            b%x(ic,jc+p*jflag) = h00*x0 + h10*total*tx0 + &
                                  h01*x10 + h11*total*tx10

            b%y(ic,jc+p*jflag) = h00*y0 + h10*total*ty0 + &
                                  h01*y10 + h11*total*ty10

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(s)

    END SUBROUTINE fit_ten_point_corner_spline_j

    SUBROUTINE corner_patch_our_topology(blk, pitch, cor_fac, apply_patch)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: cor_fac
        LOGICAL, INTENT(IN) :: apply_patch

        TYPE(corner_patch_type) :: corner(4)

        CALL initialise_corner_patch_groups_our_topology(blk, pitch, corner)
        CALL corner_shrink_patch(blk, corner, cor_fac, apply_patch)

    END SUBROUTINE corner_patch_our_topology

    SUBROUTINE initialise_corner_patch_groups_our_topology(blk, pitch, corner)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        TYPE(corner_patch_type), INTENT(OUT) :: corner(4)

        INTEGER :: ni1, nj1, ni2, nj2, ni3, nj3, ni4, nj4
        INTEGER :: ni5, nj5, ni6, nj6, ni7, nj7, ni8, nj8
        INTEGER :: ni9, nj9
        INTEGER :: n

        DO n = 1, 4
            corner(n)%Nb = 0
            corner(n)%block = 0
            corner(n)%i = 0
            corner(n)%j = 0
            corner(n)%yoff = 0.0D0
        END DO

        ni1 = SIZE(blk(1)%x,1); nj1 = SIZE(blk(1)%x,2)
        ni2 = SIZE(blk(2)%x,1); nj2 = SIZE(blk(2)%x,2)
        ni3 = SIZE(blk(3)%x,1); nj3 = SIZE(blk(3)%x,2)
        ni4 = SIZE(blk(4)%x,1); nj4 = SIZE(blk(4)%x,2)

        ni5 = SIZE(blk(5)%x,1); nj5 = SIZE(blk(5)%x,2)
        ni6 = SIZE(blk(6)%x,1); nj6 = SIZE(blk(6)%x,2)
        ni7 = SIZE(blk(7)%x,1); nj7 = SIZE(blk(7)%x,2)
        ni8 = SIZE(blk(8)%x,1); nj8 = SIZE(blk(8)%x,2)

        ni9 = SIZE(blk(9)%x,1); nj9 = SIZE(blk(9)%x,2)

        ! Four main multi-block junctions around the O-grid/passsage.
        ! These are based on OUR block numbering, not the Python meshing_iw
        ! Leading/top periodic corner:
        ! Blocks 5,6,1 meet physically; Block 9 is the periodic copy of 6.
        corner(1)%Nb = 4
        corner(1)%block(1:4) = (/ 5, 6, 1, 9 /)
        corner(1)%i(1:4)     = (/ ni5, 1, ni1, 1 /)
        corner(1)%j(1:4)     = (/ nj5, nj6, nj1, 1 /)
        corner(1)%yoff(1:4)  = (/ 0.0D0, 0.0D0, 0.0D0, pitch /)

        ! Leading/bottom corner:
        ! Blocks 8,5,1,2 meet; Block 9 is the periodic return-side copy.
        corner(2)%Nb = 5
        corner(2)%block(1:5) = (/ 8, 5, 1, 2, 9 /)
        corner(2)%i(1:5)     = (/ ni8, 1, ni1, ni2, 1 /)
        corner(2)%j(1:5)     = (/ nj8, nj5, 1, nj2, nj9 /)
        corner(2)%yoff(1:5)  = (/ 0.0D0, 0.0D0, 0.0D0, 0.0D0, 0.0D0 /)

        ! Trailing/top periodic corner:
        ! Blocks 6,7,3 meet physically; Block 9 is the periodic copy of 6.
        corner(3)%Nb = 4
        corner(3)%block(1:4) = (/ 6, 7, 3, 9 /)
        corner(3)%i(1:4)     = (/ ni6, 1, 1, ni9 /)
        corner(3)%j(1:4)     = (/ nj6, nj7, nj3, 1 /)
        corner(3)%yoff(1:4)  = (/ 0.0D0, 0.0D0, 0.0D0, pitch /)

        ! Trailing/bottom corner:
        ! Blocks 7,8,3,4 meet; Block 9 is the periodic return-side copy.
        corner(4)%Nb = 5
        corner(4)%block(1:5) = (/ 7, 8, 3, 4, 9 /)
        corner(4)%i(1:5)     = (/ ni7, 1, 1, 1, ni9 /)
        corner(4)%j(1:5)     = (/ nj7, nj8, 1, nj4, nj9 /)
        corner(4)%yoff(1:5)  = (/ 0.0D0, 0.0D0, 0.0D0, 0.0D0, 0.0D0 /)

        IF (pitch < -1.0D300) PRINT *, pitch

    END SUBROUTINE initialise_corner_patch_groups_our_topology

    SUBROUTINE corner_shrink_patch(blk, corner, cor_fac, apply_patch)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        TYPE(corner_patch_type), INTENT(IN) :: corner(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: cor_fac
        LOGICAL, INTENT(IN) :: apply_patch

        INTEGER :: ncorner, n, m, ib
        INTEGER :: ic, jc, ni, nj
        REAL(8) :: xsum, ysum, xx, yy
        INTEGER :: nic
        INTEGER :: istart, jstart
        INTEGER :: iflag, jflag
        INTEGER :: ii, jj

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: fc(:,:), f(:)

        ncorner = SIZE(corner)

        DO n = 1, ncorner

            IF (corner(n)%Nb <= 0) CYCLE

            xsum = 0.0D0
            ysum = 0.0D0

            ! Average the shared corner in the common pitch frame.
            DO m = 1, corner(n)%Nb

                ib = corner(n)%block(m)
                ic = corner(n)%i(m)
                jc = corner(n)%j(m)

                ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1)
                nj = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,2)

                IF (ic /= 1) ic = ni
                IF (jc /= 1) jc = nj

                xsum = xsum + blk(ib)%x(ic,jc)
                ysum = ysum + blk(ib)%y(ic,jc) + corner(n)%yoff(m)

            END DO

            xx = xsum/REAL(corner(n)%Nb,8)
            yy = ysum/REAL(corner(n)%Nb,8)

            DO m = 1, corner(n)%Nb

                ib = corner(n)%block(m)
                ic = corner(n)%i(m)
                jc = corner(n)%j(m)

                ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1)
                nj = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,2)

                IF (ic /= 1) ic = ni
                IF (jc /= 1) jc = nj

                ! Always snap the actual shared corner point.
                blk(ib)%x(ic,jc) = xx
                blk(ib)%y(ic,jc) = yy - corner(n)%yoff(m)

                ! In the main optimisation loop we only snap corners.
                ! cumulatively collapses the first few cells into the
                IF (.NOT. apply_patch) CYCLE

                nic = 16
                IF (nic >= ni) nic = ni - 1
                IF (nic >= nj) nic = nj - 1
                IF (nic < 2) CYCLE

                ALLOCATE(fc(nic,nic))
                fc = 1.0D0

                ! Only 5-block junctions get a tanh corner-shrink patch.
                ! Other corners just have their corner node snapped.
                IF (corner(n)%Nb == 5) THEN

                    ALLOCATE(f(nic))

                    DO ii = 1, nic
                        f(ii) = REAL(ii,8)/REAL(nic,8)
                        f(ii) = TANH(f(ii)*REAL(nic,8)*cor_fac)
                    END DO

                    fc(:,1) = f(:)
                    fc(1,:) = f(:)

                    DO jj = 2, nic-1
                        DO ii = 2, nic-1
                            fc(ii,jj) = 0.25D0*(fc(ii+1,jj) + fc(ii-1,jj) + &
                                                 fc(ii,jj+1) + fc(ii,jj-1))
                        END DO
                    END DO

                    DEALLOCATE(f)

                END IF

                istart = 1
                jstart = 1
                iflag  = 1
                jflag  = 1

                IF (corner(n)%i(m) /= 1) THEN
                    istart = ni
                    iflag  = -1
                END IF

                IF (corner(n)%j(m) /= 1) THEN
                    jstart = nj
                    jflag  = -1
                END IF

                DO jj = 1, nic-1
                    DO ii = 1, nic-1

                        blk(ib)%x(istart + ii*iflag, jstart + jj*jflag) = xx + &
                            (blk(ib)%x(istart + ii*iflag, jstart + jj*jflag) - xx)*fc(ii+1,jj+1)

                        blk(ib)%y(istart + ii*iflag, jstart + jj*jflag) = &
                            (yy - corner(n)%yoff(m)) + &
                            (blk(ib)%y(istart + ii*iflag, jstart + jj*jflag) - &
                            (yy - corner(n)%yoff(m)))*fc(ii+1,jj+1)

                    END DO
                END DO

                DEALLOCATE(fc)

            END DO

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE corner_shrink_patch

    SUBROUTINE order_corner_blocks_by_sector_angle(blk, c, xx, yy, order, midang)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        TYPE(corner_patch_type), INTENT(IN) :: c
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xx, yy
        INTEGER, INTENT(OUT) :: order(6)
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: midang(6)

        INTEGER :: m, nswap, ib, ni, nj, ic, jc
        INTEGER :: istart, jstart, iflag, jflag
        REAL(8) :: vi_x, vi_y, vj_x, vj_y, cx, cy
        INTEGER :: oi
        REAL(8) :: oa

        DO m = 1, 6
            order(m) = m
            midang(m) = 0.0D0
        END DO

        DO m = 1, c%Nb

            ib = c%block(m)
            ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1)
            nj = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,2)

            ic = c%i(m); jc = c%j(m)
            IF (ic /= 1) ic = ni
            IF (jc /= 1) jc = nj

            istart = 1; iflag = 1
            jstart = 1; jflag = 1

            IF (c%i(m) /= 1) THEN
                istart = ni
                iflag = -1
            END IF
            IF (c%j(m) /= 1) THEN
                jstart = nj
                jflag = -1
            END IF

            vi_x = blk(ib)%x(istart+iflag,jstart) - xx
            vi_y = blk(ib)%y(istart+iflag,jstart) + c%yoff(m) - yy
            vj_x = blk(ib)%x(istart,jstart+jflag) - xx
            vj_y = blk(ib)%y(istart,jstart+jflag) + c%yoff(m) - yy

            cx = vi_x + vj_x
            cy = vi_y + vj_y
            IF (SQRT(cx*cx + cy*cy) < 1.0D-14) THEN
                cx = vi_x
                cy = vi_y
            END IF

            midang(m) = ATAN2(cy, cx)
            order(m) = m

        END DO

        DO nswap = 1, c%Nb
            DO m = 1, c%Nb-1
                IF (midang(order(m+1)) < midang(order(m))) THEN
                    oi = order(m)
                    order(m) = order(m+1)
                    order(m+1) = oi
                END IF
            END DO
        END DO

        IF (c%Nb > 0) THEN
            DO m = 1, c%Nb
                oa = midang(order(1)) + 2.0D0*3.1415926535897932384626433832795D0*REAL(m-1,8)/REAL(c%Nb,8)
            END DO
        END IF

    END SUBROUTINE order_corner_blocks_by_sector_angle

    SUBROUTINE nearest_ordered_sector_angle(order, midang, nb, mblock, angle_out)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: order(6), nb, mblock
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: midang(6)
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: angle_out

        INTEGER :: p, pos
        REAL(8) :: base

        pos = 1
        DO p = 1, nb
            IF (order(p) == mblock) pos = p
        END DO

        base = midang(order(1))
        angle_out = base + 2.0D0*3.1415926535897932384626433832795D0*REAL(pos-1,8)/REAL(nb,8)

        ! Shift by whole revolutions so the target centre is closest to the
        ! current sector centre.
        DO WHILE (angle_out - midang(mblock) > 3.1415926535897932384626433832795D0)
            angle_out = angle_out - 2.0D0*3.1415926535897932384626433832795D0
        END DO
        DO WHILE (angle_out - midang(mblock) < -3.1415926535897932384626433832795D0)
            angle_out = angle_out + 2.0D0*3.1415926535897932384626433832795D0
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE nearest_ordered_sector_angle

    REAL(8) FUNCTION angular_distance(a, b)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: a, b
        REAL(8) :: d

        d = a - b
        DO WHILE (d > 3.1415926535897932384626433832795D0)
            d = d - 2.0D0*3.1415926535897932384626433832795D0
        END DO
        DO WHILE (d < -3.1415926535897932384626433832795D0)
            d = d + 2.0D0*3.1415926535897932384626433832795D0
        END DO

        angular_distance = ABS(d)

    END FUNCTION angular_distance

    REAL(8) FUNCTION local_edge_distance_i(b, istart, jstart, iflag, jflag, ii)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: istart, jstart, iflag, jflag, ii
        INTEGER :: suppress_unused_jflag
        INTEGER :: p
        REAL(8) :: dx, dy

        suppress_unused_jflag = jflag

        local_edge_distance_i = 0.0D0
        IF (ii <= 0) RETURN

        DO p = 1, ii
            dx = b%x(istart + p*iflag, jstart) - b%x(istart + (p-1)*iflag, jstart)
            dy = b%y(istart + p*iflag, jstart) - b%y(istart + (p-1)*iflag, jstart)
            local_edge_distance_i = local_edge_distance_i + SQRT(dx*dx + dy*dy)
        END DO

    END FUNCTION local_edge_distance_i

    REAL(8) FUNCTION local_edge_distance_j(b, istart, jstart, iflag, jflag, jj)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: istart, jstart, iflag, jflag, jj
        INTEGER :: suppress_unused_iflag
        INTEGER :: p
        REAL(8) :: dx, dy

        suppress_unused_iflag = iflag

        local_edge_distance_j = 0.0D0
        IF (jj <= 0) RETURN

        DO p = 1, jj
            dx = b%x(istart, jstart + p*jflag) - b%x(istart, jstart + (p-1)*jflag)
            dy = b%y(istart, jstart + p*jflag) - b%y(istart, jstart + (p-1)*jflag)
            local_edge_distance_j = local_edge_distance_j + SQRT(dx*dx + dy*dy)
        END DO

    END FUNCTION local_edge_distance_j

    SUBROUTINE miw_snap_all_multiblock_corners(blk, pitch)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch

        INTEGER :: ni1, nj1, ni2, nj2, ni3, nj3, ni4, nj4
        INTEGER :: ni5, nj5, ni6, nj6, ni7, nj7, ni8, nj8
        INTEGER :: ni9, nj9

        ni1 = SIZE(blk(1)%x,1); nj1 = SIZE(blk(1)%x,2)
        ni2 = SIZE(blk(2)%x,1); nj2 = SIZE(blk(2)%x,2)
        ni3 = SIZE(blk(3)%x,1); nj3 = SIZE(blk(3)%x,2)
        ni4 = SIZE(blk(4)%x,1); nj4 = SIZE(blk(4)%x,2)

        ni5 = SIZE(blk(5)%x,1); nj5 = SIZE(blk(5)%x,2)
        ni6 = SIZE(blk(6)%x,1); nj6 = SIZE(blk(6)%x,2)
        ni7 = SIZE(blk(7)%x,1); nj7 = SIZE(blk(7)%x,2)
        ni8 = SIZE(blk(8)%x,1); nj8 = SIZE(blk(8)%x,2)

        ni9 = SIZE(blk(9)%x,1); nj9 = SIZE(blk(9)%x,2)

        ! Exact movable corner clusters.  These remove the small corner
        ! cracks that remain after line-wise interface averaging.
        CALL snap2(blk(1)%x(1,1),   blk(1)%y(1,1),   0.0D0, &
                   blk(2)%x(1,nj2), blk(2)%y(1,nj2), 0.0D0)

        CALL snap2(blk(1)%x(1,nj1), blk(1)%y(1,nj1), 0.0D0, &
                   blk(2)%x(1,1),   blk(2)%y(1,1),   pitch)

        CALL snap2(blk(3)%x(ni3,1),   blk(3)%y(ni3,1),   0.0D0, &
                   blk(4)%x(ni4,nj4), blk(4)%y(ni4,nj4), 0.0D0)

        CALL snap2(blk(3)%x(ni3,nj3), blk(3)%y(ni3,nj3), 0.0D0, &
                   blk(4)%x(ni4,1),   blk(4)%y(ni4,1),   pitch)

        CALL snap3(blk(5)%x(ni5,nj5), blk(5)%y(ni5,nj5), 0.0D0, &
                   blk(6)%x(1,nj6),   blk(6)%y(1,nj6),   0.0D0, &
                   blk(1)%x(ni1,nj1), blk(1)%y(ni1,nj1), 0.0D0)

        CALL snap4(blk(8)%x(ni8,nj8), blk(8)%y(ni8,nj8), 0.0D0, &
                   blk(5)%x(1,nj5),   blk(5)%y(1,nj5),   0.0D0, &
                   blk(1)%x(ni1,1),   blk(1)%y(ni1,1),   0.0D0, &
                   blk(2)%x(ni2,nj2), blk(2)%y(ni2,nj2), 0.0D0)

        CALL snap3(blk(6)%x(ni6,nj6), blk(6)%y(ni6,nj6), 0.0D0, &
                   blk(7)%x(1,nj7),   blk(7)%y(1,nj7),   0.0D0, &
                   blk(3)%x(1,nj3),   blk(3)%y(1,nj3),   0.0D0)

        CALL snap4(blk(7)%x(ni7,nj7), blk(7)%y(ni7,nj7), 0.0D0, &
                   blk(8)%x(1,nj8),   blk(8)%y(1,nj8),   0.0D0, &
                   blk(3)%x(1,1),     blk(3)%y(1,1),     0.0D0, &
                   blk(4)%x(1,nj4),   blk(4)%y(1,nj4),   0.0D0)

        CALL snap2(blk(6)%x(1,nj6),   blk(6)%y(1,nj6),   -pitch, &
                   blk(9)%x(1,1),     blk(9)%y(1,1),     0.0D0)

        CALL snap2(blk(6)%x(ni6,nj6), blk(6)%y(ni6,nj6), -pitch, &
                   blk(9)%x(ni9,1),   blk(9)%y(ni9,1),   0.0D0)

        CALL snap2(blk(8)%x(ni8,nj8), blk(8)%y(ni8,nj8), 0.0D0, &
                   blk(9)%x(1,nj9),   blk(9)%y(1,nj9),   0.0D0)

        CALL snap2(blk(8)%x(1,nj8),   blk(8)%y(1,nj8),   0.0D0, &
                   blk(9)%x(ni9,nj9), blk(9)%y(ni9,nj9), 0.0D0)

    END SUBROUTINE miw_snap_all_multiblock_corners

    SUBROUTINE snap2(x1, y1, off1, x2, y2, off2)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(INOUT) :: x1, y1, x2, y2
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: off1, off2

        REAL(8) :: xa, ya

        xa = 0.5D0*(x1 + x2)
        ya = 0.5D0*((y1 + off1) + (y2 + off2))

        x1 = xa
        y1 = ya - off1

        x2 = xa
        y2 = ya - off2

    END SUBROUTINE snap2

    SUBROUTINE snap3(x1, y1, off1, x2, y2, off2, x3, y3, off3)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(INOUT) :: x1, y1, x2, y2, x3, y3
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: off1, off2, off3

        REAL(8) :: xa, ya

        xa = (x1 + x2 + x3)/3.0D0
        ya = ((y1 + off1) + (y2 + off2) + (y3 + off3))/3.0D0

        x1 = xa
        y1 = ya - off1

        x2 = xa
        y2 = ya - off2

        x3 = xa
        y3 = ya - off3

    END SUBROUTINE snap3

    SUBROUTINE snap4(x1, y1, off1, x2, y2, off2, x3, y3, off3, x4, y4, off4)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(INOUT) :: x1, y1, x2, y2, x3, y3, x4, y4
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: off1, off2, off3, off4

        REAL(8) :: xa, ya

        xa = 0.25D0*(x1 + x2 + x3 + x4)
        ya = 0.25D0*((y1 + off1) + (y2 + off2) + (y3 + off3) + (y4 + off4))

        x1 = xa
        y1 = ya - off1

        x2 = xa
        y2 = ya - off2

        x3 = xa
        y3 = ya - off3

        x4 = xa
        y4 = ya - off4

    END SUBROUTINE snap4

    SUBROUTINE enforce_miw_hard_boundaries(blk)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)

    ! Boundary handling.
        IF (SIZE(blk) < 1) PRINT *, SIZE(blk)

    END SUBROUTINE enforce_miw_hard_boundaries

    SUBROUTINE elliptic_poisson_any_block_free_edges( &
        blk, blk_old, ib, pitch, niter, relax &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk_old(:)

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ib
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: niter
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax

        INTEGER :: ni, nj
        INTEGER :: iter, i, j

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xext(:,:), yext(:,:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: gimx(:), gimy(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: gipx(:), gipy(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: gjmx(:), gjmy(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: gjpx(:), gjpy(:)

        REAL(8) :: xs, ys

        ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x, 1)
        nj = SIZE(blk(ib)%x, 2)

        ALLOCATE(xext(0:ni+1,0:nj+1))
        ALLOCATE(yext(0:ni+1,0:nj+1))

        ALLOCATE(gimx(nj), gimy(nj))
        ALLOCATE(gipx(nj), gipy(nj))
        ALLOCATE(gjmx(ni), gjmy(ni))
        ALLOCATE(gjpx(ni), gjpy(ni))

        CALL get_all_block_ghost_lines( &
            blk_old, ib, pitch, gimx, gimy, gipx, gipy, &
            gjmx, gjmy, gjpx, gjpy &
        )

        DO iter = 1, niter

            xext = 0.0D0
            yext = 0.0D0

            DO j = 1, nj
                DO i = 1, ni
                    xext(i,j) = blk(ib)%x(i,j)
                    yext(i,j) = blk(ib)%y(i,j)
                END DO
            END DO

            DO j = 1, nj
                xext(0,j) = gimx(j)
                yext(0,j) = gimy(j)

                xext(ni+1,j) = gipx(j)
                yext(ni+1,j) = gipy(j)
            END DO

            DO i = 1, ni
                xext(i,0) = gjmx(i)
                yext(i,0) = gjmy(i)

                xext(i,nj+1) = gjpx(i)
                yext(i,nj+1) = gjpy(i)
            END DO

            ! Corner ghost values.
            xext(0,0) = 0.5D0*(gimx(1) + gjmx(1))
            yext(0,0) = 0.5D0*(gimy(1) + gjmy(1))

            xext(ni+1,0) = 0.5D0*(gipx(1) + gjmx(ni))
            yext(ni+1,0) = 0.5D0*(gipy(1) + gjmy(ni))

            xext(0,nj+1) = 0.5D0*(gimx(nj) + gjpx(1))
            yext(0,nj+1) = 0.5D0*(gimy(nj) + gjpy(1))

            xext(ni+1,nj+1) = 0.5D0*(gipx(nj) + gjpx(ni))
            yext(ni+1,nj+1) = 0.5D0*(gipy(nj) + gjpy(ni))

            DO j = 1, nj
                DO i = 1, ni

                    IF (is_hard_boundary_point(ib, i, j, ni, nj)) CYCLE

                    CALL elliptic_poisson_ext_point_update( &
                        xext, yext, ni, nj, i, j, xs, ys &
                    )

                    CALL apply_maximum_point_movement( &
                        xext, yext, ni, nj, i, j, xs, ys, relax, &
                        max_point_movement_fraction, blk(ib)%x(i,j), blk(ib)%y(i,j) &
                    )

                END DO
            END DO

            ! Re-impose hard boundary points exactly.
            DO j = 1, nj
                DO i = 1, ni
                    IF (is_hard_boundary_point(ib, i, j, ni, nj)) THEN
                        blk(ib)%x(i,j) = xext(i,j)
                        blk(ib)%y(i,j) = yext(i,j)
                    END IF
                END DO
            END DO

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(xext, yext)
        DEALLOCATE(gimx, gimy, gipx, gipy, gjmx, gjmy, gjpx, gjpy)

    END SUBROUTINE elliptic_poisson_any_block_free_edges

    SUBROUTINE apply_maximum_point_movement( &
        xext, yext, ni, nj, i, j, xs, ys, relax, move_frac, xout, yout &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ni, nj, i, j
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xext(0:ni+1,0:nj+1), yext(0:ni+1,0:nj+1)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xs, ys
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: relax
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: move_frac
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: xout, yout

        REAL(8) :: xold, yold
        REAL(8) :: xtry, ytry
        REAL(8) :: dx, dy, dmove
        REAL(8) :: hloc, max_move

        xold = xext(i,j)
        yold = yext(i,j)

        xtry = (1.0D0-relax)*xold + relax*xs
        ytry = (1.0D0-relax)*yold + relax*ys

        dx = xtry - xold
        dy = ytry - yold
        dmove = SQRT(dx*dx + dy*dy)

        CALL local_point_spacing_radius( &
            xext, yext, ni, nj, i, j, hloc &
        )

        max_move = move_frac*hloc

        IF (max_move <= 1.0D-14 .OR. dmove <= max_move) THEN
            xout = xtry
            yout = ytry
        ELSE
            xout = xold + dx*max_move/dmove
            yout = yold + dy*max_move/dmove
        END IF

    END SUBROUTINE apply_maximum_point_movement

    SUBROUTINE local_point_spacing_radius(xext, yext, ni, nj, i, j, hloc)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ni, nj, i, j
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xext(0:ni+1,0:nj+1), yext(0:ni+1,0:nj+1)
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: hloc

        INTEGER :: di(8), dj(8)
        INTEGER :: k, ii, jj
        REAL(8) :: d, dx, dy

        di = (/ -1, 1, 0, 0, -1, -1, 1, 1 /)
        dj = (/ 0, 0, -1, 1, -1, 1, -1, 1 /)

        hloc = 1.0D300

        DO k = 1, 8
            ii = i + di(k)
            jj = j + dj(k)

            IF (ii < 0 .OR. ii > ni+1) CYCLE
            IF (jj < 0 .OR. jj > nj+1) CYCLE

            dx = xext(ii,jj) - xext(i,j)
            dy = yext(ii,jj) - yext(i,j)
            d = SQRT(dx*dx + dy*dy)

            IF (d > 1.0D-14) hloc = MIN(hloc, d)
        END DO

        IF (hloc > 0.5D300) hloc = 0.0D0

    END SUBROUTINE local_point_spacing_radius

    LOGICAL FUNCTION is_hard_boundary_point(ib, i, j, ni, nj)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ib, i, j, ni, nj
        INTEGER :: suppress_unused_nj

        suppress_unused_nj = nj

        is_hard_boundary_point = .FALSE.

        ! Blade wall for the four blade blocks.
        IF (ib >= 5 .AND. ib <= 8) THEN
            IF (j == 1) is_hard_boundary_point = .TRUE.
        END IF

        ! Far-left inlet boundary.
        IF (ib == 1 .OR. ib == 2) THEN
            IF (i == 1) is_hard_boundary_point = .TRUE.
        END IF

        ! Far-right outlet boundary.
        IF (ib == 3 .OR. ib == 4) THEN
            IF (i == ni) is_hard_boundary_point = .TRUE.
        END IF

    END FUNCTION is_hard_boundary_point

    SUBROUTINE enforce_blade_wall_profile_and_spacing_jm( &
        blk, xprof, yprof, npts, ywall &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xprof(:), yprof(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: npts
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: ywall

        INTEGER :: ib

        DO ib = 5, 8
            CALL project_wall_jm_to_profile_ordered(blk(ib), xprof, yprof, npts)
            CALL redistribute_wall_normal_spacing_jm(blk(ib), ywall)
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE enforce_blade_wall_profile_and_spacing_jm

    SUBROUTINE project_wall_jm_to_profile_ordered(b, xprof, yprof, npts)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xprof(:), yprof(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: npts

        INTEGER :: ni, nj
        INTEGER :: i
        INTEGER :: nprof
        INTEGER :: kprev, kbest
        INTEGER :: kwin
        REAL(8) :: lam

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        IF (ni < 1 .OR. nj < 2) RETURN
        IF (npts < 2) RETURN

        nprof = npts
        IF ((xprof(1)-xprof(npts))**2 + (yprof(1)-yprof(npts))**2 < 1.0D-24) THEN
            nprof = npts - 1
        END IF

        IF (nprof < 2) RETURN

        ! meshing_iw uses a secondary distance to the first off-wall line
        ! to keep the selected wall profile side/order stable.
        lam = 0.25D0
        kwin = MAX(50, nprof/20)

        CALL nearest_profile_index_with_offset( &
            xprof, yprof, nprof, b%x(1,1), b%y(1,1), &
            b%x(1,2), b%y(1,2), lam, kbest &
        )

        kprev = kbest
        b%x(1,1) = xprof(kbest)
        b%y(1,1) = yprof(kbest)

        DO i = 2, ni
            CALL nearest_profile_index_window_with_offset( &
                xprof, yprof, nprof, b%x(i,1), b%y(i,1), &
                b%x(i,2), b%y(i,2), lam, kprev, kwin, kbest &
            )

            kprev = kbest
            b%x(i,1) = xprof(kbest)
            b%y(i,1) = yprof(kbest)
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE project_wall_jm_to_profile_ordered

    SUBROUTINE nearest_profile_index_with_offset( &
        xp, yp, np, xw, yw, xi, yi, lam, idx &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: np
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xp(np), yp(np)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xw, yw, xi, yi, lam
        INTEGER, INTENT(OUT) :: idx

        INTEGER :: k
        REAL(8) :: d, dmin

        idx = 1
        dmin = 1.0D300

        DO k = 1, np
            d = (xw-xp(k))**2 + (yw-yp(k))**2 + &
                lam*((xi-xp(k))**2 + (yi-yp(k))**2)

            IF (d < dmin) THEN
                dmin = d
                idx = k
            END IF
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE nearest_profile_index_with_offset

    SUBROUTINE nearest_profile_index_window_with_offset( &
        xp, yp, np, xw, yw, xi, yi, lam, kprev, kwin, idx &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: np
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xp(np), yp(np)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xw, yw, xi, yi, lam
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: kprev, kwin
        INTEGER, INTENT(OUT) :: idx

        INTEGER :: jj, k
        REAL(8) :: d, dmin

        idx = kprev
        dmin = 1.0D300

        DO jj = -kwin, kwin
            k = MODULO(kprev + jj - 1, np) + 1

            d = (xw-xp(k))**2 + (yw-yp(k))**2 + &
                lam*((xi-xp(k))**2 + (yi-yp(k))**2)

            IF (d < dmin) THEN
                dmin = d
                idx = k
            END IF
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE nearest_profile_index_window_with_offset

    SUBROUTINE diagnose_all_connected_interface_mismatch(blk, pitch)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch

        REAL(8) :: max_gap

        CALL validate_full_interface_matching( &
            blk, next_block, next_patch, pitch, "connected-interface diagnostic", max_gap &
        )

        PRINT *, "Maximum connected-interface gap = ", max_gap

    END SUBROUTINE diagnose_all_connected_interface_mismatch

    SUBROUTINE validate_full_interface_matching(blk, nb, np, pitch, label, max_gap)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nb(9,4), np(9,4)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        CHARACTER(LEN=*), INTENT(IN) :: label
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: max_gap

        INTEGER :: ib, side, b2, p2
        INTEGER :: n1, n2, k, k2
        LOGICAL :: rev
        REAL(8) :: yoff, d, local_max
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x1(:), y1(:), x2(:), y2(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x2r(:), y2r(:)

        max_gap = 0.0D0

        DO ib = 1, 9
            DO side = 1, 4

                b2 = nb(ib,side)
                p2 = np(ib,side)

                IF (b2 <= 0 .OR. p2 <= 0) CYCLE
                IF (b2 < ib) CYCLE

                CALL get_edge_line(blk(ib), side, x1, y1)
                CALL get_edge_line(blk(b2), p2, x2, y2)

                n1 = SIZE(x1)
                n2 = SIZE(x2)

                ALLOCATE(x2r(n1), y2r(n1))
                CALL resample_line(x2, y2, n2, x2r, y2r, n1)

                CALL periodic_match_endpoints( &
                    x1(1), y1(1), x1(n1), y1(n1), &
                    x2r(1), y2r(1), x2r(n1), y2r(n1), &
                    pitch, rev, yoff &
                )

                local_max = 0.0D0

                DO k = 1, n1
                    IF (rev) THEN
                        k2 = n1 - k + 1
                    ELSE
                        k2 = k
                    END IF

                    d = SQRT((x1(k)-x2r(k2))**2 + &
                             (y1(k)-(y2r(k2)+yoff))**2)

                    local_max = MAX(local_max, d)
                END DO

                max_gap = MAX(max_gap, local_max)

                IF (local_max > 1.0D-6) THEN
                    PRINT *, "Interface gap warning [", TRIM(label), "]: ", &
                             ib, side, " -> ", b2, p2, " gap = ", local_max
                END IF

                DEALLOCATE(x1, y1, x2, y2, x2r, y2r)

            END DO
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE validate_full_interface_matching

    SUBROUTINE redistribute_wall_normal_spacing_jm(b, ywall)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: ywall

        INTEGER :: ni, nj
        INTEGER :: i, j
        REAL(8) :: total_len
        REAL(8) :: r
        REAL(8) :: target_first_spacing

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xtmp(:), ytmp(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: snorm(:), snew(:), frac(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xnew(:), ynew(:)

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        IF (ni < 2 .OR. nj < 3) RETURN

        ALLOCATE(xtmp(nj), ytmp(nj))
        ALLOCATE(snorm(nj), snew(nj), frac(nj))
        ALLOCATE(xnew(nj), ynew(nj))

        DO i = 1, ni

            ! Current wall-normal grid line for fixed i.
            ! i=1 and i=ni are blade-block interface edges.
            DO j = 1, nj
                xtmp(j) = b%x(i,j)
                ytmp(j) = b%y(i,j)
            END DO

            CALL curve_length_1d(xtmp, ytmp, snorm)

            total_len = snorm(nj)

            IF (total_len <= 1.0D-14) CYCLE

            target_first_spacing = MAX(ywall, 1.0D-12*total_len)

            CALL fexpan_miw(total_len/target_first_spacing, nj, r)

            CALL spacing_miw(nj, r, frac)

            DO j = 1, nj
                snew(j) = total_len*frac(j)
            END DO

            CALL interp_1d_linear_many_local(snorm, xtmp, nj, snew, nj, xnew)
            CALL interp_1d_linear_many_local(snorm, ytmp, nj, snew, nj, ynew)

            ! Keep the wall and outer end exactly fixed, and only move
            ! intermediate points along the existing grid line.
            b%x(i,1)  = xtmp(1)
            b%y(i,1)  = ytmp(1)
            b%x(i,nj) = xtmp(nj)
            b%y(i,nj) = ytmp(nj)

            DO j = 2, nj-1
                b%x(i,j) = xnew(j)
                b%y(i,j) = ynew(j)
            END DO

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(xtmp, ytmp)
        DEALLOCATE(snorm, snew, frac)
        DEALLOCATE(xnew, ynew)

    END SUBROUTINE redistribute_wall_normal_spacing_jm

    SUBROUTINE curve_length_1d(x, y, s)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x(:), y(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: s(:)

        INTEGER :: n, i

        n = SIZE(x)

        s(1) = 0.0D0

        DO i = 2, n
            s(i) = s(i-1) + SQRT((x(i)-x(i-1))**2 + &
                                  (y(i)-y(i-1))**2)
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE curve_length_1d

    SUBROUTINE fexpan_miw(ratio, n, r)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: ratio
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: n
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: r

        INTEGER :: it
        REAL(8) :: lo, hi, mid
        REAL(8) :: f_lo, f_mid
        REAL(8) :: target

        ! Geometric expansion ratio for wall-normal spacing.
        IF (n <= 2) THEN
            r = 1.0D0
            RETURN
        END IF

        target = MAX(ratio, REAL(n-1,8))

        IF (ABS(target - REAL(n-1,8)) <= 1.0D-12) THEN
            r = 1.0D0
            RETURN
        END IF

        lo = 1.0D0
        hi = 1.05D0

        DO WHILE (geom_sum_ratio(hi, n) < target)
            hi = hi*1.25D0
            IF (hi > 100.0D0) EXIT
        END DO

        f_lo = geom_sum_ratio(lo, n) - target

        DO it = 1, 80
            mid = 0.5D0*(lo + hi)
            f_mid = geom_sum_ratio(mid, n) - target
            IF (ABS(f_mid) <= 1.0D-12) EXIT
            IF (f_lo*f_mid <= 0.0D0) THEN
                hi = mid
            ELSE
                lo = mid
                f_lo = f_mid
            END IF
        END DO

        r = 0.5D0*(lo + hi)

        ! becomes uniform rather than risking folded cells.
        IF (r < 1.0D0 .OR. r > 1.9D0) r = 1.0D0

    END SUBROUTINE fexpan_miw

    REAL(8) FUNCTION geom_sum_ratio(r, n)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: r
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: n

        INTEGER :: k
        REAL(8) :: term

        geom_sum_ratio = 1.0D0
        term = 1.0D0

        DO k = 2, n-1
            term = term*r
            geom_sum_ratio = geom_sum_ratio + term
        END DO

    END FUNCTION geom_sum_ratio

    SUBROUTINE spacing_miw(n, fx_expan, fx)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: n
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: fx_expan
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: fx(n)

        INTEGER :: i
        REAL(8) :: df

        IF (n <= 1) THEN
            IF (n == 1) fx(1) = 0.0D0
            RETURN
        END IF

        fx(1) = 0.0D0
        df = 1.0D0

        DO i = 2, n
            fx(i) = fx(i-1) + df
            df = df*fx_expan
        END DO

        IF (ABS(fx(n)) > 1.0D-14) THEN
            fx(:) = fx(:)/fx(n)
        END IF

        fx(1) = 0.0D0
        fx(n) = 1.0D0

    END SUBROUTINE spacing_miw

    SUBROUTINE interp_1d_linear_many_local(xsrc, ysrc, nsrc, xtgt, ntgt, ytgt)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nsrc, ntgt
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xsrc(nsrc), ysrc(nsrc)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xtgt(ntgt)
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: ytgt(ntgt)

        INTEGER :: i, k
        REAL(8) :: f, denom

        IF (nsrc <= 1) THEN
            ytgt(:) = ysrc(1)
            RETURN
        END IF

        k = 1

        DO i = 1, ntgt

            IF (xtgt(i) <= xsrc(1)) THEN
                ytgt(i) = ysrc(1)
                CYCLE
            END IF

            IF (xtgt(i) >= xsrc(nsrc)) THEN
                ytgt(i) = ysrc(nsrc)
                CYCLE
            END IF

            DO WHILE (k < nsrc-1 .AND. xsrc(k+1) < xtgt(i))
                k = k + 1
            END DO

            denom = xsrc(k+1) - xsrc(k)

            IF (ABS(denom) > 1.0D-14) THEN
                f = (xtgt(i) - xsrc(k))/denom
            ELSE
                f = 0.0D0
            END IF

            ytgt(i) = (1.0D0-f)*ysrc(k) + f*ysrc(k+1)

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE interp_1d_linear_many_local

    SUBROUTINE compute_global_min_jacobian(blk, nbcheck, min_jac)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nbcheck
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: min_jac

        INTEGER :: ib
        REAL(8) :: block_min

        min_jac = 1.0D300

        DO ib = 1, nbcheck
            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE
            CALL compute_block_min_signed_area(blk(ib), block_min)
            min_jac = MIN(min_jac, block_min)
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE compute_global_min_jacobian

    SUBROUTINE compute_block_min_signed_area(b, min_area)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: min_area

        INTEGER :: ni, nj
        INTEGER :: i, j
        REAL(8) :: x11, y11, x21, y21, x12, y12, x22, y22
        REAL(8) :: area

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)
        min_area = 1.0D300

        DO j = 1, nj-1
            DO i = 1, ni-1
                x11 = b%x(i,  j  ); y11 = b%y(i,  j  )
                x21 = b%x(i+1,j  ); y21 = b%y(i+1,j  )
                x12 = b%x(i,  j+1); y12 = b%y(i,  j+1)
                x22 = b%x(i+1,j+1); y22 = b%y(i+1,j+1)

                area = 0.5D0*( &
                    ((x21-x11)*(y22-y11) - (y21-y11)*(x22-x11)) + &
                    ((x22-x11)*(y12-y11) - (y22-y11)*(x12-x11)) )

                min_area = MIN(min_area, area)
            END DO
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE compute_block_min_signed_area

    SUBROUTINE validate_next_patch_topology(blk, nb, np, pitch)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nb(9,4), np(9,4)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch

        INTEGER :: ib, side, b2, p2
        INTEGER :: n1, n2
        LOGICAL :: rev
        REAL(8) :: yoff
        REAL(8) :: d1, d2, worst
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x1(:), y1(:), x2(:), y2(:)

        worst = 0.0D0

        PRINT *, "Checking next_block/next_patch topology..."

        DO ib = 1, 9
            DO side = 1, 4

                b2 = nb(ib,side)
                p2 = np(ib,side)

                IF (b2 == 0 .OR. p2 == 0) CYCLE

                CALL get_edge_line(blk(ib), side, x1, y1)
                CALL get_edge_line(blk(b2), p2, x2, y2)

                n1 = SIZE(x1)
                n2 = SIZE(x2)

                CALL periodic_match_endpoints( &
                    x1(1), y1(1), x1(n1), y1(n1), &
                    x2(1), y2(1), x2(n2), y2(n2), &
                    pitch, rev, yoff &
                )

                IF (rev) THEN
                    d1 = SQRT((x1(1)-x2(n2))**2 + (y1(1)-(y2(n2)+yoff))**2)
                    d2 = SQRT((x1(n1)-x2(1))**2 + (y1(n1)-(y2(1)+yoff))**2)
                ELSE
                    d1 = SQRT((x1(1)-x2(1))**2 + (y1(1)-(y2(1)+yoff))**2)
                    d2 = SQRT((x1(n1)-x2(n2))**2 + (y1(n1)-(y2(n2)+yoff))**2)
                END IF

                worst = MAX(worst, d1, d2)

                IF (MAX(d1,d2) > 1.0D-3) THEN
                    PRINT *, "WARNING topology mismatch:"
                    PRINT *, "  block,side -> block,patch = ", ib, side, " -> ", b2, p2
                    PRINT *, "  endpoint errors = ", d1, d2
                    PRINT *, "  rev,yoff = ", rev, yoff
                END IF

                DEALLOCATE(x1, y1, x2, y2)

            END DO
        END DO

        PRINT *, "Worst topology endpoint mismatch = ", worst

    END SUBROUTINE validate_next_patch_topology

    SUBROUTINE initialise_nine_block_topology(nb, np)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(OUT) :: nb(9,4), np(9,4)

        nb = 0
        np = 0

        ! Block 1: upper inlet.
        nb(1,2) = 5; np(1,2) = 4
        nb(1,3) = 2; np(1,3) = 4
        nb(1,4) = 2; np(1,4) = 3

        ! Block 2: lower inlet.
        nb(2,2) = 9; np(2,2) = 1
        nb(2,3) = 1; np(2,3) = 4
        nb(2,4) = 1; np(2,4) = 3

        ! Block 3: upper outlet.
        nb(3,1) = 7; np(3,1) = 4
        nb(3,3) = 4; np(3,3) = 4
        nb(3,4) = 4; np(3,4) = 3

        ! Block 4: lower outlet.
        nb(4,1) = 9; np(4,1) = 2
        nb(4,3) = 3; np(4,3) = 4
        nb(4,4) = 3; np(4,4) = 3

        ! Block 5: leading-edge cap.
        nb(5,1) = 8; np(5,1) = 2
        nb(5,2) = 6; np(5,2) = 1
        nb(5,4) = 1; np(5,4) = 2

        ! Block 6: upper surface.
        nb(6,1) = 5; np(6,1) = 2
        nb(6,2) = 7; np(6,2) = 1
        nb(6,4) = 9; np(6,4) = 3

        ! Block 7: trailing-edge cap.
        nb(7,1) = 6; np(7,1) = 2
        nb(7,2) = 8; np(7,2) = 1
        nb(7,4) = 3; np(7,4) = 1

        ! Block 8: lower surface.
        nb(8,1) = 7; np(8,1) = 2
        nb(8,2) = 5; np(8,2) = 1
        nb(8,4) = 9; np(8,4) = 4

        ! Block 9: lower return block.
        nb(9,1) = 2; np(9,1) = 2
        nb(9,2) = 4; np(9,2) = 1
        nb(9,3) = 6; np(9,3) = 4
        nb(9,4) = 8; np(9,4) = 4

    END SUBROUTINE initialise_nine_block_topology

    SUBROUTINE fill_ghost_for_side(blk, ib, side, pitch, gx, gy)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ib, side
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        REAL(8), INTENT(INOUT) :: gx(:), gy(:)

        INTEGER :: b2, p2
        INTEGER :: ntgt, nsrc, k, k2
        LOGICAL :: rev
        REAL(8) :: yoff

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xb(:), yb(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xs(:), ys(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xr(:), yr(:)

        b2 = next_block(ib,side)
        p2 = next_patch(ib,side)

        IF (b2 == 0 .OR. p2 == 0) RETURN

        ntgt = SIZE(gx)

        CALL get_edge_line(blk(ib), side, xb, yb)
        CALL get_edge_line(blk(b2), p2, xs, ys)

        CALL periodic_match_endpoints( &
            xb(1), yb(1), xb(SIZE(xb)), yb(SIZE(yb)), &
            xs(1), ys(1), xs(SIZE(xs)), ys(SIZE(ys)), &
            pitch, rev, yoff &
        )

        DEALLOCATE(xb, yb, xs, ys)

        CALL get_interior_line_next_to_patch(blk(b2), p2, xs, ys)

        nsrc = SIZE(xs)
        ALLOCATE(xr(ntgt), yr(ntgt))

        CALL resample_line(xs, ys, nsrc, xr, yr, ntgt)

        DO k = 1, ntgt
            IF (rev) THEN
                k2 = ntgt - k + 1
            ELSE
                k2 = k
            END IF
            gx(k) = xr(k2)
            gy(k) = yr(k2) + yoff
        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(xs, ys, xr, yr)

    END SUBROUTINE fill_ghost_for_side

    SUBROUTINE make_periodic_topology(blk, nb, np, ib, pitch)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nb(9,4), np(9,4)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ib
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch

        INTEGER :: side, b2, p2
        INTEGER :: ntgt, nsrc, k, k2
        LOGICAL :: rev
        REAL(8) :: yoff
        REAL(8) :: xa, ya

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x1(:), y1(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x2(:), y2(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x2r(:), y2r(:)

        DO side = 1, 4

            b2 = nb(ib,side)
            p2 = np(ib,side)

            IF (b2 == 0 .OR. p2 == 0) CYCLE

            ! Avoid processing each pair twice in the same sweep.
            IF (b2 < ib) CYCLE

            CALL get_edge_line(blk(ib), side, x1, y1)
            CALL get_edge_line(blk(b2), p2, x2, y2)

            ntgt = SIZE(x1)
            nsrc = SIZE(x2)

            CALL periodic_match_endpoints( &
                x1(1), y1(1), x1(ntgt), y1(ntgt), &
                x2(1), y2(1), x2(nsrc), y2(nsrc), &
                pitch, rev, yoff &
            )

            ALLOCATE(x2r(ntgt), y2r(ntgt))

            IF (nsrc == ntgt) THEN
                x2r(:) = x2(:)
                y2r(:) = y2(:)
            ELSE
                CALL resample_line(x2, y2, nsrc, x2r, y2r, ntgt)
            END IF

            DO k = 1, ntgt

                IF (rev) THEN
                    k2 = ntgt - k + 1
                ELSE
                    k2 = k
                END IF

                xa = 0.5D0*(x1(k) + x2r(k2))
                ya = 0.5D0*(y1(k) + y2r(k2) + yoff)

                x1(k) = xa
                y1(k) = ya

                x2r(k2) = xa
                y2r(k2) = ya - yoff

            END DO

            CALL set_edge_line(blk(ib), side, x1, y1)

            IF (nsrc == ntgt) THEN
                CALL set_edge_line(blk(b2), p2, x2r, y2r)
            ELSE
                CALL set_edge_line_resampled(blk(b2), p2, x2r, y2r)
            END IF

            DEALLOCATE(x1, y1, x2, y2, x2r, y2r)

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE make_periodic_topology

    SUBROUTINE get_edge_line(b, side, xedge, yedge)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE, INTENT(OUT) :: xedge(:), yedge(:)

        INTEGER :: ni, nj, k

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        SELECT CASE (side)
        CASE (1)
            ALLOCATE(xedge(nj), yedge(nj))
            DO k = 1, nj
                xedge(k) = b%x(1,k)
                yedge(k) = b%y(1,k)
            END DO
        CASE (2)
            ALLOCATE(xedge(nj), yedge(nj))
            DO k = 1, nj
                xedge(k) = b%x(ni,k)
                yedge(k) = b%y(ni,k)
            END DO
        CASE (3)
            ALLOCATE(xedge(ni), yedge(ni))
            DO k = 1, ni
                xedge(k) = b%x(k,1)
                yedge(k) = b%y(k,1)
            END DO
        CASE (4)
            ALLOCATE(xedge(ni), yedge(ni))
            DO k = 1, ni
                xedge(k) = b%x(k,nj)
                yedge(k) = b%y(k,nj)
            END DO
        CASE DEFAULT
            PRINT *, "Bad side in get_edge_line: ", side
            STOP
        END SELECT

    END SUBROUTINE get_edge_line

    SUBROUTINE get_interior_line_next_to_patch(b, side, xline, yline)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE, INTENT(OUT) :: xline(:), yline(:)

        INTEGER :: ni, nj, k
        INTEGER :: ii, jj

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)

        SELECT CASE (side)
        CASE (1)
            ALLOCATE(xline(nj), yline(nj))
            ii = MIN(2,ni)
            DO k = 1, nj
                xline(k) = b%x(ii,k)
                yline(k) = b%y(ii,k)
            END DO
        CASE (2)
            ALLOCATE(xline(nj), yline(nj))
            ii = MAX(ni-1,1)
            DO k = 1, nj
                xline(k) = b%x(ii,k)
                yline(k) = b%y(ii,k)
            END DO
        CASE (3)
            ALLOCATE(xline(ni), yline(ni))
            jj = MIN(2,nj)
            DO k = 1, ni
                xline(k) = b%x(k,jj)
                yline(k) = b%y(k,jj)
            END DO
        CASE (4)
            ALLOCATE(xline(ni), yline(ni))
            jj = MAX(nj-1,1)
            DO k = 1, ni
                xline(k) = b%x(k,jj)
                yline(k) = b%y(k,jj)
            END DO
        CASE DEFAULT
            PRINT *, "Bad side in get_interior_line_next_to_patch: ", side
            STOP
        END SELECT

    END SUBROUTINE get_interior_line_next_to_patch

    SUBROUTINE set_edge_line(b, side, xedge, yedge)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xedge(:), yedge(:)

        INTEGER :: ni, nj, n, k

        ni = SIZE(b%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(b%x,2)
        n = SIZE(xedge)

        SELECT CASE (side)
        CASE (1)
            DO k = 1, MIN(n,nj)
                b%x(1,k) = xedge(k)
                b%y(1,k) = yedge(k)
            END DO
        CASE (2)
            DO k = 1, MIN(n,nj)
                b%x(ni,k) = xedge(k)
                b%y(ni,k) = yedge(k)
            END DO
        CASE (3)
            DO k = 1, MIN(n,ni)
                b%x(k,1) = xedge(k)
                b%y(k,1) = yedge(k)
            END DO
        CASE (4)
            DO k = 1, MIN(n,ni)
                b%x(k,nj) = xedge(k)
                b%y(k,nj) = yedge(k)
            END DO
        CASE DEFAULT
            PRINT *, "Bad side in set_edge_line: ", side
            STOP
        END SELECT

    END SUBROUTINE set_edge_line

    SUBROUTINE set_edge_line_resampled(b, side, xedge, yedge)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: side
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xedge(:), yedge(:)

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xr(:), yr(:)
        INTEGER :: ntgt

        SELECT CASE (side)
        CASE (1,2)
            ntgt = SIZE(b%x,2)
        CASE (3,4)
            ntgt = SIZE(b%x,1)
        CASE DEFAULT
            PRINT *, "Bad side in set_edge_line_resampled: ", side
            STOP
        END SELECT

        ! Preserve point-by-point spacing when the counts already match.
        ! Calling resample_line here used to redistribute the edge uniformly
        IF (SIZE(xedge) == ntgt) THEN
            CALL set_edge_line(b, side, xedge, yedge)
            RETURN
        END IF

        ALLOCATE(xr(ntgt), yr(ntgt))
        CALL resample_line(xedge, yedge, SIZE(xedge), xr, yr, ntgt)
        CALL set_edge_line(b, side, xr, yr)
        DEALLOCATE(xr, yr)

    END SUBROUTINE set_edge_line_resampled

    SUBROUTINE periodic_match_endpoints( &
            xa1, ya1, xa2, ya2, xb1, yb1, xb2, yb2, pitch, rev, yoff)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN)  :: xa1, ya1, xa2, ya2
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN)  :: xb1, yb1, xb2, yb2
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN)  :: pitch
        LOGICAL, INTENT(OUT) :: rev
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: yoff

        REAL(8) :: offs(3), best, d
        INTEGER :: io

        offs = (/ -pitch, 0.0D0, +pitch /)
        best = 1.0D300
        rev  = .FALSE.
        yoff = 0.0D0

        DO io = 1, 3

            d = (xa1-xb1)**2 + (ya1-(yb1+offs(io)))**2 + &
                (xa2-xb2)**2 + (ya2-(yb2+offs(io)))**2

            IF (d < best) THEN
                best = d
                rev  = .FALSE.
                yoff = offs(io)
            END IF

            d = (xa1-xb2)**2 + (ya1-(yb2+offs(io)))**2 + &
                (xa2-xb1)**2 + (ya2-(yb1+offs(io)))**2

            IF (d < best) THEN
                best = d
                rev  = .TRUE.
                yoff = offs(io)
            END IF

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE periodic_match_endpoints

    SUBROUTINE resample_line(xsrc, ysrc, nsrc, xtgt, ytgt, ntgt)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nsrc, ntgt
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xsrc(nsrc), ysrc(nsrc)
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: xtgt(ntgt), ytgt(ntgt)

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: ssrc(:), stgt(:)
        REAL(8) :: total, target, seg, f
        INTEGER :: i, k

        IF (ntgt <= 0) RETURN

        IF (nsrc <= 1) THEN
            xtgt(:) = xsrc(1)
            ytgt(:) = ysrc(1)
            RETURN
        END IF

        ALLOCATE(ssrc(nsrc), stgt(ntgt))

        ssrc(1) = 0.0D0
        DO i = 2, nsrc
            ssrc(i) = ssrc(i-1) + SQRT((xsrc(i)-xsrc(i-1))**2 + &
                                        (ysrc(i)-ysrc(i-1))**2)
        END DO

        total = ssrc(nsrc)

        IF (total <= 1.0D-14) THEN
            xtgt(:) = xsrc(1)
            ytgt(:) = ysrc(1)
            DEALLOCATE(ssrc, stgt)
            RETURN
        END IF

        IF (ntgt == 1) THEN
            stgt(1) = 0.0D0
        ELSE
            DO i = 1, ntgt
                stgt(i) = total*REAL(i-1,8)/REAL(ntgt-1,8)
            END DO
        END IF

        k = 1
        DO i = 1, ntgt

            target = stgt(i)

            DO WHILE (k < nsrc-1 .AND. ssrc(k+1) < target)
                k = k + 1
            END DO

            seg = ssrc(k+1) - ssrc(k)

            IF (seg > 1.0D-14) THEN
                f = (target - ssrc(k))/seg
            ELSE
                f = 0.0D0
            END IF

            xtgt(i) = (1.0D0-f)*xsrc(k) + f*xsrc(k+1)
            ytgt(i) = (1.0D0-f)*ysrc(k) + f*ysrc(k+1)

        END DO

        xtgt(1) = xsrc(1)
        ytgt(1) = ysrc(1)

        xtgt(ntgt) = xsrc(nsrc)
        ytgt(ntgt) = ysrc(nsrc)

        DEALLOCATE(ssrc, stgt)

    END SUBROUTINE resample_line

    SUBROUTINE get_all_block_ghost_lines( &
        blk, ib, pitch, gimx, gimy, gipx, gipy, gjmx, gjmy, gjpx, gjpy &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ib
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch

        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: gimx(:), gimy(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: gipx(:), gipy(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: gjmx(:), gjmy(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: gjpx(:), gjpy(:)

        INTEGER :: ni, nj
        INTEGER :: i, j

        ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1)
        nj = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,2)

        ! Default ghost lines are one-sided reflections.  These are used
        ! on open boundaries with no neighbour in next_block.
        DO j = 1, nj
            gimx(j) = 2.0D0*blk(ib)%x(1,j)  - blk(ib)%x(MIN(2,ni),j)
            gimy(j) = 2.0D0*blk(ib)%y(1,j)  - blk(ib)%y(MIN(2,ni),j)

            gipx(j) = 2.0D0*blk(ib)%x(ni,j) - blk(ib)%x(MAX(ni-1,1),j)
            gipy(j) = 2.0D0*blk(ib)%y(ni,j) - blk(ib)%y(MAX(ni-1,1),j)
        END DO

        DO i = 1, ni
            gjmx(i) = 2.0D0*blk(ib)%x(i,1)  - blk(ib)%x(i,MIN(2,nj))
            gjmy(i) = 2.0D0*blk(ib)%y(i,1)  - blk(ib)%y(i,MIN(2,nj))

            gjpx(i) = 2.0D0*blk(ib)%x(i,nj) - blk(ib)%x(i,MAX(nj-1,1))
            gjpy(i) = 2.0D0*blk(ib)%y(i,nj) - blk(ib)%y(i,MAX(nj-1,1))
        END DO

        ! Replace default ghosts wherever a neighbour exists.
        CALL fill_ghost_for_side(blk, ib, 1, pitch, gimx, gimy)
        CALL fill_ghost_for_side(blk, ib, 2, pitch, gipx, gipy)
