        CALL fill_ghost_for_side(blk, ib, 3, pitch, gjmx, gjmy)
        CALL fill_ghost_for_side(blk, ib, 4, pitch, gjpx, gjpy)

    END SUBROUTINE get_all_block_ghost_lines

    SUBROUTINE elliptic_poisson_ext_point_update( &
        xext, yext, ni, nj, i, j, xnew, ynew &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ni, nj
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: i, j

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xext(0:ni+1,0:nj+1)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: yext(0:ni+1,0:nj+1)

        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: xnew, ynew

        CALL elliptic_poisson_stencil_update( &
            xext(i,j),   yext(i,j), &
            xext(i-1,j), yext(i-1,j), &
            xext(i+1,j), yext(i+1,j), &
            xext(i,j-1), yext(i,j-1), &
            xext(i,j+1), yext(i,j+1), &
            xext(i-1,j-1), yext(i-1,j-1), &
            xext(i-1,j+1), yext(i-1,j+1), &
            xext(i+1,j-1), yext(i+1,j-1), &
            xext(i+1,j+1), yext(i+1,j+1), &
            xnew, ynew &
        )

    END SUBROUTINE elliptic_poisson_ext_point_update

    SUBROUTINE elliptic_poisson_stencil_update( &
        xc, yc, xl, yl, xr, yr, xd, yd, xu, yu, &
        xld, yld, xlu, ylu, xrd, yrd, xru, yru, xnew, ynew &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xc, yc
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xl, yl, xr, yr
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xd, yd, xu, yu
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xld, yld, xlu, ylu
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xrd, yrd, xru, yru

        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: xnew, ynew

        REAL(8) :: x_xi, y_xi
        REAL(8) :: x_et, y_et
        REAL(8) :: alpha, beta, gamma
        REAL(8) :: denom
        REAL(8) :: x_cross, y_cross
        REAL(8) :: eps

        eps = 1.0D-14

        x_xi = 0.5D0*(xr - xl)
        y_xi = 0.5D0*(yr - yl)

        x_et = 0.5D0*(xu - xd)
        y_et = 0.5D0*(yu - yd)

        alpha = x_et*x_et + y_et*y_et
        beta  = x_xi*x_et + y_xi*y_et
        gamma = x_xi*x_xi + y_xi*y_xi

        denom = 2.0D0*(alpha + gamma)

        IF (ABS(denom) < eps) THEN
            xnew = xc
            ynew = yc
            RETURN
        END IF

        x_cross = xru - xrd - xlu + xld
        y_cross = yru - yrd - ylu + yld

        xnew = ( &
            alpha*(xr + xl) + &
            gamma*(xu + xd) - &
            0.5D0*beta*x_cross &
        )/denom

        ynew = ( &
            alpha*(yr + yl) + &
            gamma*(yu + yd) - &
            0.5D0*beta*y_cross &
        )/denom

    END SUBROUTINE elliptic_poisson_stencil_update

    SUBROUTINE copy_blocks_xy(src, dst)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: src(:)
        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: dst(:)

        INTEGER :: ib
        INTEGER :: ni, nj

        DO ib = 1, SIZE(src)

            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(src(ib)%x)) CYCLE
            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(src(ib)%y)) CYCLE

            ni = SIZE(src(ib)%x, 1)
            nj = SIZE(src(ib)%x, 2)

            IF (ALLOCATED(dst(ib)%x)) DEALLOCATE(dst(ib)%x)
            IF (ALLOCATED(dst(ib)%y)) DEALLOCATE(dst(ib)%y)

            ALLOCATE(dst(ib)%x(ni,nj))
            ALLOCATE(dst(ib)%y(ni,nj))

            dst(ib)%x = src(ib)%x
            dst(ib)%y = src(ib)%y

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE copy_blocks_xy

    SUBROUTINE free_blocks_xy(b)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: b(:)

        INTEGER :: ib

        DO ib = 1, SIZE(b)

            IF (ALLOCATED(b(ib)%x)) DEALLOCATE(b(ib)%x)
            IF (ALLOCATED(b(ib)%y)) DEALLOCATE(b(ib)%y)

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE free_blocks_xy

    SUBROUTINE make_outer_passage_blocks(blk, nwall, nnormal, pitch)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: nwall, nnormal
        INTEGER :: suppress_unused_nwall

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(INOUT) :: blk(:)

        REAL(8) :: xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax
        REAL(8) :: chord_len

        ! This is the only manual corner section for the five extra
        ! blocks: 1, 2, 3, 4, and 9.
        REAL(8) :: xL, yLTop, yLMid, yLBot
        REAL(8) :: xR, yRTop, yRMid, yRBot

        REAL(8) :: xB6TL, yB6TL
        REAL(8) :: xB6TR, yB6TR
        REAL(8) :: xB8BL, yB8BL
        REAL(8) :: xB8BR, yB8BR
        REAL(8) :: xB9BL, yB9BL
        REAL(8) :: xB9BR, yB9BR

        REAL(8) :: coeff_dummy(4,6,2)

        INTEGER :: n5wall, n6wall, n7wall, n8wall

        suppress_unused_nwall = nwall

        n5wall = SIZE(blk(5)%x,1)
        n6wall = SIZE(blk(6)%x,1)
        n7wall = SIZE(blk(7)%x,1)
        n8wall = SIZE(blk(8)%x,1)

        CALL block_bounds_5_to_8(blk, xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax)

        chord_len = SQRT((xmax-xmin)**2 + (ymax-ymin)**2)
        IF (chord_len <= 1.0D-12) chord_len = 1.0D0

        ! Replace these right-hand sides with the exact point coordinates
        xL    = xmin - 0.7D0*chord_len
        yLTop = ymin + 0.12D0*chord_len
        yLBot = yLTop - pitch
        yLMid = 0.5D0*(yLTop + yLBot)

        xR    = xmax + 0.7D0*chord_len
        yRTop = ymax + 0.10D0*chord_len
        yRBot = yRTop - pitch
        yRMid = 0.5D0*(yRTop + yRBot)

        xB9BL = xmin + 0.05D0*chord_len
        yB9BL = ymin - 1.0D0*chord_len

        xB9BR = xmax + 0.15D0*chord_len
        yB9BR = ymax - 1.05D0*chord_len

        ! Fixed central attachment points from already generated blocks.
        xB6TL = blk(6)%x(1,nnormal)
        yB6TL = blk(6)%y(1,nnormal)

        xB6TR = blk(6)%x(n6wall,nnormal)
        yB6TR = blk(6)%y(n6wall,nnormal)

        xB8BR = blk(8)%x(1,nnormal)
        yB8BR = blk(8)%y(1,nnormal)

        xB8BL = blk(8)%x(n8wall,nnormal)
        yB8BL = blk(8)%y(n8wall,nnormal)

        ! Periodic bottom boundary corner constraints.
        ! For the passage to tile, the lower boundary must be the upper
        xB9BL = xB6TL
        yB9BL = yB6TL - pitch

        xB9BR = xB6TR
        yB9BR = yB6TR - pitch

        ! Four edges per block. Each edge has six polynomial coefficients
        ! inside make_coeff_quad_block for the straight-line case.
        coeff_dummy = 0.0D0

        ! Create the five outer blocks from exactly four corners each.
        ! Corner order used by make_coeff_quad_block:
        ! Block 1: use the actual outer edge of Block 5 as the right edge.
        ! Do not replace this with a straight line between B8BL and B6TL.
        CALL make_coeff_quad_block_with_given_right_edge( &
            xL, yLMid, xL, yLTop, &
            blk(5)%x(:,nnormal), blk(5)%y(:,nnormal), &
            coeff_dummy, nnormal, n5wall, blk(1) &
        )

        ! Block 2: same-orientation periodic edge construction.
        ! Bottom edge = Block 1 top edge shifted down by one pitch.
        CALL make_quad_bt_same( &
            blk(1)%x(:,n5wall), blk(1)%y(:,n5wall) - pitch, &
            blk(1)%x(:,1),      blk(1)%y(:,1), &
            coeff_dummy, nnormal, nnormal, blk(2) &
        )

        ! Block 3: use the actual outer edge of Block 7 as the left edge.
        ! Do not replace this with a straight line between B8BR and B6TR.
        CALL make_coeff_quad_block_with_given_left_edge( &
            xR, yRMid, xR, yRTop, &
            blk(7)%x(:,nnormal), blk(7)%y(:,nnormal), &
            coeff_dummy, nnormal, n7wall, blk(3) &
        )

        ! Block 4: same-orientation periodic edge construction.
        ! Bottom edge = Block 3 top edge shifted down by one pitch.
        CALL make_quad_bt_same( &
            blk(3)%x(:,n7wall), blk(3)%y(:,n7wall) - pitch, &
            blk(3)%x(:,1),      blk(3)%y(:,1), &
            coeff_dummy, nnormal, nnormal, blk(4) &
        )

        ! Block 9: use the actual outer edge of Block 8 as the top edge,
        ! and use the actual outer edge of Block 6 shifted down by one
        CALL make_coeff_quad_block_with_given_bottom_and_top_edges( &
            blk(6)%x(:,nnormal), blk(6)%y(:,nnormal) - pitch, &
            blk(8)%x(:,nnormal), blk(8)%y(:,nnormal), &
            coeff_dummy, n6wall, nnormal, blk(9) &
        )

        PRINT *, "Manual outer point coordinates:"
        PRINT *, "  LTop = ", xL, yLTop
        PRINT *, "  LMid = ", xL, yLMid
        PRINT *, "  LBot = ", xL, yLBot
        PRINT *, "  RTop = ", xR, yRTop
        PRINT *, "  RMid = ", xR, yRMid
        PRINT *, "  RBot = ", xR, yRBot
        PRINT *, "  B9BL = ", xB9BL, yB9BL
        PRINT *, "  B9BR = ", xB9BR, yB9BR

        PRINT *, "Central attachment points:"
        PRINT *, "  B6TL = ", xB6TL, yB6TL
        PRINT *, "  B6TR = ", xB6TR, yB6TR
        PRINT *, "  B8BL = ", xB8BL, yB8BL
        PRINT *, "  B8BR = ", xB8BR, yB8BR

    END SUBROUTINE make_outer_passage_blocks

    SUBROUTINE block_bounds_5_to_8(blk, xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)

        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax

        INTEGER :: ib

        xmin = HUGE(1.0D0)
        xmax = -HUGE(1.0D0)
        ymin = HUGE(1.0D0)
        ymax = -HUGE(1.0D0)

        DO ib = 5, 8
            xmin = MIN(xmin, MINVAL(blk(ib)%x))
            xmax = MAX(xmax, MAXVAL(blk(ib)%x))
            ymin = MIN(ymin, MINVAL(blk(ib)%y))
            ymax = MAX(ymax, MAXVAL(blk(ib)%y))
        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE block_bounds_5_to_8

    SUBROUTINE make_quad_bt_same( &
        xbottom_given, ybottom_given, xtop_given, ytop_given, &
        coeff_unused, ni, nj, blk &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ni, nj

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xbottom_given(ni), ybottom_given(ni)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xtop_given(ni), ytop_given(ni)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: coeff_unused(4,6,2)

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(OUT) :: blk

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xb(:), yb(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xt(:), yt(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xl(:), yl(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xr(:), yr(:)

        REAL(8) :: clx(6), cly(6)
        REAL(8) :: crx(6), cry(6)

        REAL(8) :: x_bl, y_bl
        REAL(8) :: x_br, y_br
        REAL(8) :: x_tl, y_tl
        REAL(8) :: x_tr, y_tr

        INTEGER :: i, j
        REAL(8) :: s, t

        IF (ALLOCATED(blk%x)) DEALLOCATE(blk%x)
        IF (ALLOCATED(blk%y)) DEALLOCATE(blk%y)

        ALLOCATE(blk%x(ni,nj), blk%y(ni,nj))

        IF (SIZE(coeff_unused) < 0) PRINT *, coeff_unused(1,1,1)

        ALLOCATE(xb(ni), yb(ni))
        ALLOCATE(xt(ni), yt(ni))
        ALLOCATE(xl(nj), yl(nj))
        ALLOCATE(xr(nj), yr(nj))

        ! Bottom and top edges are supplied in the same left-to-right
        ! orientation.  This is required for Blocks 2 and 4.
        DO i = 1, ni
            xb(i) = xbottom_given(i)
            yb(i) = ybottom_given(i)

            xt(i) = xtop_given(i)
            yt(i) = ytop_given(i)
        END DO

        x_bl = xb(1)
        y_bl = yb(1)

        x_br = xb(ni)
        y_br = yb(ni)

        x_tl = xt(1)
        y_tl = yt(1)

        x_tr = xt(ni)
        y_tr = yt(ni)

        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(x_bl, x_tl, clx)
        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(y_bl, y_tl, cly)

        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(x_br, x_tr, crx)
        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(y_br, y_tr, cry)

        CALL make_polynomial_edge(clx, cly, nj, xl, yl)
        CALL make_polynomial_edge(crx, cry, nj, xr, yr)

        DO j = 1, nj

            t = REAL(j-1, KIND=8)/REAL(nj-1, KIND=8)

            DO i = 1, ni

                s = REAL(i-1, KIND=8)/REAL(ni-1, KIND=8)

                blk%x(i,j) = &
                    (1.0D0-s)*xl(j) + s*xr(j) + &
                    (1.0D0-t)*xb(i) + t*xt(i) - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*(1.0D0-t)*x_bl - &
                    s*(1.0D0-t)*x_br - &
                    s*t*x_tr - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*t*x_tl

                blk%y(i,j) = &
                    (1.0D0-s)*yl(j) + s*yr(j) + &
                    (1.0D0-t)*yb(i) + t*yt(i) - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*(1.0D0-t)*y_bl - &
                    s*(1.0D0-t)*y_br - &
                    s*t*y_tr - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*t*y_tl

            END DO

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(xb, yb, xt, yt, xl, yl, xr, yr)

    END SUBROUTINE make_quad_bt_same

    SUBROUTINE make_coeff_quad_block_with_given_bottom_and_top_edges( &
        xbottom_given, ybottom_given, xtop_given, ytop_given, &
        coeff_unused, ni, nj, blk &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ni, nj

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xbottom_given(ni), ybottom_given(ni)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xtop_given(ni), ytop_given(ni)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: coeff_unused(4,6,2)

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(OUT) :: blk

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xb(:), yb(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xt(:), yt(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xl(:), yl(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xr(:), yr(:)

        REAL(8) :: clx(6), cly(6)
        REAL(8) :: crx(6), cry(6)

        REAL(8) :: x_bl, y_bl
        REAL(8) :: x_br, y_br
        REAL(8) :: x_tl, y_tl
        REAL(8) :: x_tr, y_tr

        INTEGER :: i, j
        REAL(8) :: s, t

        IF (ALLOCATED(blk%x)) DEALLOCATE(blk%x)
        IF (ALLOCATED(blk%y)) DEALLOCATE(blk%y)

        ALLOCATE(blk%x(ni,nj), blk%y(ni,nj))

        IF (SIZE(coeff_unused) < 0) PRINT *, coeff_unused(1,1,1)

        ALLOCATE(xb(ni), yb(ni))
        ALLOCATE(xt(ni), yt(ni))
        ALLOCATE(xl(nj), yl(nj))
        ALLOCATE(xr(nj), yr(nj))

        ! Bottom edge is the supplied periodic copy of Block 6:
        ! left -> right.
        DO i = 1, ni
            xb(i) = xbottom_given(i)
            yb(i) = ybottom_given(i)
        END DO

        ! Top edge is the supplied Block 8 outer edge.
        ! Block 8 is stored right -> left on its outer edge, so reverse it
        DO i = 1, ni
            xt(i) = xtop_given(ni-i+1)
            yt(i) = ytop_given(ni-i+1)
        END DO

        x_bl = xb(1)
        y_bl = yb(1)

        x_br = xb(ni)
        y_br = yb(ni)

        x_tl = xt(1)
        y_tl = yt(1)

        x_tr = xt(ni)
        y_tr = yt(ni)

        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(x_bl, x_tl, clx)
        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(y_bl, y_tl, cly)

        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(x_br, x_tr, crx)
        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(y_br, y_tr, cry)

        CALL make_polynomial_edge(clx, cly, nj, xl, yl)
        CALL make_polynomial_edge(crx, cry, nj, xr, yr)

        DO j = 1, nj

            t = REAL(j-1, KIND=8)/REAL(nj-1, KIND=8)

            DO i = 1, ni

                s = REAL(i-1, KIND=8)/REAL(ni-1, KIND=8)

                blk%x(i,j) = &
                    (1.0D0-s)*xl(j) + s*xr(j) + &
                    (1.0D0-t)*xb(i) + t*xt(i) - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*(1.0D0-t)*x_bl - &
                    s*(1.0D0-t)*x_br - &
                    s*t*x_tr - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*t*x_tl

                blk%y(i,j) = &
                    (1.0D0-s)*yl(j) + s*yr(j) + &
                    (1.0D0-t)*yb(i) + t*yt(i) - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*(1.0D0-t)*y_bl - &
                    s*(1.0D0-t)*y_br - &
                    s*t*y_tr - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*t*y_tl

            END DO

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(xb, yb, xt, yt, xl, yl, xr, yr)

    END SUBROUTINE make_coeff_quad_block_with_given_bottom_and_top_edges

    SUBROUTINE make_coeff_quad_block_with_given_right_edge( &
        x_bl, y_bl, x_tl, y_tl, xright_given, yright_given, &
        coeff_unused, ni, nj, blk &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ni, nj

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x_bl, y_bl
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x_tl, y_tl
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xright_given(nj), yright_given(nj)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: coeff_unused(4,6,2)

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(OUT) :: blk

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xb(:), yb(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xr(:), yr(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xt(:), yt(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xl(:), yl(:)

        REAL(8) :: cbx(6), cby(6)
        REAL(8) :: ctx(6), cty(6)
        REAL(8) :: clx(6), cly(6)

        REAL(8) :: x_br, y_br
        REAL(8) :: x_tr, y_tr

        INTEGER :: i, j
        REAL(8) :: s, t

        ALLOCATE(blk%x(ni,nj), blk%y(ni,nj))

        IF (SIZE(coeff_unused) < 0) PRINT *, coeff_unused(1,1,1)

        ALLOCATE(xb(ni), yb(ni))
        ALLOCATE(xt(ni), yt(ni))
        ALLOCATE(xl(nj), yl(nj))
        ALLOCATE(xr(nj), yr(nj))

        ! Right edge is exactly the supplied near-blade block edge.
        ! For Block 1 this is Block 5's outer edge.
        DO j = 1, nj
            xr(j) = xright_given(j)
            yr(j) = yright_given(j)
        END DO

        x_br = xr(1)
        y_br = yr(1)

        x_tr = xr(nj)
        y_tr = yr(nj)

        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(x_bl, x_br, cbx)
        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(y_bl, y_br, cby)

        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(x_tl, x_tr, ctx)
        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(y_tl, y_tr, cty)

        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(x_bl, x_tl, clx)
        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(y_bl, y_tl, cly)

        ! Bottom, top, and left stay straight for now.
        CALL make_polynomial_edge(cbx, cby, ni, xb, yb)
        CALL make_polynomial_edge(ctx, cty, ni, xt, yt)
        CALL make_polynomial_edge(clx, cly, nj, xl, yl)

        DO j = 1, nj

            t = REAL(j-1, KIND=8)/REAL(nj-1, KIND=8)

            DO i = 1, ni

                s = REAL(i-1, KIND=8)/REAL(ni-1, KIND=8)

                blk%x(i,j) = &
                    (1.0D0-s)*xl(j) + s*xr(j) + &
                    (1.0D0-t)*xb(i) + t*xt(i) - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*(1.0D0-t)*x_bl - &
                    s*(1.0D0-t)*x_br - &
                    s*t*x_tr - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*t*x_tl

                blk%y(i,j) = &
                    (1.0D0-s)*yl(j) + s*yr(j) + &
                    (1.0D0-t)*yb(i) + t*yt(i) - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*(1.0D0-t)*y_bl - &
                    s*(1.0D0-t)*y_br - &
                    s*t*y_tr - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*t*y_tl

            END DO

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(xb, yb, xr, yr, xt, yt, xl, yl)

    END SUBROUTINE make_coeff_quad_block_with_given_right_edge

    SUBROUTINE make_coeff_quad_block_with_given_left_edge( &
        x_br, y_br, x_tr, y_tr, xleft_given, yleft_given, &
        coeff_unused, ni, nj, blk &
    )

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ni, nj

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x_br, y_br
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: x_tr, y_tr
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xleft_given(nj), yleft_given(nj)
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: coeff_unused(4,6,2)

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(OUT) :: blk

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xb(:), yb(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xr(:), yr(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xt(:), yt(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xl(:), yl(:)

        REAL(8) :: cbx(6), cby(6)
        REAL(8) :: crx(6), cry(6)
        REAL(8) :: ctx(6), cty(6)

        REAL(8) :: x_bl, y_bl
        REAL(8) :: x_tl, y_tl

        INTEGER :: i, j
        REAL(8) :: s, t

        ALLOCATE(blk%x(ni,nj), blk%y(ni,nj))

        IF (SIZE(coeff_unused) < 0) PRINT *, coeff_unused(1,1,1)

        ALLOCATE(xb(ni), yb(ni))
        ALLOCATE(xt(ni), yt(ni))
        ALLOCATE(xl(nj), yl(nj))
        ALLOCATE(xr(nj), yr(nj))

        ! Left edge is exactly the supplied near-blade block edge.
        ! For Block 3 this is Block 7's outer edge.
        DO j = 1, nj
            xl(j) = xleft_given(nj-j+1)
            yl(j) = yleft_given(nj-j+1)
        END DO

        x_bl = xl(1)
        y_bl = yl(1)

        x_tl = xl(nj)
        y_tl = yl(nj)

        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(x_bl, x_br, cbx)
        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(y_bl, y_br, cby)

        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(x_br, x_tr, crx)
        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(y_br, y_tr, cry)

        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(x_tl, x_tr, ctx)
        CALL straight_line_poly_coeff(y_tl, y_tr, cty)

        ! Bottom, right, and top stay straight for now.
        CALL make_polynomial_edge(cbx, cby, ni, xb, yb)
        CALL make_polynomial_edge(crx, cry, nj, xr, yr)
        CALL make_polynomial_edge(ctx, cty, ni, xt, yt)

        DO j = 1, nj

            t = REAL(j-1, KIND=8)/REAL(nj-1, KIND=8)

            DO i = 1, ni

                s = REAL(i-1, KIND=8)/REAL(ni-1, KIND=8)

                blk%x(i,j) = &
                    (1.0D0-s)*xl(j) + s*xr(j) + &
                    (1.0D0-t)*xb(i) + t*xt(i) - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*(1.0D0-t)*x_bl - &
                    s*(1.0D0-t)*x_br - &
                    s*t*x_tr - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*t*x_tl

                blk%y(i,j) = &
                    (1.0D0-s)*yl(j) + s*yr(j) + &
                    (1.0D0-t)*yb(i) + t*yt(i) - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*(1.0D0-t)*y_bl - &
                    s*(1.0D0-t)*y_br - &
                    s*t*y_tr - &
                    (1.0D0-s)*t*y_tl

            END DO

        END DO

        DEALLOCATE(xb, yb, xr, yr, xt, yt, xl, yl)

    END SUBROUTINE make_coeff_quad_block_with_given_left_edge

    SUBROUTINE straight_line_poly_coeff(q0, q1, coeff)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: q0, q1
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: coeff(6)

        coeff(1) = 0.0D0
        coeff(2) = 0.0D0
        coeff(3) = 0.0D0
        coeff(4) = 0.0D0
        coeff(5) = q1 - q0
        coeff(6) = q0

    END SUBROUTINE straight_line_poly_coeff

    SUBROUTINE make_polynomial_edge(xcoeff, ycoeff, n, xedge, yedge)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: n

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xcoeff(6), ycoeff(6)
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: xedge(n), yedge(n)

        INTEGER :: i
        REAL(8) :: s

        DO i = 1, n

            s = REAL(i-1, KIND=8)/REAL(n-1, KIND=8)

            xedge(i) = evaluate_quintic_polynomial(s, xcoeff)
            yedge(i) = evaluate_quintic_polynomial(s, ycoeff)

        END DO

    END SUBROUTINE make_polynomial_edge

    REAL(8) FUNCTION evaluate_quintic_polynomial(s, coeff)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: s
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: coeff(6)

        evaluate_quintic_polynomial = &
            coeff(1)*s**5 + coeff(2)*s**4 + coeff(3)*s**3 + &
            coeff(4)*s**2 + coeff(5)*s + coeff(6)

    END FUNCTION evaluate_quintic_polynomial
        SUBROUTINE compute_small_2d_cell_edge_length(blk, min_edge)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)
        REAL(8), INTENT(OUT) :: min_edge

        INTEGER :: ib, i, j, ni, nj
        REAL(8) :: dl

        min_edge = HUGE(1.0D0)

        DO ib = 1, SIZE(blk)

            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE
            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%y)) CYCLE

            ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1)
            nj = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,2)

            ! i-direction edges
            DO j = 1, nj
                DO i = 1, ni-1
                    dl = SQRT( &
                        (blk(ib)%x(i+1,j)-blk(ib)%x(i,j))**2 + &
                        (blk(ib)%y(i+1,j)-blk(ib)%y(i,j))**2 &
                    )
                    IF (dl > 1.0D-14) min_edge = MIN(min_edge, dl)
                END DO
            END DO

            ! j-direction edges
            DO j = 1, nj-1
                DO i = 1, ni
                    dl = SQRT( &
                        (blk(ib)%x(i,j+1)-blk(ib)%x(i,j))**2 + &
                        (blk(ib)%y(i,j+1)-blk(ib)%y(i,j))**2 &
                    )
                    IF (dl > 1.0D-14) min_edge = MIN(min_edge, dl)
                END DO
            END DO

        END DO

        IF (min_edge == HUGE(1.0D0)) min_edge = 0.0D0

    END SUBROUTINE compute_small_2d_cell_edge_length

    SUBROUTINE add_tet_conn(conn, tet_id, a, b, c, d)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t), INTENT(INOUT) :: conn(:)
        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t), INTENT(INOUT) :: tet_id
        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t), INTENT(IN) :: a, b, c, d

        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t) :: p

        tet_id = tet_id + INT(1, KIND=cgsize_t)

        p = INT(4, KIND=cgsize_t)*(tet_id - INT(1, KIND=cgsize_t))

        conn(p+1) = a
        conn(p+2) = b
        conn(p+3) = c
        conn(p+4) = d

    END SUBROUTINE add_tet_conn

SUBROUTINE write_blade_and_four_blocks_to_cgns( &
        filename, pitch, xprof, yprof, npts, blk, &
        make_3d, extrusion_span, extrusion_nplanes &
    )

        USE cgns
        IMPLICIT NONE

        CHARACTER(LEN=*), INTENT(IN) :: filename

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: npts

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: pitch
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: xprof(npts), yprof(npts)

        TYPE(block_type), INTENT(IN) :: blk(:)

        LOGICAL, INTENT(IN) :: make_3d
        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: extrusion_span
        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: extrusion_nplanes

        INTEGER :: ierr
        INTEGER :: file_id

        INTEGER :: blade_base_id
        INTEGER :: blade_zone_id
        INTEGER :: blade_coord_id
        INTEGER :: blade_section_id

        INTEGER :: mesh_base_id
        INTEGER :: zone_id
        INTEGER :: coord_id

        INTEGER :: ib, i, j, k
        INTEGER :: ni, nj, nk

        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t) :: blade_isize(3)
        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t) :: block_isize_2d(2,3)
        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t) :: block_isize_3d(3,3)
        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t) :: unstruct_isize(3)

        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t) :: element_start
        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t) :: element_end

        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t) :: nverts
        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t) :: ntets
        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t) :: tet_id
        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t) :: n000, n100, n010, n110
        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t) :: n001, n101, n011, n111

        INTEGER(KIND=cgsize_t), ALLOCATABLE :: conn(:)

        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xblade_lower(:), yblade_lower(:), zblade(:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: zblock(:,:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: x3(:,:,:), y3(:,:,:), z3(:,:,:)
        REAL(8), ALLOCATABLE :: xflat(:), yflat(:), zflat(:)

        CHARACTER(LEN=32) :: zone_name

        CALL cg_open_f(filename, CG_MODE_WRITE, file_id, ierr)
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_open_f")

        ! Blade curve as a 1D unstructured BAR_2 zone.
        CALL cg_base_write_f(file_id, "BladeCurveBase", 1, 3, blade_base_id, ierr)
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_base_write_f BladeCurveBase")

        blade_isize(1) = INT(npts, KIND=cgsize_t)
        blade_isize(2) = INT(npts, KIND=cgsize_t)
        blade_isize(3) = INT(0, KIND=cgsize_t)

        CALL cg_zone_write_f( &
            file_id, blade_base_id, "BladeCurve", &
            blade_isize, Unstructured, blade_zone_id, ierr &
        )
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_zone_write_f BladeCurve")

        CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
            file_id, blade_base_id, blade_zone_id, RealDouble, &
            "CoordinateX", xprof, blade_coord_id, ierr &
        )
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "BladeCurve CoordinateX")

        CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
            file_id, blade_base_id, blade_zone_id, RealDouble, &
            "CoordinateY", yprof, blade_coord_id, ierr &
        )
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "BladeCurve CoordinateY")

        ALLOCATE(zblade(npts))
        zblade = 0.0D0

        CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
            file_id, blade_base_id, blade_zone_id, RealDouble, &
            "CoordinateZ", zblade, blade_coord_id, ierr &
        )
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "BladeCurve CoordinateZ")

        DEALLOCATE(zblade)

        ALLOCATE(conn(2*npts))

        DO i = 1, npts-1
            conn(2*i-1) = INT(i, KIND=cgsize_t)
            conn(2*i)   = INT(i+1, KIND=cgsize_t)
        END DO

        conn(2*npts-1) = INT(npts, KIND=cgsize_t)
        conn(2*npts)   = INT(1, KIND=cgsize_t)

        element_start = INT(1, KIND=cgsize_t)
        element_end   = INT(npts, KIND=cgsize_t)

        CALL cg_section_write_f( &
            file_id, blade_base_id, blade_zone_id, &
            "BladeLineElements", BAR_2, &
            element_start, element_end, &
            0, conn, blade_section_id, ierr &
        )
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_section_write_f BladeLineElements")

        DEALLOCATE(conn)

        ! Lower pitch-shifted blade curve for visual checking.
        ALLOCATE(xblade_lower(npts), yblade_lower(npts), zblade(npts))
        xblade_lower = xprof
        yblade_lower = yprof - pitch
        zblade       = 0.0D0

        CALL cg_zone_write_f( &
            file_id, blade_base_id, "BladeCurveLowerPitch", &
            blade_isize, Unstructured, blade_zone_id, ierr &
        )
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_zone_write_f BladeCurveLowerPitch")

        CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
            file_id, blade_base_id, blade_zone_id, RealDouble, &
            "CoordinateX", xblade_lower, blade_coord_id, ierr &
        )
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "BladeCurveLowerPitch CoordinateX")

        CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
            file_id, blade_base_id, blade_zone_id, RealDouble, &
            "CoordinateY", yblade_lower, blade_coord_id, ierr &
        )
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "BladeCurveLowerPitch CoordinateY")

        CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
            file_id, blade_base_id, blade_zone_id, RealDouble, &
            "CoordinateZ", zblade, blade_coord_id, ierr &
        )
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "BladeCurveLowerPitch CoordinateZ")

        DEALLOCATE(zblade)

        ALLOCATE(conn(2*npts))

        DO i = 1, npts-1
            conn(2*i-1) = INT(i, KIND=cgsize_t)
            conn(2*i)   = INT(i+1, KIND=cgsize_t)
        END DO

        conn(2*npts-1) = INT(npts, KIND=cgsize_t)
        conn(2*npts)   = INT(1, KIND=cgsize_t)

        CALL cg_section_write_f( &
            file_id, blade_base_id, blade_zone_id, &
            "BladeLineElements", BAR_2, &
            element_start, element_end, &
            0, conn, blade_section_id, ierr &
        )
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_section_write_f BladeCurveLowerPitch Elements")

        DEALLOCATE(conn)
        DEALLOCATE(xblade_lower, yblade_lower)

        ! Structured mesh blocks.
        !   Base cell dimension = 2, physical dimension = 3.
        IF (make_3d) THEN
            CALL cg_base_write_f(file_id, "BladeMeshBase", 3, 3, mesh_base_id, ierr)
        ELSE
            CALL cg_base_write_f(file_id, "BladeMeshBase", 2, 3, mesh_base_id, ierr)
        END IF

        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_base_write_f BladeMeshBase")

        nk = MAX(2, extrusion_nplanes)

        DO ib = 1, SIZE(blk)

            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%x)) CYCLE
            IF (.NOT. ALLOCATED(blk(ib)%y)) CYCLE

            ni = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,1)
            nj = SIZE(blk(ib)%x,2)

            WRITE(zone_name, '(A,I0)') "Block", ib

            IF (make_3d) THEN

                IF (write_tetrahedral_grid_enabled) THEN

                    nverts = INT(ni*nj*nk, KIND=cgsize_t)
                    ntets  = INT(6*(ni-1)*(nj-1)*(nk-1), KIND=cgsize_t)

                    unstruct_isize(1) = nverts
                    unstruct_isize(2) = ntets
                    unstruct_isize(3) = INT(0, KIND=cgsize_t)

                    CALL cg_zone_write_f( &
                        file_id, mesh_base_id, TRIM(zone_name), &
                        unstruct_isize, Unstructured, zone_id, ierr &
                    )
                    CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_zone_write_f tetra "//TRIM(zone_name))

                    ALLOCATE(xflat(nverts), yflat(nverts), zflat(nverts))

                    DO k = 1, nk
                        DO j = 1, nj
                            DO i = 1, ni
                                tet_id = INT((k-1)*ni*nj + (j-1)*ni + i, KIND=cgsize_t)

                                xflat(tet_id) = blk(ib)%x(i,j)
                                yflat(tet_id) = blk(ib)%y(i,j)

                                IF (nk > 1) THEN
                                    zflat(tet_id) = extrusion_span*REAL(k-1,8)/REAL(nk-1,8)
                                ELSE
                                    zflat(tet_id) = 0.0D0
                                END IF
                            END DO
                        END DO
                    END DO

                    CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
                        file_id, mesh_base_id, zone_id, RealDouble, &
                        "CoordinateX", xflat, coord_id, ierr &
                    )
                    CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "Tetra CoordinateX "//TRIM(zone_name))

                    CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
                        file_id, mesh_base_id, zone_id, RealDouble, &
                        "CoordinateY", yflat, coord_id, ierr &
                    )
                    CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "Tetra CoordinateY "//TRIM(zone_name))

                    CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
                        file_id, mesh_base_id, zone_id, RealDouble, &
                        "CoordinateZ", zflat, coord_id, ierr &
                    )
                    CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "Tetra CoordinateZ "//TRIM(zone_name))

                    DEALLOCATE(xflat, yflat, zflat)

                    ALLOCATE(conn(4*ntets))
                    tet_id = INT(0, KIND=cgsize_t)

                    DO k = 1, nk-1
                        DO j = 1, nj-1
                            DO i = 1, ni-1

                                n000 = INT((k-1)*ni*nj + (j-1)*ni + i, KIND=cgsize_t)
                                n100 = n000 + INT(1, KIND=cgsize_t)
                                n010 = n000 + INT(ni, KIND=cgsize_t)
                                n110 = n010 + INT(1, KIND=cgsize_t)

                                n001 = n000 + INT(ni*nj, KIND=cgsize_t)
                                n101 = n100 + INT(ni*nj, KIND=cgsize_t)
                                n011 = n010 + INT(ni*nj, KIND=cgsize_t)
                                n111 = n110 + INT(ni*nj, KIND=cgsize_t)

                                ! Six-tetra split of each structured hex.
                                ! The chosen diagonal is n000--n111.
                                CALL add_tet_conn(conn, tet_id, n000, n100, n110, n111)
                                CALL add_tet_conn(conn, tet_id, n000, n110, n010, n111)
                                CALL add_tet_conn(conn, tet_id, n000, n010, n011, n111)
                                CALL add_tet_conn(conn, tet_id, n000, n011, n001, n111)
                                CALL add_tet_conn(conn, tet_id, n000, n001, n101, n111)
                                CALL add_tet_conn(conn, tet_id, n000, n101, n100, n111)

                            END DO
                        END DO
                    END DO

                    element_start = INT(1, KIND=cgsize_t)
                    element_end   = ntets

                    CALL cg_section_write_f( &
                        file_id, mesh_base_id, zone_id, &
                        "TetraElements", TETRA_4, &
                        element_start, element_end, &
                        0, conn, coord_id, ierr &
                    )
                    CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_section_write_f TetraElements "//TRIM(zone_name))

                    DEALLOCATE(conn)

                ELSE

                block_isize_3d = INT(0, KIND=cgsize_t)

                block_isize_3d(1,1) = INT(ni, KIND=cgsize_t)
                block_isize_3d(2,1) = INT(nj, KIND=cgsize_t)
                block_isize_3d(3,1) = INT(nk, KIND=cgsize_t)

                block_isize_3d(1,2) = INT(ni-1, KIND=cgsize_t)
                block_isize_3d(2,2) = INT(nj-1, KIND=cgsize_t)
                block_isize_3d(3,2) = INT(nk-1, KIND=cgsize_t)

                block_isize_3d(1,3) = INT(0, KIND=cgsize_t)
                block_isize_3d(2,3) = INT(0, KIND=cgsize_t)
                block_isize_3d(3,3) = INT(0, KIND=cgsize_t)

                CALL cg_zone_write_f( &
                    file_id, mesh_base_id, TRIM(zone_name), &
                    block_isize_3d, Structured, zone_id, ierr &
                )
                CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_zone_write_f "//TRIM(zone_name))

                ALLOCATE(x3(ni,nj,nk), y3(ni,nj,nk), z3(ni,nj,nk))

                DO k = 1, nk
                    DO j = 1, nj
                        DO i = 1, ni
                            x3(i,j,k) = blk(ib)%x(i,j)
                            y3(i,j,k) = blk(ib)%y(i,j)

                            IF (nk > 1) THEN
                                z3(i,j,k) = extrusion_span*REAL(k-1,8)/REAL(nk-1,8)
                            ELSE
                                z3(i,j,k) = 0.0D0
                            END IF
                        END DO
                    END DO
                END DO

                CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
                    file_id, mesh_base_id, zone_id, RealDouble, &
                    "CoordinateX", x3, coord_id, ierr &
                )
                CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "CoordinateX "//TRIM(zone_name))

                CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
                    file_id, mesh_base_id, zone_id, RealDouble, &
                    "CoordinateY", y3, coord_id, ierr &
                )
                CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "CoordinateY "//TRIM(zone_name))

                CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
                    file_id, mesh_base_id, zone_id, RealDouble, &
                    "CoordinateZ", z3, coord_id, ierr &
                )
                CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "CoordinateZ "//TRIM(zone_name))

                DEALLOCATE(x3, y3, z3)

                END IF

            ELSE

                block_isize_2d = INT(0, KIND=cgsize_t)

                block_isize_2d(1,1) = INT(ni, KIND=cgsize_t)
                block_isize_2d(2,1) = INT(nj, KIND=cgsize_t)

                block_isize_2d(1,2) = INT(ni-1, KIND=cgsize_t)
                block_isize_2d(2,2) = INT(nj-1, KIND=cgsize_t)

                block_isize_2d(1,3) = INT(0, KIND=cgsize_t)
                block_isize_2d(2,3) = INT(0, KIND=cgsize_t)

                CALL cg_zone_write_f( &
                    file_id, mesh_base_id, TRIM(zone_name), &
                    block_isize_2d, Structured, zone_id, ierr &
                )
                CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_zone_write_f "//TRIM(zone_name))

                CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
                    file_id, mesh_base_id, zone_id, RealDouble, &
                    "CoordinateX", blk(ib)%x, coord_id, ierr &
                )
                CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "CoordinateX "//TRIM(zone_name))

                CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
                    file_id, mesh_base_id, zone_id, RealDouble, &
                    "CoordinateY", blk(ib)%y, coord_id, ierr &
                )
                CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "CoordinateY "//TRIM(zone_name))

                ALLOCATE(zblock(ni,nj))
                zblock = 0.0D0

                CALL cg_coord_write_f( &
                    file_id, mesh_base_id, zone_id, RealDouble, &
                    "CoordinateZ", zblock, coord_id, ierr &
                )
                CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "CoordinateZ "//TRIM(zone_name))

                DEALLOCATE(zblock)

            END IF

        END DO

        CALL cg_goto_f(file_id, mesh_base_id, ierr, "end")
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_goto_f")

        CALL cg_descriptor_write_f("Pitch", TRIM(real_to_string(pitch)), ierr)
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_descriptor_write_f Pitch")

        IF (make_3d) THEN
            CALL cg_descriptor_write_f("ExtrusionSpan", TRIM(real_to_string(extrusion_span)), ierr)
            CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_descriptor_write_f ExtrusionSpan")

            IF (write_tetrahedral_grid_enabled) THEN
                CALL cg_descriptor_write_f("CellType", "TETRA_4 unstructured", ierr)
                CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_descriptor_write_f CellType")
            ELSE
                CALL cg_descriptor_write_f("CellType", "Structured hexahedral", ierr)
                CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_descriptor_write_f CellType")
            END IF
        END IF

        CALL cg_close_f(file_id, ierr)
        CALL check_cgns_error(ierr, "cg_close_f")

    END SUBROUTINE write_blade_and_four_blocks_to_cgns

    CHARACTER(LEN=64) FUNCTION real_to_string(value)

        IMPLICIT NONE

        REAL(8), INTENT(IN) :: value

        WRITE(real_to_string, "(ES24.16)") value
        real_to_string = ADJUSTL(real_to_string)

    END FUNCTION real_to_string

    SUBROUTINE check_cgns_error(ierr, location)

        USE cgns
        IMPLICIT NONE

        INTEGER, INTENT(IN) :: ierr
        CHARACTER(LEN=*), INTENT(IN) :: location

        IF (ierr /= 0) THEN
            PRINT *, "CGNS error at ", TRIM(location)
            CALL cg_error_print_f()
            STOP
        END IF

    END SUBROUTINE check_cgns_error

END PROGRAM create_nine_block_blade_topology
